

$(1)      AXR$.   
MARTIME   EQU       0                  .  
. *******************************************************************   
.     
. STAND ALONE SORT
.  1. READS SORT PARAMETER CARDS TO BUILD SORT TABLE. 
.  2. READS RECORDS FROM A LABELED OR NON-LABELED FILE AND  
.      RELEASES THE RECORDS TO THE SORT.  
.  3. CALLS THE SORT PROGRAM. 
.  4. RETRIEVES SORTED RECORDS AND WRITES THEM ON A LABELED 
.     OR NON-LABELED FILE.    
.     
. *******************************************************************   
.     
. STAND ALONE MERGE     
.  1. READS MERGE PARAMETER CARDS TO BUILD MERGE TABLE.     
.  2. READS RECORDS FROM LABELED OR NON-LABELED FILES AND RELEASES
.     THE RECORDS TO THE MERGE ROUTINE    
.  3. RETRIEVES MERGED RECORDS AND WRITES THEM ON A LABELED 
.     OR NON-LABELED FILE.    
.     
. **********************************************************************
.     
. READ AND PROCESS PARAMETER CARDS  
.     
$(1)      LIT     
COMMUN*   PROC      .   
          L         A0,(WFORM 0,2,7,MSG21) .    
          ER        PRINT$            .   
          TNZ       JMPERR            .   
          J         0,X11             .   
          L         A0,(01,0)         .   
          ER        IALL$             .   
          ER        ERR$              .   
          END       .   
START*.     
          LMJ       X11,INITCD        . PICK UP OPTIONS AND PARAMS
.     
. IF SORT AND VOLUME PARAMETER NOT INPUT, USE SMALL VOLUME  
. (RETURN FROM PARAMETER CARD EOF)  
CEOF. 
          L,U       A0,1              . LOAD ASCII OFFSET   
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . NON  ZERO IS ASCII  
          L,U       A0,0              . LOAD CFH OFFSET     
          L         A1,HDSW,A0        . LOAD CFH OR ASCII CK HEADING CODE     
          S         A1,MAINSW         . STORE HEAD CHECK ADDRESS  
          L         A1,UCKSW,A0       . LOAD CK UNLABLED SWITCH   
          S         A1,MAINSW+1       . STORE UNLABED CK ADDRESS  
          L         A1,INSW,A0        . LOAD INPUT ADDRESS  
          S         A1,MAINSW+2       . STORE INPUT CODE ADDRESS  
          L         A1,WHDSW,A0       . LOAD WRITE HEADER ADDRESS 
          S         A1,MAINSW+3       . STORE WRITE HEADER ADDRESS
          L         A1,OUTSW,A0       . LOAD OUTPUT CODE ADDRESS  
          S         A1,MAINSW+4       . STORE OUTPUT CODE ADDRESS 
          L         A1,FINSW,A0       . LOAD NO MORE REC CODE ADDRESS   
          S         A1,MAINSW+5       . STORE FIN ADDRESS   
          S         A1,RETNOW         . STORE IN I BAND FOR NOW   
          S,H2      A1,RETNOM         . STORE IN CKPNT I BANK FOR NOW   
          TNE       A10,(' MERGE')    .   
          J         CEOFA             .   
.     
. HANDLES TAPE POSITIONING OF INPUT TAPE FILE   
          TNZ       MRKCTRVAL         .   
          J         MOVER+4           .   
          L,U       A0,INPKT          .   
          L,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          S         A1,TEMP1          .   
          L,U       A1,052            .   
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          L         A2,MRKCTRVAL      .   
          AN,U      A2,1              .   
          S         A2,R1             .   
MOVER     ER        IOW$              .   
          JGD       R1,MOVER          .   
          L         A1,TEMP1          .   
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          TNZ       FASTFILEIN        . FASTRAND INPUT FILE 
          TNZ       INPOPENSW         . SHOULD INPUT FILE BE MOVED?     
          J         INPOK             . NO
          S         A0,TEMP0          . STORE A0 AND A1     
          S         A1,TEMP1          .   
          L,U       A0,INPKT          . LOAD INPUT PACKET ADDRESS 
          L,U       A1,040            . LOAD REWIND CODE    
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          ER        IOW$              .   
          L,U       A1,020            . RESTORE READ CODE   
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          L         A0,TEMP0          . RESTORE A0 AND A1   
          L         A1,TEMP1          .   
INPOK.
          TNE       A10,(' MERGE')      . TEST IF MERGE     
          J         CEOFA               . YES, BYPASS IAS ASSIGNMENT    
          L,U       A0,VOLSML           . CORE SCRATCH AREA FOR SMALL VOLUME  
          TNZ,H2    PARAM+1           . IS CORE PARAMETER USED?   
          S,H2      A0,PARAM+1        . STORETHIS CORE PARAMETER  
          L         A0,CORESAVE       .   
          TZ        CORESAVE          .   
          S,H2      A0,PARAM+1        .   
          TNZ       YOPTFL            . IS IT A CORE ONLY SRT?(NO XA OR XB    
          J         CEOFA             . YES  DO NOT DYNAMIC ASG XA OR XB
          DL        A1,XA               . IAS SCRATCH FILE  
          L,U       A3,XAASGP.        . LOAD XA POINTER     
          TNZ       XOPTFL             . AN XA SORT ONLY    
          L,U       A3,XAASGB         . LOAD XA BIG POINTER 
          LMJ       X11,FACASIGN       . GO ASSIGN XA UNLESS ASSIGNED EXTERNAL
          TNZ       XOPTFL             . XA SORT ONLY 
          J         CEOFA              . YES , NO XB FILE   
          DL        A1,XB               . 
          L,U       A3,XBASGP         . LOAD XB POINTER     
          LMJ       X11,FACASIGN       . GO ASSIGN XB UNLESS ASSIGNED EXTERNAL
          J         CEOFA               . 
FACASIGN  . ENTRY VIA LMJ X11,FACASIGN    
          DS        A1,FACPKT           . STORE FILE NAME IN PACKET     
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . 
          ER        FACIL$              . SEE IF FILE IS ASSIGNED 
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6            . 
          J         FAC1                . NOT ASSIGNED
          . CLEAR THE INTERNALLY ASSIGN FLAG    
          L,S1      A1,FACPKT+6         . GET EQUIPMENT CODE
          TLE,U     A1,020              . OR NOT DRUM 
          J         FACERR              . IS AN ERROR 
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
FAC1      L         A0,0,A3           . LOAD CSF$ PACKET    
          ER        CSF$                . ASSIGN IAS  
          SZ        1,A3              . SET INTERNAL ASIGNED FLAG 
          JP        A0,0,X11           . GOOD ASSIGN , RETURN     
FACERR    LMJ       X10,OPREQ          . RESULT RETRY OR ERROR    
          J         FACASIGN+1         . RETURN 
.     
. TEST IF REQUIRED PARAMETERS WERE INPUT  
CEOFA     TNZ       REQKEY              . REQUIRED PARAMETERS PRESENT?  
          TZ        REQFIN            .   
          J         REQE              . NO
          L         A0,INPKT            . 
          TNE       A0,('CARDS ')       . CARD INPUT? 
          J         CEOF1               . YES   
          TZ        STDLBL              . TEST IF LABELED TAPE    
          J         CEOF0               . NO    
          L,U       A0,INPKT          . LOAD FOR POINTER    
          L         X9,MAINSW+1       . LOAD UNLABLED FILE CK ADDRESS   
          LMJ        X9,0,X9          . GO CHECK UNLABLED CALCULATIONS  
.     
. IF SORT AND A LABELED FILE, CHECK THE HEADER LABEL  
CEOF0     TNE       A10,(' MERGE')      . IS THIS MERGE REQUEST   
          J         MEXECUTE            . YES   
          TNZ       STDLBL              . LABELED TAPES?    
          J         CEF1                . 
          L         X9,MAINSW         . LOAD HEAD CK CODE   
          L,U       A0,INPKT            . 
          LMJ       X11,0,X9           . GO CHECK THE HEADER
          J         CEF1                . 
REQE      L         A0,REQP             . REQUIRED PARAMETER MISSING    
          J         ERROR1              . 
REQE1     LA        A0,ILLP            . ILLEGAL PARAMETER CASE   
          J         ERROR1             . ERROR  
/.    
. *************************************************************************   
. SORT
.     
. CARD INPUT
.     
CEOF1     TNZ       REQBLK              . TEST IF BLOCK PARAMETER INPUT 
          J         CEOF2               . YES   
          L,U       A0,1                . CONSTANT RCD/BLK COUNT  
          S         A0,BLKSIZ           . 
          SZ        REQBLK              . 
CEOF2     TNZ       REQRSZ              . TEST IF RECORD SIZE INPUT     
          J         $+5                 . YES   
          L,U       A0,14               . RECORD SIZE OF 80 
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . TEST IF ASCII 
          L,U       A0,20             . LOAD FOR ASCII
          S         A0,RECSIZ           . 
          SZ        REQRSZ              . 
          L,U       A0,INPKT          . LOAD INPKT  FOR POINTER   
          L         X9,MAINSW+1       . LOAD UNLAB CHK FOR BLK SIZE     
          LMJ       X9,0,X9           . USE FOR LBLED AND UNLBLED 
.     
. REQUEST CORE FOR SORT AND BUFFER  
CEF1      L,H1      A0,INPKT+4          . CORE SIZE FOR INPUT BUFFER    
          TLE,H1    A0,OUTPKT+4         . CORE SIZE FOR OUTPUT BUFFER   
          L,H1      A0,OUTPKT+4         . USE LAREGEST CORE SIZE  
          A,U       A0,1                . INCREMENT IN CASE OF ODD BLOCK
          TLE,U     A0,29              . SHORT BLOCK  
          LA,U      A0,28              .  
          A,H2      A0,INPKT+4          . ADD START ADDRESS OF BUFFER   
          A,U       A0,30             . COVER ASCII ROUNDING UP   
          S,H2      A0,PARAM+2          . START ADDRESS FOR SORT CORE   
          A,H2      A0,PARAM+1          . ADD LENGTH OF SORT CORE 
          A         A0,SORTLENG         . 
          A,U       A0,4              .   
          ER        MCORE$              . 
          AN        A0,SORTLENG         . 
          S         A0,INPKT+7          . 
          LMJ       X11,CKFRM1        . CHECK FORM01 VALIDITY     
. CLOSE PARAMETER TABLE AND ASSIGN OUTPUT FILE IF NONE SPECIFIED  
. INITIALIZE THE SORT   
          LMJ       X11,CPTAOF         . GO CLOSE PARAM AND ASSIGN FILE 
          L,U       A3,PARAM          . PARAMETER TABLE LOCATION  
          R$NCDS                      . GO INITIALIZE SORT  
/.    
.     SORT HAS BEEN INITIALIZED THIS SECTION WILL     
. READ AND RELEASE RECORDS TO THE SORT    
FPOC. 
          L         A1,INPKT          . LOAD IN FILE NAME   
          TNE       A1,('CARDS ')     . TEST IF CARD INPUT  
          J         FCARD             . CARD INPUT JUMP     
          TNZ       SKIPFLG            . SKIP SOME RECORDS  
          LMJ       X11,SKIPSOME       .  
FPOCA     . 
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          TZ        STDLBL             .  
          J         RECIN              .  
COMOUTT   LA,U      A0,CONSOLIN        .  
          ER        COM$               .  
          LA,S1     A5,INNUM           .  
          ANA,U     A5,060             .  
          TG,U      A5,4               .  
          J         COMOUTT            .  
          SA        A5,ACTNUM          .  
          SZ        A3                 .  
          LA,U      A4,BUFSIZ          .  
          DI        A3,BLKLGN          .  
          LA,U      A3,ENDBUF          .  
          ANA       A3,A4              .  
          SA,H1     A3,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          LA        A0,(0100,READAS)   .  
          ER        FORK$              .  
          TNE,U     A5,1               .  
          J         RECIN              .  
          LA,U      A0,CSFINA          .  
          ER        CSF$               .  
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       .  
          LA        A3,BLKLGN          .  
          SA,H1     A3,INPKT1+4        .  
          LA        A0,(0200,READBS)   .  
          ER        FORK$              .  
          TNE,U     A5,2               .  
          J         RECIN              .  
          SA,H1     A3,INPKT2+4        .  
          LA,U      A0,CSFINB          .  
          ER        CSF$               .  
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       .  
          LA        A0,(0300,READCS)   .  
          ER        FORK$              .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
RECIN     TZ        NUMFLG            . TEST IF NUMREC PARAM ?    
          J         NONUM             . JUMP NO PARAM 
          L         A0,RECSAVE        . LOAD NUM REC TO SORT
          JZ        A0,REWIND         . JUMP THATS ALL
          AN,U      A0,1              . SUBTRACT 1    
          S         A0,RECSAVE        . STORE NEW TOTAL     
NONUM     . 
          L,U       A1,INPKT            . 
          L         X9,MAINSW+2       .  LOAD INPUT ADDRESS 
          LMJ       X8,0,X9           .  GO GET RECORD
          J         REWIND              . END OF FILE RETURN
          TNZ       CONVERTWORD     
          J         TAGDONE   
          SZ          X6
          LX        X5,TGCNT  
          A,U         X5,1    
         SZ        X4   
          LA        A1,STBUF          . LOAD ADDR TO BUILD REC    
          L,U       A2,2              . LOAD TEST OF TAG    
          TE        A2,CONVERTWORD    . TEST IF TAG   
          J         $+4               . JUMP IF SELECT SORT 
          L         A2,ADRSAV         . LOAD ADDRESS SAVE   
          S         A2,0,A1           . STORE ADDR AS 1ST WD OF REC     
          A,U       A1,1              . INCREMENT POINTER IN RECORD     
          L         A2,ORDERCNT2      . LOAD COUNT FOR MAKE REC   
NXFD      LX        X6,NBYTE,A2     . LOAD # CHARACTER THIS FLD   
          L         X4,TGCNT          . LOAD CHAR CNT 5 FD 3 ASCII
          A,U       X4,1              . AD TO TO CHARACTER EITHR 4 OR 6 
          AN        X4,QBYTE,A2       . FIND 1ST CHAR OFFSET
          A         A0,BWORD,A2    . ADD OFFSET BEGIN OF REC
          J         SINDX             .   
LINDX     JGD      X4,SINDX    . ZERO NO MORE CH IN WORD    
          A,U       A0,1     . BUMP RECORD ADDRESS    
          LX        X4,TGCNT    . RESET CHARACTER COUNT     
SINDX     JGD      X5,GOAHED    . ZERO NO MORE CH IN WORD   
          A,U       A1,1     . INCREMENT NEW REC ADDR 
          LX        X5,TGCNT      . RESET CHARACER COUNT    
GOAHED    EX        TAGLD,X4      . LOAD CH FROM INPUT REC  
          EX        TAGST,X5    . STORE IN TAG OR SEL REC   
          JGD      X6,LINDX   
          JGD      A2,NXFD    
          J         TAGREL    
TAGLD     L,Q4      A14,0,A0  
          L,Q3      A14,0,A0  
          L,Q2      A14,0,A0  
          L,Q1      A14,0,A0  
         L,S2       A14,0,A0  
          L,S1      A14,0,A0  
TAGST     S,Q4      A14,0,A1  
          S,Q3      A14,0,A1  
          S,Q2      A14,0,A1  
          S,Q1      A14,0,A1  
          S,S2      A14,0,A1  
          S,S1      A14,0,A1  
TAGREL.     
          L         A0,STBUF  
$(1). 
TAGDONE.    
          TZ,S6     INRCSW            . TEST USER CHECKING IN RECS
          J         $+4               . NO RELEASE TO SORT  
          LMJ       X9,CKUSRR         . LET USER CHECK
          J         RECIN             . DELETE RECORD RETURN
          J         $+1               . USER IS INSERTING RECORDS 
          LMJ       X11,RREL$         . RELEASE REC TO SUBROUTINE 
          TZ        HDINRC            . IS USER INSERTING RECORDS 
          J         $-5               . YES GET NEXT INSERT REC   
          ON        MARTIME   
          J         RECIN+1             . 
          OFF       MARTIME   
          J         FPOCA              .  
REWIND    . 
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          LA,U      A1,077             .  
          SA,S2     A1,BUFFDUB         .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
          TNZ       TAPEDRUM           . FASTRAN FILE 
          J         CKPTCK             . YES    
RETCK     . 
          DL        A1,INPKT           . GET THE INPUT FILENAME   
          DTE       A1,OUTPKT           . 
          J         SORT1              .  
          TZ        INPKT+8             . TEST IF TAPE INPUT
          J         SORT                . NO    
           L,U      A0,OUTPKT 
           ER       TSWAP$    
/.    
.  THIS SECTION NOTIFIES SORT NO MORE INPUT RECORDS AND     
.     
. HANDLES DISPOSITION OF LAST INPUT TAPE FILE   
.     
SORT. 
          TNZ       FREEINF           . IS F OPTION SET?    
          J         CKDISC            . YES - SKIP REWIND CODE(TAPE)    
          TNZ       FASTFILEIN        .   
          TNZ       INPCLOSESW        . SHOULD INPUT FILE BE REWOUND?   
          J         CKDISC            .   
          S         A0,TEMP0          .   
          S         A1,TEMP1          .   
          L         A1,INPCLOSESW     . LOAD CODE     
          TE,U      A1,1              . REWIND? 
          J         UNLD1             . NO
          L,U       A1,040            . LOAD CODE     
          J         $+2               .   
UNLD1     L,U       A1,041            .   
          L,U       A0,INPKT          . LOAD INPUT PACKET ADDRESS 
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          ER        IOW$              .   
          L,U       A1,020            . RESTORE READ CODE   
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          L         A0,TEMP0          .   
          L         A1,TEMP1          .   
CKDISC    TNZ       INPCHANGESW       . CHANGE DISCS? 
          J         RSORT$            . NO
          S         A0,TEMP           .   
          S         A1,TEMP0          .   
          S         A2,TEMP1          .   
          DL        A1,INPKT          . LOAD INPUT FILE NAME
          DS        A1,FREFLN         .   
          L,U       A0,FREEIM         .   
          ER        CSF$              .   
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2      . ERROR   
          L         A0,TEMP           .   
          L         A1,TEMP0          .   
          L         A2,TEMP1          .   
          J         RSORT$            . CALL SORT     
/.    
. * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * *   
.  CONROL IS RETURNED HERE AFTER RSORT$ CALL    
.     
SRETURN.    
          TZ        OUTLENG           .   
          J         $+3               . OUTPUT RECORD LENGTH WAS GIVEN  
          L         A0,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD LENGTH  
          S         A0,OUTLENG        . STORE RECORD LENGTH INTO OUTPUT LENGTH
          L         A14,OUTBLKSIZ     .   
          S         A14,BLKSIZ        .   
          TNZ       CONVERTWORD       .   
          J         $+3               .   
          L         A6,FLDLENG        .   
          J         $+2               .   
          L         A6,OUTLENG        .   
          S         A6,RECSIZ         .   
          L         A14,NEWBLKLN      .   
          S         A14,BLKLGN        .   
          S,H1      A14,OUTPKT+4      .   
          TZ        STDLBL            . TEST IF  STANDARD LABEL   
           A,U       A14,1        .  ADD ONE    
           S         A14,NEWBLKLN  . STORE IN OUT WD SIZ    
          TZ        STDLBL. TEST IF STD LABEL   
           AN,U      A14,1         . RESTORE A14
NORLD.
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          LA,U      A0,BUFFDUB+2       .  
          SA,H2     A0,OUTPKT+4        .  
          LA,H1     A0,OUTPKT+4        .  
          SA,H1     A0,OUTPKT1+4       .  
          SZ,H1     BUFFDUB            .  
          LMJ       X10,HEADWR         .  
          LA        A0,(0401,WRITEAA)  .  
          ER        FORK$              .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
          J         RECOUT              . JUMP TO RETREIVE RECS   
.     
.  CHECK TO SEE IF FREE INPUT FILE  
SORT1     . 
          TZ        FREEINF            . FREE INPUT FILE    
          J         SORT               . NO     
          DL        A1,INPKT           .  
          DS        A1,FREFLN          .  
          LA,U      A0,FREEIM          .  
          ER        CSF$               . FREE THE INPUT FILE
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       . ERROR  
          J         SORT               .  
CKPTCK    . 
          TZ        CKPFLG             . CHECK POINT TRY?   
          J         RETCK              . NO     
          LMJ       X10,CKPNT          .  
CHECKERS  J         RETCK              .  
/.    
. ***********************************************************************     
.     
.   THIS SECTION RETRIEVES RECORDS FROM  THE SORT     
.     
RECOUT .    
          TNZ       POPFLG            . TEST IF PRINT ONLY  
          J         OUTOK+4           . YES JUMP
          LMJ       X11,OUTASGT        . LOOK FOR OUTPUT FILE ASSIGN    
.     
. HANDLES TAPE POSITIONING OF OUTPUT FILE 
.     
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . FASTRAND OR DRUM FILE?    
          TNZ       OUTOPENSW         . POSITION OUTPUT FILE?     
          J         OUTOK             . NO, NO REWIND 
          S         A0,TEMP0          .   
          S         A1,TEMP1          .   
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT         . YES, LOAD OUTPUT PACKET ADDRESS 
          L,U       A1,040            . LOAD FACILITY CODE  
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          ER        IOW$              .   
          L,U       A1,010            . RESTORE WRITE CODE  
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          L         A0,TEMP0          .   
          L         A1,TEMP1          .   
OUTOK.
          TNZ       STDLBL            . NONZERO IS STANDARTD LABEL
          J         $+3               . JUMP IF NON LABLED  
          L         X9,MAINSW+3       . LOAD WRITE HEADER CODE    
          LMJ       X10,0,X9          . IF LABLED GO WRITE HEADER 
          SZ        OHDFLG            . SET FLAG FINISHED WHITH HEADERS 
          DSL       A7,72             . ZERO A7 AND A8
          SZ        A9                  .  ZERO     A9
          SZ        A11               . ZERO A11
          LXM,H2    X3,OUTPKT+4         . BUFFER ADDRESS    
          LXI,U     X3,1                . 
RECOUT1 .   
          TZ        HDOREC            . TEST USER INSERTING RECORDS     
          J         USRR+2            . YES GET NEXT INSERT RECORD
NOEX      LMJ       X11,RRET$         . GET RECORD FROM  SORT     
RETNOW    +         0             . NO MORE REC ADDR STORED HERE  
USRR      TZ,S6     OUTRECSW          . IS USER CHECKING OUTPUT   
          J         $+4               . NO CONTINUE  PROCESSING   
          LMJ       X9,DOUSRR         . LET USER CHECK OUTPUT RECS
          J         NOEX              . DELETE RECORD RETURN
          J         $+1               . USER IS INSERTING RECORDS 
          LMJ       X11,CARDCK         . CHECK FOR CARD OUTPUT    
          TNZ       POZFLG            . TEST ZERO IS L OR P OPTION
          J         PRTCK             . JUMP TO CHECK PRINTING    
ZSKIP1.     
          TNZ       POPFLG            . IS PRINT ONLY SET   
          J         RECOUT1           . YES GO GET NEXT RECORD    
          L         A13,OUTPKT        . LOAD OUTPUT FILE NAME     
          TNE       A13,('CARDS ')    . IS IT CARD OUTPUT?  
          J         RECOUT1           . YES, GET NEXT RECORD
          L         X9,MAINSW+4       . LOAD OUTPUT CODE ADDR     
          LMJ       X7,0,X9           . GO OUTPUT RECORD    
          J         RECOUT1           .   
/.    
. * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * 
. CT IS TRANSFERRED HERE TO FINISHED END OF SORT
. CT COMES FROM ANSFIN LABIN OR CARDS     
PRTOTRCD  L        A2,A11               . TOTAL NR RECOREDS OUTPUT
          LMJ       X9,OTD             . CONVERT TOTAL NR RECORDS TO OCTAL    
          S         A3,ENDMM+6          . STORE TOTAL NR RECORDS OUTPUT 
          S         A10,ENDMM+1         . STORE SORT OR MERGE     
          L         A0,ENDMP            . END OF SORT OR MERGE MESSAGE  
          ER        PRINT$              . 
          TNZ       POPFLG            . TEST IF PRINT ONLY  
          J         GOTOEXIT          . YES JUMP
.     
. HANDLES DISPOSITION OF LAST OUTPUT TAPE FILE  
.     
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . FASTRAND FILE?
          TNZ       OUTCLOSESW        . SHOULD OUTPUT FILE BE REWOUND?  
          J         CHGCK             .   
          L         A1,OUTCLOSESW     .   
          TE,U      A1,1              .   
          J         UNLD2             . REWIND? 
          L,U       A1,040            . LOAD CODE     
          J         $+2               .   
UNLD2     L,U       A1,041            .   
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT         . LOAD ADDRESS OF OUTPKT PACKET   
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          ER        IOW$              .   
          L,U       A1,010            . RESTORE WRITE CODE  
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
CHGCK     TNZ       OUTCHANGESW       .   
          J         GOTOEXIT          .   
          DL        A1,OUTPKT         .   
          DS        A1,FREFLN         .   
          L,U       A0,FREEIM         .   
          ER        CSF$              .   
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2      .   
GOTOEXIT.   
          LA,U      A0,XAFLAG          . SET XA POINTER     
          LA        A1,FREEIM1         .  
          TZ        0,A0               . XA ASSIGNED INTERNALLY   
          J         $+4                . NO     
          SA        A1,0,A0            .  
          ER        CSF$               . FREE XA
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       . ERROR ON ERROR     
          LA,U      A0,XAOFLAG         .  
          TZ        0,A0               . XA ONLY SORT XA ASSIGNED INTERNALLY  
          J         $+4                . NO     
          SA        A1,0,A0            .  
          ER        CSF$               . FREE XA
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       . ERROR ON OERROR    
          LA,U      A0,XBFLAG          . SET XB POINTER     
          TZ        0,A0               . XB ASSIGNED INTERNALLY   
          J         $+4                . NO     
          SA        A1,0,A0            .  
          ER        CSF$               . FREE XB
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       . ERROR ON ERROR     
          TNZ       SCRTPC            . ARE WORKING TAPES USED ?  
          ER        EXIT$               . 
          L,H2      A4,SCRTPC         . YES GET # SCR TAPES ASSIGNED    
          LX        X2,(2,TAPTAB)     . SET UP FILE NAMES XC - XZ 
FSCRTP    L,U       A2,'$'            . REPLACE 'X' WITH '$' IN   
          S,S1      A2,0,X2           .    FILENAMES OF SCRATCH TAPES   
          DL        A0,0,X2           . LOAD FILENAME 
          DS        A0,FACPKT         . HAS IT BEEN ASSIGNED
          ER        FACIL$            . REQUEST STATUS
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          . TEST EQUIPMENT TYPE NE 0  
          ER        EXIT$             . NOT ASSIGNED   FINISHED END SORT
          DL        A1,0,*X2          . LOADFILE NAME AND BUMP INDEX    
          DS        A1,FREFLN         . STORE IN FREE PACKET
          LA,U      A0,FREEIM         . LOAD ADDR OF FREE  PACKET 
          ER        CSF$              . FREE    $(SCRATCH TAPE USE NAME)
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2      . ERROR IF BAD CSF$   
          JGD       A4,FSCRTP         . CONTINUE UNTIL ALL FREED  
          ER        EXIT$             . END SORT THIS EXIT  
/.    
CKPNT     . ENTRY VIA LMJ X10,CKPNT TO TRY CKPOINT    
          . 
          DS        A0,A0SAVE          . SAVE A0 AND A1     
          DS        A2,A2SAVE          . SAVE A2 AN A3
          SX        X8,X8SAVE          .  
          SX        X10,X10SAVE        .  
          LA        A3,NUMCKS          . GET THE NUMBER OF CHECKS POSSIBLE    
          JZ        A3,0,X10           . NO MORE RETURN     
          ANA,U     A3,1               .  
          SA        A3,NUMCKS          . RESTORE COUNT
          LA        A3,DEVTYPE         .  
          TE,U      A3,'T'             . TAPES  
          J         OTHERCK            .  
          LA        A3,X10             .  
ADDRCK1   TE,U      A3,CHECKERS       .   
          J         OTHERCK            . YES    
          LA,U      A0,INPKT           .  
          ER        TSWAP$             .  
          TNZ       STDLBL             . LABELLED TAPE
          J         OTHERCK            . NO     
          LA,U      A3,050             . A MOVE FUNCTION    
          SA,S2     A3,INPKT+3         .  
          LA,U      A0,INPKT           .  
          ER        IOW$               . MOVE PAST THE LABEL
          ER        IOW$               . MOVE PAST THE LABEL
          LA,U      A0,020             .  
          SA,S2     A3,INPKT+3         .  
OTHERCK   . 
          LA,U      A0,CONSOL1         .  
          ER        COM$               . CKPT OR NOT  
          LA,S1     A2,INCOME          .  
          TNE,U     A2,'Y'             .  
          J         CKPTRUN            .  
          TE,U      A2,'N'             .  
          J         OTHERCK            .  
PUTCON    LA,U      A0,CONSOL2         .  
          ER        COM$               .  
          LA,S1     A2,INCOME2         .  
          TNE,U     A2,'Y'             .  
          J         RETADDR            .  
          TE,U      A2,'N'             .  
          J         PUTCON             .  
ADDRCK3.    
          LA        A3,X10             .  
          LA        A0,A0SAVE          . RESTORE REGISTERS  
          LA        A1,A1SAVE          .  
          LA        A2,A2SAVE          .  
ADDRCK2.    
          J         0,X10              .  
CKPTRUN   . 
          TE,U      A3,CHECKERS        .  
          J         NOFOF              .  
          LA        A1,DEVTYPE        . LOAD DEVICE TYPE    
          TNE,U     A1,'T'            . TAPE?   
          J         NOFOF             . YES     
          DL        A1,INPKT           .  
          DS        A1,FREFL           .  
          LA,U      A0,FREEIM1         . GET THE @FREE IMAGE
          ER        CSF$               . FREE THIS PART OF INPUT  
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       . ERR$   
NOFOF     . 
          TNZ       CKPASG             .  
          J         CKPTASG            .  
          DL        A0,CKFLNM          .  
          DS        A0,FNASG           .  
          LA,U      A0,'A'             .  
          SA,S6     A0,ASGIM  
          LA        A0,(4,ASGIM)       .  
          ER        CSF$               .  
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       .  
          SZ        CKPASG             .  
CKPTASG   . 
          LA,U      A0,CKPTIM          .  
          ER        CSF$               . CKPT   
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       . ERROR  
RECOM3    . 
          LA,U      A0,CONSOL3         .  
          ER        COM$               . ASK IF EXIT OR CONTINUE  
          LA,S1     A2,INCOME3         . GET THE RESPONSE   
          TNE,U     A2,'E'             . EXIT   
          ER        EXIT$              . YES    
          TE,U      A2,'C'             .  
          J         RECOM3             .  
RETADDR   . 
          LA        A0,(0177701,CONTADD) .
          ER        IALL$              .  
          LA        A4,DEVTYPE         . DEVICE TYPE  
          LA,H2     A3,X10SAVE         .  
          TE,U      A3,CHECKERS        . TAPE EOR     
          J         ADDRCK3            . YES    
          DL        A1,INPKT           .  
          AA,U      A1,1               .  
          DS        A1,INPKT           .  
          LA,U      A0,2               .  
          SA        A0,INPKT+5         .  
          LA,H1     A0,INPKT+4         .  
          AA,H2     A0,PARAM+1         .  
          SA        A0,INPKT+7         .  
          SZ        INPKT+9            .  
          DS        A1,FACPKT          .  
          DS        A1,FNASG           .  
          LA,U      A0,FACPKT          .  
          ER        FACIL$             .  
          TZ,S1     FACPKT+6           .  
          J         ADDRCK3A           .  
          LA,U      A3,'T'             .  
          TE,U      A4,'T'             .  
          LA,U      A3,'A'             .  
          SA,S6     A3,ASGIM           .  
RASGA     LA        A0,(4,ASGIM)       .  
          ER        CSF$               . ASSIGN NEXT PART   
          LA,U      A3,ASGIM           .  
          JP        A0,$+3             .  
          LMJ       X10,OPREQ          .  
          J         RASGA              .  
ADDRCK3A .  
          DL        A0,A0SAVE          . RESTORE A0 A1
          LA        A2,A2SAVE         . RESTORE A2    
          J         FPOCA              .  
/.    
.  CHECK FOR PRINTING OPTIONS 
.     
PRTCK .    CHECK PRINTING OPTIONS   
          TZ        ZOP                 . Z OPTION?   
          J         $+3               . L OPTION TO PRINT IS SET  
          TZ        POPFLG            . IS PRINT ONLY FLAG SET    
          J         ZSKIP1              . NO, SKIP    
          S         A0,TEMP0          . SAVE A0 
          DS        A1,RSAVE          . SAVE A1 A2    
          DS        A3,RSAVE+2        . SAVE A3 A4    
          L         A1,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD  SIZE   
          TNZ       VARFLG            . TEST IF VARIABLE    
          L,H1      A1,TEMP0          . LOAD VARIABLE RECORD SIZE 
          L,U       A2,2              . LOAD SKIP ONE LINE  
          S,S2      A2,ABC            . STORE LINE CONTROL  
          L,H2      A4,TEMP0          . LOAD BUFFER ADDRESS 
          S,H2      A4,ABC            . STORE BEGIN REC ADDRESS   
          L,U       A3,22             . LOAD MAX FLD DATA PRINT LINE    
          TNZ       MIXFLG            . TEST IF MIX MODE    
          J         $+3               . YS USE FLD DATA LIMIT     
          TZ        ANSFLG            . TEST IF ASCII 
          L,U       A3,33             . LOAD MAX ASCII PRINT LINE 
          L         A2,A3             . SAVE MAX FOR DONE CK
PLOOPC    L         A0,A1             . SAVE A1 
          AN        A1,A3             . SUBTRACT MAX PRT LINE     
          TP        A1                . IF POSITIVE OR GT MAX     
          S         A0,A3             . IF NEG STR A0 IN A3 
          S,S3      A3,ABC            . STORE NUM WORDS     
          L         A0,ABC            . LOAD PRINT CT WORD  
          TNZ       MIXFLG            . TEST IF MIXED MODE  
          J         $+2               . YES USE PRINT$
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . ASCII FILES?  
          J         $+3               .   
          ER        APRINT$           .   
          J         $+2               .   
          ER        PRINT$              . 
          TNZ       A1                . TEST IF REC WAS MAX WDS   
          J         PTALL             . JUMP HAVE PRINTED RECORD  
          TE        A2,A3             . TEST IF STILL IN RECORDS  
          J         PTALL             . JUMP HAVE PRINTED RECORD  
          L,U       A0,1              . LOAD SINGLE SPACE PRINT   
          S,S2      A0,ABC            . SET TO SINGLE SPACE 
          A,S3      A4,ABC            . ADD WORDS PRINTED   
          S,H2      A4,ABC            . STORE NEXT ADDR     
          J         PLOOPC            . CONTINUE PRINTING   
PTALL     L         A0,TEMP0            . RESET REGISTERS   
          TNZ       POPFLG            . TEST IF PRINT ONLY  
          A,U       A11,1             . YESS ADD 1 TO COUNT 
          DL        A1,RSAVE            . RESTORE A1 A2     
          DL        A3,RSAVE+2          . RESTORE A3 A4     
          TNE       A10,(' MERGE')    . TEST IF MERGE 
          J         0,X6              . JUMP IF MERGE 
          J         ZSKIP1            . JUMP TO CONTINUE    
/.    
. READ AND RELEASE CARD RECORDS TO THE SORT     
FCARD.
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . TEST IF ASCII 
          J         $+6               . NOT ASCII JUMP
          TNZ       MIXFLG            . TEST IF MIX MODE    
          J         $+4               . YES JUMP
          L         A0,ASPACE         . LOAD ASCII SPACES   
          L,U       A1,33             . LOAD MAX ASCII WORD 
          J         $+3               . JUMP OVER LOAD F D LOAD CODE    
          L         A0,BLANKS 
          L,U       A1,20     
          S         A0,CRDBUF,A1      . BLANK CARD INPUT AREA     
          JGD       A1,$-1    
          L         A0,(SORT,CRDBUF)
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . ASCII DATA FILES?   
          J         $+3               . NO
          ER        AREAD$            . YES, DO AREAD 
          J         $+2               .   
          ER        READ$             . READ INPUT CARD FOR SORT  
          TNZ       SKIPFLG           . SKIP CARDS ?  
          LMJ       X11,SKIPCARD      . YES     
          TZ        NUMFLG            . NUM CARDS TO SORT   
          J         $+5               . NO NUM  
          LA        A0,RECSAVE        . YES NUM TO SORT     
          JZ        A0,SORT           . NO  MORE
          ANA,U     A0,1              . SUBTRACT ONE  
          SA        A0,RECSAVE        . STORE NUM LEFT
          L,U       A0,CRDBUF         . ADDRESS OF CARD IMAGE     
          LMJ       X11,RREL$         . RELEASE RECORD TO SORT    
          J         FCARD             .   
SKIPCARD.   
          L         A0,SKIPSAVE. LOAD NUM TO SKIP     
          AN,U      A0,1              . SUBTRACT ONE  
          S         A0,SKIPSAVE       . STORE NUM TO SKP    
          TN        A0                . NUM SKIPPED SPECIFIED YET 
          J         FCARD             . NO NOT YET    
          L,U       A0,1              . SET FLAG TO STOP SKIP     
          S         A0,SKIPFLG        . SET FLAG
          J         0,X11             . RET TO CK NUM CARDS IN SORT     
.   SORTED CARDS ARE RELEASED TO PUNCH$ FILE    
CARDCK    . 
          TNZ       POPFLG            . IS K PRINT ONLY SET??     
          J         PRTCK             . YES     
          LA        A13,OUTPKT         .  
          TNE       A13,BLANKS        . IS FILE TO BE PUNCHED?    
          J         $+3               . YES     
          TE        A13,('CARDS ')     . OUTPKT EQUAL CARDS 
          J         0,X11              . NO     
          SA,H2     A0,PUNCHPKT        . STORE THE RECORD ADDRESS IN PUNCH PKT
          LA        A0,RECSIZ          . GET TEH RECORD SIZE
          TZ        VARFLG             . VARIABLE LENGTH RECORDS  
          J         STOREP             . NO     
          LDSL      A0,18              .  
          LXI,U     A1,0               .  
          SZ        A0                 .  
          DI        A0,TYPECONST1     .DIVIDE BY CHAR/WORD  
          TZ        A1                 .  
          AA,U      A0,1               . CALCULATE VARIBLE RECORD SIZE  
STOREP    SA,H1     A0,PUNCHPKT        . STORE THE RECORD WORD LENGTH   
          LA        A0,PUNCHPKT        .  
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . ASCII DATA FILES?   
          J         $+3               . NO
          ER        APUNCH$           . YES, DO AN APUNCH$  
          J         $+2               .   
          ER        PUNCH$             . PUNCH THE RECORD   
          AA,U      A11,1              .  
          J         0,X11              .  
/.    
. *************************************************************************   
. MERGE     
.     
. DETERMINE INPUT BUFFER ADDRESSES  
MEXECUTE.   
          L         A9,TCORE$           . ADDRESS OF OUTPUT BUFFER
          L,U       A0,MERGEPKT         . ADDRESS OF 1ST INPUT PKT
MEXEC0    TNZ       0,A0                . TEST IF LAST PACKET     
          J         MEXEC1              . LAST  
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD HEADING INDICATOR    
          S         A2,FILFLG. SET FILE NOT CHECKED   
          S         A2,HDGFLG         . STORE HEADING INDICATOR   
          L         A1,BLKLGN           . SIZE OF DATA BLOCK IF   
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . DO NOT STORE FOR ASCII    
          S,H1      A1,4,A0             . NONLABELED TAPE   
          L         X9,MAINSW         . LOAD HEAD CK ADDRESS
          TZ        STDLBL              . TEST IF LABELED TAPE    
          LMJ       X11,0,X9           . YES , CHECK HEADER 
          TNE       A9,TCORE$           . TEST IF FIRST FILE
          A         A9,NEWBLKLN       . ADD OUTBUF SIZE     
          S,H2      A9,4,A0             . STORE BUFFER ADDRESS IN INPUT PACKET
          A,H1      A9,4,A0             . INCREMENT TO NEXT BUFFER
          A,U       A9,1                . INCREMENT IN CASE OF ODD BLOCK
          S         A9,7,A0             . FORCE 1ST READ IN GETREC
          A,U       A0,PACKSIZE         . INCREMENT TO NEXT INPUT PACKET
          J         MEXEC0              . 
MEXEC1    L         A0,A9               . 
          ER        MCORE$              . REQUEST CORE
.     
. CLOSE PARAMETER TABLE AND ASSIGN OUTPUT FILE IF NONE SPECIFIED  
. INITIALIZE MERGE
MEXEC2    LMJ       X11,CPTAOF         . CLOSE PARAM , ASSIGN OUTPUT FILE     
          LMJ       X11,OUTASGT       . CK FOR OUT ASSIGN   
          L,U       A0,PARAM            . PARAMETER TABLE ADDRESS 
          SLJ       RMGOPN              . INITIALIZE MERGE  
          L,U       A1,MERGEPKT         . ADDRESS OF FIRST INPUT PACKET 
.     
. READ FIRST RECORD OF EACH INPUT FILE AND RELEASE TO THE MERGE   
MEXEC3    TNZ       0,A1                . TEST IF LAST PACKET     
          J         MEXEC4              . LAST  
          L         X9,MAINSW+2       . LOAD INPUT ADDRESS  
          LMJ       X8,0,X9           . GO GET RECORD 
          J         $+1               . NOP FOR MERGE 
          TZ,S6     INRCSW            . IS USER CHECKING IN RECS  
          J         $+4               . NO CONTINUE PROCESSING    
          LMJ       X9,CKUSRR         . GO TO USER    
          J         $-6               . DELETE RET GET NXT REC    
          J         $+1               . INSERT RETURN 
          SLJ       RMGREL              . RELEASE FIRST RECORD OF FILE TO MERGE     
          A,U       A1,PACKSIZE         . INCREMENT BY PACKET SIZE
          J         MEXEC3              . 
MEXEC4    J         MNLABEL           . ONLY ONE BRANCH NOW 
MEXER     DL        A2,0,A1             . 
          DS        A2,NDROTM+3         . 
          L         A0,NDROTP           . NO DATA RECORD ON TAPE  
          J         ERROR1              . 
/.    
. ***********************************************************************     
.     
.     
.  WRITE  MERGED RECORDS
.     
MNLABEL.    
          LMJ       X11,OUTASGT       .   
          L         A2,NEWBLKLN       . LOAD OUT BLK SIZE   
          S         A2,BLKLGN         . SET BLOCK FOR HEADER
         TNZ        STDLBL            . IS IS STANDARD LABEL
          J         $+3               . NO JUMP OVER HEAD WRITE   
          L         X9,MAINSW+3       . LOAD HEADWRITE ADDRESS    
          LMJ       X10,0,X9          .  GO DO HEADWRITE    
          DSL       A7,72             . ZERO A7 A8    
          SZ        A9                . ZERO A9 
          SZ        A11               . ZERO A11
          LXM,H2    X3,OUTPKT+4         . BUFFER ADDRESS    
          LXI,U     X3,1                . SET POINTER TO START OF OUTPUT BUFFER     
          L         A2,NEWBLKLN       . LOAD MAX OUTBUF SIZE
          A,U       A2,1              . ADD 1   
          S         A2,NEWBLKLN       . SET LIMIT AT +1     
MNLABEL1.   
          TZ        HDOREC            . IS USER INSERTING RECORDS 
          J         MUSROR            . YES GET NEXT REC    
NOEX2     SLJ       RMGREQ            .   
RETNOM    JN        A0,MAINSW+5       . FIN CODE STORE HERE 
          L         R4,A1             . SAVE FILE POINTER   
          TZ,S6     OUTRECSW          . IS USER CHECKING OUT RECS 
          J         $+4               . NO CONTINUE   
MUSROR    LMJ       X9,DOUSRR         . YES GO TO USER CK OUT     
          J         NOEX2             . DELETE RECORD RETURN
          J         $+1               . INSERT REC RETURN   
          TNZ       POZFLG            . IS ONE OF PRINT OPTIONS SET     
          LMJ       X6,PRTCK          . YES GO PRINT  
          TNZ       POPFLG            . WAS IT PRINT ONLY   
          J         $+3               . YES DONT OUTPUT     
          L         X9,MAINSW+4       . LOAD OUTPUT ADDRESS 
          LMJ       X7,0,X9           . GO WRITE RECORD     
          TZ        HDINRC            . IS USER INSERTING RECORDS 
          J         MUSRA             . YES GO GET USER REC 
          L         A1,R4             . RESTORE  FILE POINTER     
          L          X9,MAINSW+2      . LOAD INPUT ADDRESS  
          LMJ       X8,0,X9           . YES, GET NEXT RECORD
          L         A0,( -0 )           . END OF FILE RETURN
          TZ,S6     INRCSW            . IS USER CHECKING IN RECS  
          J         $+4               . NO CONTINUE PROCESSING    
MUSRA     LMJ       X9,CKUSRR         . GO CHECK USER RECS  
          J         $-7               . DELETE RECORD RETURN
          J         $+1               . INSERT RECORD RETURN
          J         MNLABEL1            . 
/.    
.     
.  THIS SECTION HANDLES INPUT AND OUTPUT OF CFH DATA  
. ***********************************************************************     
.     
. END OF SORTED OR MERGED RECORDS                 CFH  DATA 
.     
NLABFIN1 LMJ        X11,WRTEOF        .  GOMAKE  EOF  
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF         . WRITE EOF    
          TNZ       A11               . HAS OUTPUT CNT BEEN INCREMENTED??     
          A         A11,A9            . NO  INCREMENT IT    
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       .   
          J         $+3                . NO     
          LA,U      A2,'EOF'           .  
          LMJ       X10,TRLWRT        . WRITE EOF TRAILER   
          J         PRTOTRCD            . GO PRINT TOTAL RECORDS OUTPUT 
.     
.     
.     
. END OF SORTED OR MERGED RECORDS   
.     
LABFIN.     
          TZ        STDLBL            . TEST IF STANDARD LABEL    
          J         $+4               . NOT FORM01    
          S         A7,SAVEIN7
          TE        A7,BLKSIZ         . IS THE BLOCK ALREADY FULL 
          LMJ       X7,GET9           .  NOT FULL GET PADDING 9'S 
          TNZ       POPFLG            . IS PRINT ONLY  SET  
          J         PRTOTRCD          . YES     
          LA        A0,OUTPKT          .  
          TNE       A0,('CARDS ')      .  
          J         PRTOTRCD           .  
          LXI,U     X3,0                . 
          AN,H2     X3,OUTPKT+4         . FIND NUMBER WORDS IN BLOCK    
          TZ        STDLBL            .   
          AN,U      X3,1                . 
          TNZ       X3                  . NO RECORDS IN BLOCK?    
          J         LABFIN1             . NO RECORDS  
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT 
          TZ        STDLBL            .   
          A,U       X3,1                . 
          S,H1      X3,4,A0             . NUM WORDS TO ACCESS WORD
          L,H2      A1,OUTPKT+4         . NUM WORDS TO ACCESS WORD
          TNZ       STDLBL            . CHECK IF STANDARD LABEL   
          J         $+3               . JUMP FORM01   
          S,H2      X3,0,A1             .  AND BLOCK CONTROL WORD 
          S,H1      A7,0,A1             . = NUM REC IN BLOCK
          A,U       A8,1              . UPDATE BLOCK COUNT  
          TNZ       STDLBL            . TESTS NOT STANDARD LABEL  
          L         A7,SAVEIN7        .   
          A         A9,A7               .  AND REC COUNT    
          LMJ       X11,WRITE          . WRITE BLOCK  
          TNZ       A1                . GOOD    
          J         LABFIN1             . YES   
          TE,U      A1,2              . END OF TAPE   
          LMJ       X9,IOERR            . NO, ERROR   
LABFIN1   L,U       A2,1              . SET HDG FLG   
          S         A2,OHDFLG         . SET HEADING FLAG    
          TNZ       STDLBL            .   TEST IF STAND LABEL     
          J         NLABFIN1          . NO JUMP TO WRITE EOFS     
          L,U       A2,'EOF'          . LOAD EOF INDICATAOR 
          LMJ       X10,TRLWRT         . WRITE 'EOF' ON FILE LABEL
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       .   
          J         PRTOTRCD            . NO    
          L,U       A2,1                . 
          S         A2,OUTPKT+5         . SET ADDRESS TO 1ST EOF BLOCK  
          L,U       A2,'EOF'            . 
          LMJ       X8,HTCOMMON        . REWRITE EOF WITH RECORD ON BLOCK COUNT     
          J         PRTOTRCD          . JUMP TO END SORT CODE     
GET9      L         A6,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD SIZE    
          AN,U      A6,1              . SUBTRACT 1    
          L         R1,A6             . MOVE TO R1    
          L         A6,NINES          . LOAD FORM01 FILL CHAR     
          S         A6,0,*X3          . STORE IN OUT BUFFER 
          JGD       R1,$-1            . FILL BUFFER   
          A,U       A7,1              . BUMP A7 
          TNE       A7,BLKSIZ         . TEST IF FULL  
          J         0,X7              . RETURN IF FULL
          J         GET9              . LOOP UNTIL BLOCK IS FULL  
. PRINT TOTAL RECORDS OUTPUT  
. ***********************************************************************     
.     
. LABELED FILE WRITE    
. X3 - POINTER TO NEXT RECORD IN OUTPUT BUFFER  
. A6 - RECORD SIZE FOR RECORD CONTROL WORD
. A7 - RECORDS PER BLOCK COUNT
. A8 - BLOCKS PER REEL COUNT  
. A9 - RECORDS PER REEL COUNT 
. A10 - SORT OR MERGE   
. A11 - TOTAL RECORDS OUTPUT  
. * * *  ** *** *** **** **** *** *** *** 
.  THIS SECTION NOW HANDLES OUTPUT OF ALL CFH RECORDS 
.     
LTWRITE.    
          . CALL VIA LMJ X7,LTWRITE 
. TRANSFER RECORD (ADDRESS IN A0) TO OUTPUT BUFFER    
          TNZ       FSTOUT            . IS IT FIRST REC     
          J         $+4               . RESET JUMP    
          SZ        FSTOUT            . SET TO NOT FIRST REC
          TZ        STDLBL            .   
          A,U       X3,1              . ADD 1 FOR CT WORD   
          TNZ       VARFLG             . VARIABLE LENGTH RECORDS  
          J         LTVRWRT            . YES    
          TNZ       STDLBL            .   
          J         $+4               .   
          TNZ       QLPFLG             . AQLP SORT    
          LA        A6,QLPREC          . YES GET SORT CONTROL WORD
          S         A6,0,*X3            . RECORD CONTROL WORD     
          LXI,U     A0,1                . 
          L         R1,RECSIZ           . NUMBER WORDS IN RECORD  
          BT        X3,0,*A0            . MOVE RECORD TO OUTPUT BUFFER  
          A,U       A7,1                . INCREMENT RECORDS PER BLOCK CT
          TE        A7,BLKSIZ           . TEST FOR FULL BLOCK     
          J         0,X7               . NOT FULL , RETURN  
. WRITE THE BLOCK ON LABELED FILE   
          A,U       A8,1              . COUNT NUM BLOCKS    
          A         A9,A7               . COUNT TOTAL NUM RECS    
          SSL       A7,36               . CLEAR RECORDS PER BLOCK CT    
          LXM,H2    X3,OUTPKT+4         . START OF OUTPUT BUFFER  
          TZ        STDLBL            .   
          A,U       X3,1                . PLUS 1 FOR BLOCK CONTROL WORD 
          LXI,U     X3,1                . 
          LMJ       X11,WRITE          . WRITE BLOCK  
          TNZ       A1                . GOOD STATUS   
          J         0,X7               . YES , RETURN 
. IF END OF REEL IS DETECTED: WRITE TRAILER, SWAP TAPE AND WRITE HEADER 
          TE,U      A1,2              . END OF TAPE   
          LMJ       X9,IOERR            . NO, ERROR   
          L,U       A2,'EOR'          .   
          LMJ       X10,TRLWRT         . WRITE END OF REEL LABEL  
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT           . 
          ER        TSWAP$            . SWAP OUTPUT TAPES   
          LMJ       X10,HEADWR         . WRITE HEADER LABEL 
          J         0,X7               . RETURN 
.     
LTVRWRT   . 
          SX        X2,CFSERM1A+2      .  
          LX,H2     X2,OUTPKT+4        . GET THE OUTPUT BUFFER ADDRESS  
          TNZ       FIRSTVR            . IS THIS THE FIRST RECORD OF THE BLK  
          J         NOTFIRST           . NO     
          SZ        FIRSTVR            . CLEAR THE FIRST ONE FLAG 
          LA        A1,(0,1)           .  
          SA        A1,0,X2            . RESET THE BLOCK CONTROL WORD   
NOTFIRST   .
          SZ        A1                 .  
          LDSC      A0,18              .  
          SSL       A0,18              . RECORD ADDRESS IN A0     
          SA        A1,A5              . STORE THE RECORD LENGTH  
          LXI       A1,A1              . SET UP RECORD CONTROL WORD     
          LXI,U     A0,1               . SET INDEX    
STARTOVR  AA,H2     A5,0,X2            . GET BLOCK SIZE TO DATE   
          AA,U      A5,1               . ADD ONE FOR RECORD CONTROL WORD
          TNZ       BLOFLG            . NONZERO IS REC MODE 
          J         $+4               . JUMP TO CHARAC MODE CK    
          TG        A7,OUTBLKSIZ      . IS NUMBER REACHED MAX ?   
          J         WRTOUTVR          . YES GO WRITE BLOCK  
          J         $+3               . JUMP OVER REC MODE CODE   
          TG        A5,BLKLGN          . WILL THIS RECORD FIT     
          J         WRTOUTVR           . NO     
          SA,H2     A5,0,X2            . UPDATE THE BLOCK CONTROL WORD  
          SA        A1,0,*X3           . SET RECORD CONTROL WORD  
          LXI,U     A1,0               .  
          SA        A1,R1              . SET UP TRANSFER COUNT    
          LA,H1     A1,0,X2            . UPDATE THE BLOCK RECORD COUNT  
          AA,U      A1,1               .  
          SA,H1     A1,0,X2            . UPDATE THE BLOCK CONTROL WORD  
          BT        X3,0,*A0           . BLOCK TRANSFER RECORD TO THE BUFFER  
          AA,U      A7,1               . INCREMENT THE RECORD COUNT     
          LX        X2,CFSERM1A+2      .  
          J         0,X7               . RETURN 
WRTOUTVR  . 
          AA,U      A8,1               . INCREMENT THE BLOCK COUNT
          AA        A9,A7              . INCREMENT THE TOTAL NUMBER OF RECORDS
          SZ        A7                 . CLEAR COUNT  
          LX,H2     X3,OUTPKT+4        . RESET X3     
          LXI,U     X3,1               . SET INCREMENT
          AX,U      X3,1               . POINT PAST THE BLOCK CONTROL WORD    
          DS        A0,DSAVE           . SAVE A0 AND A1     
          SX        X2,CFSERM1A        . SAVE X2
          ANA,H2    A5,0,X2            . RESET A5 TO RECORD LENGTH
          SA        A5,CFSERM1A+1      . SAVE A5
          LMJ       X11,WRITE          . WRITE BLOCK  
          TNZ       A1                 . GOOD I/O STATUS    
          J         GOODVRIO           . YES    
          TE,U      A1,2               . EOR STATUS   
          LMJ       X9,IOERR           . NO , I/O ERROR     
          LA,U      A2,'EOR'           .  
          LMJ       X10,TRLWRT         . WRITE THE EOR TRAILER    
          LA,U      A0,OUTPKT          .  
          ER        TSWAP$             . SWAP TO A NEW TAPE 
          LMJ       X10,HEADWR         . WRITE THE HEADER LABEL   
GOODVRIO  . 
          LX        X2,CFSERM1A        . SAVE X2
          LA        A1,(0,1)           .  
          SA        A1,0,X2            . RESET THE BLOCK CONTROL WORD   
          DL        A0,DSAVE           . SAVE A0 AND A1     
          LA        A5,CFSERM1A+1      . SAVE A5
          ANA,U     A5,1               .  GET RID OF EXTRA WORD   
          J         STARTOVR           . STORE THIS RECORD  
/.    
.     
.     
. ***********************************************************************     
.     
. WRITE HEADER BLOCK    
.     
HEADWR.     
          . CALL VIA  LMJ X10,HEADWR
            TZ       ANSFLG      .  NON ZERO IS FLD DATA    
         J          0,X10     
          DSL       A8,72             . ZERO BLOCK AND RECORD COUNT     
          LA        A1,OUTPKT          .  
          TNE       A1,('CARDS ')      . A CARD OUTPUT
          J         0,X10              . YES    
          L         A1,RLOUT          . GET NUM OF REELS THIS FILE
          A,U       A1,1              .  AND UPDATE   
          S         A1,RLOUT          . SAVE FOR NEXT REEL  
          S,H2      A1,LABSTD+5       . AND FOR HEADER
          LXI       A2,BLKLGN           . NR WORDS/BLK
          LXM       A2,RECSIZ           . NR WORDS/RCD
          S         A2,LABSTD+1        . STORE IN HEADER    
          TZ        TPMKSW            .   
          SZ        NOEOFF            .   
          L,U       A2,'O'            . LOAD OMITTED FLAG   
          L         A1,STDLBL         . LOAD TYPE OF LABEL  
          TE,U      A1,1              . TEST IF OMITTED ?   
          L,U       A2,'S'            . LOAD STANDARD INDICATOR   
          S,S4      A2,LABSTD         . STORE STD LABEL INDICATOR 
          L,U       A2,'HDR'          .   
          LMJ       X8,HTCOMMON        . WRITE 'HDR' LABEL  
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          J         TESTTD             .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
          LXM,H2    X3,OUTPKT+4         . START OF OUTPUT BUFFER  
          LXI,U     X3,1                . 
          SSL       A7,36               . CLEAR RECORD PER BLOCK COUNT  
          LX        A0,RECSIZ           . 
          LXI       A0,RECSIZ           . 
          L         A6,A0               . RECORD CONTROL WORD     
          LXI       A0,BLKSIZ           . NR RCDS PER BLOCK 
          LXM       A0,BLKLGN           . LENGTH OF BLOCK   
          S         A0,0,*X3            . STORE BLOCK CONTROL WORD IN BUFFER  
TESTTD    . 
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       .   
          J         0,X10              . NO , RETURN  
          L,U       A2,'EOF'            . YES, WRITE EOF MARK     
          LMJ       X8,HTCOMMON        . WRITE EOF LABEL    
          J         0,X10              . RETURN 
.     
. WRITE TRAILER BLOCK   
.      (A2 = TYPE OF LABEL)   
.     
TRLWRT.     
          . CALL VIA LMJ X10,TRLWRT 
          L         A3,OUTPKT+5         . GET EOF FASTRAND ADDRESS
          S         A3,LABSTD+6         . STORE FASTRAND ADDRESS IN EOF 
          L         A12,A2              . 
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF         . WRITE EOF    
          L         A0,INPKT          . LOAD INPUT NAME     
NCDIN          L          A2,A12 .        CORRECTED FROM CORFILE  
          L         A12,LABSTD          . 
          SZ,H2     LABSTD    
          LMJ       X8,HTCOMMON        . WRITE EOF OR EOR LABEL   
          S         A12,LABSTD          . 
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF         . WRITE EOF    
          A         A11,A9              . UPDATE TOTAL RECORDS OUTPUT   
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . TEST IF MASS STORAGE
          DSL       A8,72             . ZERO A8 AND A9
          TZ        FASTFILEOUT       . TEST IF TPE OR MASS 
          J         0,X10             . RETURN IF MASS STORAGE    
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          J         0,X7               .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
          DL        A2,OUTPKT           . FILE NAME   
          DS        A2,ENDRMM           . 
          L,H2      A2,RLOUT          . LOAD OUTPUT REEL COUNT    
          LMJ       X9,OTD             . CONVERT TO DECIMAL 
          S         A3,TEMP           .   
          LSSL      A3,24             .   
          S         A3,TEMP2          .   
          L         A3,ENDRMM+3       .   
          L         R2,(0777700,0) .
          MLU       A3,TEMP2          .   
          S         A4,ENDRMM+3       .   
          L         A3,TEMP           .   
          L         A2,LABSTD+3         . RECORD
          LMJ       X9,OTD             . CONVERT TO DECIMAL 
          S         A3,ENDRMM+5         . 
          L         A2,LABSTD+2         . BLOCK COUNT 
          LMJ       X9,OTD             . CONVERT TO DECIMAL 
          S         A3,ENDRMM+8         . 
          L         A0,ENDRMP           . REEL NR, RECORD CT, BLOCK CT, FILE NAM    
          ER        PRINT$              . 
          J         0,X10              . RETURN 
.     
. COMMON CODE FOR HEADER AND TRAILER WRITES     
.      (A2 = TYPE OF LABEL)   
.     
HTCOMMON.   
          . CALL VIA LMJ X8,HTCOMMON
          TZ,S6     OUTLBS    
          J         NOUSEOL           .   
          LMJ       X9,DOUSRH         . USER CHECKING OUT LABELS  
          J         0,X8              . CONTINUE PROCESSING 1 RET 
          J         NOUSEOL           . WRITE LABEL 2 RET   
          J         HTC1              . WRITE EOF MARK F RETURN   
          L         A2,'EOR'          . LOAD FOR END OF REEL
NOUSEOL.    
          S,H1      A2,LABSTD         . STORE TYPE OF LABEL 
          DS        A8,LABSTD+2         . BLOCK AND REC CT TO LABEL     
          TZ,S4     LABSTD            . TO ILLIMINATE I/O05 CFH LABELED OUTPUTFILESORT    
          J         $+7               . IS NOT THE EOF 'MARK'- BUT THE EOF LABEL    
          L         A5,OUTPKT+4       . KEEP THIS     
          L         A1,(0,LABSTD)     . FOR EOF 'MARK' TO USE MAX BLKSIZE     
          S         A1,OUTPKT+4       .   FOR EOF 'MARK' BKSIZE   
          L         A1,BLKLGN         .      USE MAX BLKSIZE OF DATA BLOK     
          S,H1      A1,OUTPKT+4       .              AND STORE IT TO BE USED  
          J         $+4               . THEN SKIP EOF "LABELING" CODE   
          L         A1,(28,LABSTD)  
          L         A5,OUTPKT+4         . SAVE ACW    
          S         A1,OUTPKT+4         . ACW FOR LABEL     
          LMJ       X11,WRITE          . WRITE BLOCK  
          S         A5,4,A0             . RESTORE ACW 
          TNZ       A1                  . GOOD  
          J         HTC1                . YES   
          TE,U      A1,2                . IGNORE END OF REEL
          LMJ       X9,IOERR            . 
HTC1. 
          TZ        MOREUWL           .   
          J         $+3               .   
          S         X8,MOREUWL       .    
          J         HTCOMMON          .   
          TNZ       NOEOFF            .   
          J         $+3               .   
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF        .   
          J         0,X8              .   
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,NOEOFF         .   
          J         0,X8               . RETURN 
/.    
. **********************************************************************
GETREC.     
.     
. GET NEXT RECORD FROM INPUT FILE   
.      (A1 = INPUT FILE I/O PACKET ADDRESS)     
.      (ON RETURN, A0 = ADDRESS OF RECORD)
.     
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          TNZ       STDLBL             .  
          J         MARGETREC          .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
          LA,H2     A0,3,A1            . GET THE INPUT WORD COUNT 
          TZ        STDLBL            . TEST IF FORM01
          LA,H2     A0,COUNTR          . YES USE LABEL WORD COUNT 
          AA,H2     A0,4,A1            . FIND THE END 
          TLE       A0,7,A1             . POINTER TO NEXT RECORD  
          J         $+3                 . 
          TE        A0,7,A1             . 
          J         GETR7               . 
          L,H2      A0,4,A1             . INPUT BUFFER ADDRESS    
          TZ        STDLBL            . TEST IF FORM01
          A,U       A0,1                . PLUS 1 IF LABELED TAPE  
          S         A0,7,A1             . INITIALIZE POINTER TO NEXT RECORD   
.     
. READ NEXT BLOCK 
GETR1.
          L         A0,A1               . I/O PACKET ADDRESS
          LMJ       X10,READ           . READ NEXT BLOCK    
          L         A1,A0               . 
          TNZ,S1    3,A1                . GOOD I/O?   
          J         GETR7A             . YES GOOD     
          L,S1      A2,3,A1             . GET STATUS  
          TE,U      A2,1                . EOF?  
          LMJ       X9,IOERR            . NO, ERROR   
          TNZ       STDLBL              . TEST FOR LLABELED TAPE  
          J         GETR4               . NON-LABELED 
          TZ        8,A1                . TEST IF FASTRAND INPUT  
          J         MOREF             .   
.     
. LABELED TAPE END OF REEL OR FILE  
          L,U       A2,28               . NUM WORDS IN LABEL TO ACW     
          L         A12,4,A1            . SAVE ACW    
          S,H1      A2,4,A1             . 
          LMJ       X10,READ           . READ TRAILER LABEL 
          L         A1,A0               . 
          TZ,S1     3,A1                . GOOD STATUS?
          LMJ       X9,IOERR            . YES   
          S         A12,4,A1            . RESTORE ACW 
          L,H2      A2,4,A1             . ADDRESS OF INPUT BUFFER 
          L,H1      A3,0,A2             . GET FIRST WORD OF LABEL BLOCK 
          TE,U      A3,'EOF'  
          J         EORTEST   
MOREF.
          TNE       A10,(' MERGE')    . MERGE ? 
          J         0,X8              .   
          TNZ       FILECNT   
          J         0,X8
          LA        A14,FILECNT     
          AN,U      A14,1     
          S         A14,FILECNT     
          L,U       A15,FILNAMES    
          LSSL      A14,1     
          A         A15,A14   
          L         A2,A15    
          DL        A14,0,A2  
          DS        A14,INPKT 
          DS        A14,FACPKT        .   
          S         A0,TEMP1          .   
          L,U       A0,FACPKT         .   
          ER        FACIL$            .   
          L,S1      A0,FACPKT+6       .   
          SZ        INPKT+8           . DEFAULT TO TAPE AS EQUIPMENT    
          TG,U      A0,020            .   
          S         A0,INPKT+8        .   
          SZ        FASTFILEIN        .   
          TG,U      A0,020            .   
          S         A0,FASTFILEIN     .   
          L,U       A0,INPKT  
          SZ        5,A0
          SZ        9,A0              .   
          SZ        6,A0
          TNZ       STDLBL            .   
          J         $+2               .   
          LMJ       X11,HEADCK
          L,U       A1,INPKT  
          TNZ       STDLBL            . FORM01  
          J         GETREC            . GO TO GET RECORD    
          J         GETR1     
EORTEST.    
          TE,U      A3,'EOR'            . TEST FOR END OF REEL    
          J         GETR3               . NO, BAD LABEL     
          TNZ       CKPFLG             .  
          LMJ       X10,CKPNT          .  
RETCKA    . 
          ER        TSWAP$            . SWAP REELS    
          LMJ       X11,HEADCK         . CHECK HEADER 
          L         A1,A0               . 
          J         GETR1               . 
GETR3     S,H2      A3,BTLM+3           . 
          L         A0,BTLP             . BAD TRAILER LABEL 
          J         ERROR1    
.     
. NON-LABELED TAPE END OF REEL OR FILE    
GETR4.
          DL        A2,0,A1             . INPUT FILE NAME   
          DS        A2,CMOM+4           . TO CONSOLE MESSAGE
          L,U       A0,CMPK           . END OF FILE OR REEL 
          ER        COM$              .   
          L,S1      A2,CINB             . GET ANSWER  
          TNE,U     A2,'F'              . FILE  
          J         MOREF             .   
          TE,U      A2,'R'              . REEL  
          J         GETR4               . NO, GO ASK AGAIN  
          TNZ       CKPFLG             .  
          LMJ       X10,CKPNT          .  
RETCKB    . 
          L         A0,A1               . 
          ER        TSWAP$            . SWAP INPUT TAPES    
          J         GETR1               . 
.     
GETR7A     .
          TNZ       STDLBL            . TEST IF FORM01
          J         GETR7             . JUMP NO CT WORDS    
          LA,H2     A0,4,A1            . GET THE INPUT BUFFER ADDRESS   
          LA,H2     A2,0,A0            . GET THE BLOCK WORD COUNT 
          TE,H2     A2,CHKWD          . IS THIS CKPT DESIGNATOR   
          J         $+3               . NO CONTINUE READING DATA BLOCKS 
          LMJ       X6,CHECKPT        . YES SKIP OVER BLKS AND CKPT EOF 
          J         GETR1             . GO FOR NXT DATA BLOCK     
          SA,H2     A2,COUNTR          . SAVE THE BLOCK WORD COUNT
          LA        A2,1,A0            . GET THE SORT CONTROL WORD
          SA        A2,QLPREC          .  
. SET RECORD ADDRESS IN A0 AND RETURN     
GETR7.
          L         A0,7,A1             . POINTER TO NEXT RECORD  
          TZ        STDLBL            . TEST IF STANDARD LABEL    
          A,U       A0,1                . PLUS 1 IF LABELED TAPE  
          TNZ       VARFLG             . VARIABLE LENGTH RECORDS  
          J         GETR7AB            . YES    
          L         A2,0,A0           . LOAD FIRST WORD OF REC    
          LMJ       X5,NINET          . CHECK FOR FORM01 PADDING OF NINES.    
          J         GETR4             .  FILLER JUMP  
          TE,H2     A2,CHKWD          . IS THIS CKPT DESINATOR    
          J         $+3               . NO CONTINUE   
          LMJ       X6,CHECKPT        . YES SKPT OVER CKPT EOF    
          J         GETR1             . GO FOR NXT DATA BLK 
          L         A2,A0               . 
          A         A2,RECSIZ           . UPDATE POINTER WITH RECORD SIZE     
          S         A2,7,A1             . SAVE UPDATED POINTER    
          J         1,X8               . RETURN WITH ADDRESS IN A0
GETR7AB   . 
          LA,H2     A2,7,A1            . GET THE POINT
          LXI       A0,0,A2            . GET WORD COUNT IN A0     
          TE,H2     A0,CHKWD          . CKPT DESIGANTOR FOR FOR01 ?     
          J         $+3               . NO CONTINUE   
          LMJ       X6,CHECKPT        . YES SKIP THROUGH CKPT EOF 
          J         GETR1             . GET NEXT DATA BLOCK 
          AA,H2     A2,0,A2            . POINT TO NEXT RECORD     
          AA,U      A2,1               .  
          SA        A2,7,A1            . STORE POINTER
          J         1,X8               . RETURN 
.     
.     
NINET .     
          TE        A2,NINES          . IS THIS REC FORM01 PAD 9'S?     
          J         1,X5              . NO  CONTINUE  
          A         A0,RECSIZ         . INCREMENT BY ONE RECORD   
          S         A0,7,A1           . POINT TO NEXT RECORD
          L         A2,0,A0           . GET FIRST WORD OF THAT RECORD   
          TNE       A2,NINES          . IS IT A PAD 9'S FOR FORM01 AGAIN?     
          J         $-4               . YES  INCREMENT ADDRESS OF REC AGAIN   
          J         0,X5              . NO  RETURN TO J GETR4  FOR END OF FILE
          ON        MARTIME            .  
MARGETREC . 
          TS        BUFFDUB            .  
          LA,H2     A0,BUFFDUB+1       .  
TESTAV    . 
          TNE,H2    A0,BUFFDUB         .  
          J         WAITOUT            .  
          AU        A0,RECSIZ          .  
          TG,H1     A1,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          LA,U      A1,BUFFDUB+2       .  
          LA        A3,0,A0            .  
          TNE       A3,INCOMPB         .  
          J         WAITOUT            .  
          TE        A3,EOFSENT         .  
          J         RECGOOD            .  
          AA        A0,BLKLGN          .  
          TG,H1     A0,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          LA,U      A0,BUFFDUB+2       .  
          SA,H2     A0,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          J         TESTAV             .  
RECGOOD   . 
          SA,H2     A1,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          SZ,S1     BUFFDUB            .  
          J         1,X8               .  
WAITOUT   SZ,S1     BUFFDUB            .  
          LA        A1,ACTNUM          .  
          TZ        NAMESA             .  
          J         DOWAIT             .  
          TNE,U     A1,01              .  
          J         DONEINPUT          =  
          TZ        NAMESB             .  
          J         DOWAIT             .  
          TNE,U     A1,02              .  
          J         DONEINPUT          .  
          TNZ       NAMESC             .  
          J         DONEINPUT          .  
DOWAIT    . 
          LA,U      A1,03              .  
          ER        TWAIT$             .  
          J         MARGETREC          .  
DONEINPUT . 
          LA,U      A1,BUFFDUB+2       .  
          SA,H2     A1,BUFFDUB         .  
          SA,H2     A1,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          J         0,X8               .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
/.    
. **********************************************************************
HEADCK.     
. READ TAPE LABEL AND TEST FOR 'HDR',REEL NR, EOF,AND BLK AND RCD SIZE  
.     
. FORMAT OF TAPE LABEL  
. WORD    PARTIAL   CONTENT   
.   1     H1        'HDR'     
.   2     H1        BLOCK SIZE
.   2     H2        RECORD SIZE     
.   5               DATE
.   6     H2        REEL NUMBER (BINARY)  
. END OF FILE MUST FOLLOW LABEL BLOCK     
. (A0 = INPUT PACKET ADDRESS) 
.     
          . CALL VIA LMJ X11,HEADCK 
NOUSEIL.    
          DS        A0,TEMP1          . SAVE INPKT ADDRESS HERE   
          L,U       A2,28               . NUM WORDS IN LABEL
          L         A12,4,A0            . SAVE BLOCK LENGTH 
          S,H1      A2,4,A0             . 
          L,U       A2,ENPKT1         . GET ADD OF BUFF     
          S,H2      A2,4,A0           . STORE IN I/O PKT    
          LMJ       X10,READ           . READ BLOCK   
          TZ,S1     3,A0              .   
          LMJ       X9,IOERR            . BAD IO
          TZ,S6     INLBSW            . TEST USER CHECKING  LABELS
          J         $+6               . NO CONTINUE   
          LMJ       X9,CKUSRH         . LET USER CHECK LABEL
          J         0,X11             . CONTINUE  1  RET    
          J         NOUSEIL           . READ LABEL 2 RET    
          NOP       . DUMMY FOR HEADER    
          NOP       . DUMMY FORE HEADER   
. TEST FOR 'HDR'  
          TZ        USLBMOD           .   
          J         LABGO             .   
          S         A0,USLBMOD        .   
          L,U       A0,INPKT          .   
          J         HEADCK            .   
LABGO.
          L,H2      A1,4,A0             . INPUT BUFFER ADDRESS    
          L,H1      A2,0,A1             . GET FIRST 3 CHARACTERS  
          TE,U      A2,'HDR'            . IS IT HEADER?     
          J         NOHD              . NO
          TZ        QLPFLG             . A QLP SORT   
          J         NOQLPS             . NO     
          DL        A2,26,A1           . GET FILE NAME
          DS        A2,ERRIOP          . STORE FILE-NAME IN I/O PACKET  
NOQLPS    . 
. TEST FOR END OF FILE  
          L,S4      A2,0,A1           . LOAD TYPE OF FILE   
          AN,U      A2,023            . TEST IF OMITTED     
          TE,U      A2,1              . 024 IS O
          J         $+5               . NO FIT CHECK STD    
          TNE       A2,STDLBL         . TEST IF CHECKS
          J         CFLBOK            . CHECKS OK     
          L         A0,BADHP          . DOES CHECK ERROR    
          J         ERROR             . ERROR OUT     
          AN,U      A2,2              . CHECK FOR STANDARD  
          TE,U      A2,3              . IS IT STANDARD ?    
          J         $-4               . MUST BE ERROR 
          TE        A2,STDLBL         . CHECK WITH PARA     
          J         $-6               . NO CHECK IS ERROR   
CFLBOK    TNZ       STDLBL            . CHECK THAT STD
          J         CKHDR0              . NO    
          LMJ       X10,READ           . READ AGAIN   
          SZ        HDGFLG            . SET HDG FLG TO ZERO 
          L,H2      A1,4,A0             . INPUT BUFFER ADDRESS    
          L,S1      A2,3,A0             . GET STATUS  
          TE,U      A2,1                . NO EOF, ERROR     
          J         NOEOF               . NO EOF IS ERROR   
. TEST FOR SEQUENTIAL REEL NUMBER   
CKHDR0    TZ        8,A0                . TEST IF FASTRAND INPUT  
          J         CKHDR01             . YES, BYPASS REEL CHECK  
          L,H2      A2,6,A0           . LOAD LAST REEL NUMBER     
          A,U       A2,1                . 
          TNZ       SEQCKS             . SEQUENCE CHECK     
          J         NONSEQCK           .  
          TE,H2     A2,5,A1             . DOES IT MATCH LABEL     
          J         WRONGRL           . NO
NONSEQCK  . 
          S,H2      A2,6,A0           . STORE UPDATED REEL NUMBER 
CKHDR01   S         A12,4,A0            . RESTORE BLOCK LENGTH    
.     
. IF REEL ONE, CALCULATE BLOCK DATA 
          L,H2      A4,1,A1             . GET REC SIZE FROM LABEL 
          TNZ       SEQCKS             . NON SEQUENCE CHECK 
          J         $+3                . YES ALL LABELS     
          TE,U      A2,1                . TEST FOR REEL ONE 
          J         CHKSZ               . 
          L,H1      A3,1,A1             . GET BLOCK SIZE    
          S,H1      A3,4,A0             . STORE BLOCK SIZE IN ACW 
          S         A3,BLKLGN         . STORE BLOCK LENGTH  
          TZ        STDLBL            . TEST FOR FORM01     
          AN,U      A3,1                . DECREMENT FOR BLOCK CONTROL WRD     
          SSL       A2,36               . 
          TZ        STDLBL            . TEST FOR FORM01AGAIN
          A,U       A4,1                . INCREMENT FOR RECORD CONTROL WRD    
          DI        A2,A4               . BLOCK SIZE / RECORD SIZE
          TNZ       VARFLG             . VARIABLE LENGTH RECORDS  
          J         $+3                . YES    
          TZ        A3                  . TEST IF NON INTEGER     
          J         NOFIX               . YES, ERROR  
          S         A2,BLKSIZ           . SAVE NR RCDS PER BLK FOR OUTPUT     
          SZ        REQBLK    
.     
. IF REEL ONE, CALCULATE RECORD DATA
CHKDR1    L,H2      A2,1,A1             . LABEL RECORD SIZE 
          TZ        REQRSZ              . TEST IF RSZ INPUT 
          J         CHKHD2              . NO    
          TE        A2,RECSIZ           . TEST IF LABEL = INPUT   
          J         WRSZ                . NO, ERROR   
          J         CHKHD3              . 
CHKHD2    SZ        REQRSZ              . 
          S         A2,RECSIZ           . SAVE RECORD SIZE  
          LXI       A2,(010000)         . 
          L         A3,PARPNT           . POINTER TO PARAMETER TABLE    
          S         A2,0,A3             . 
          A,U       A3,1                . INCREMENT POINTER 
          S         A3,PARPNT           . 
.     
. IF FIRST TAPE, TRANSFER TO LABEL BUFFER AND   
. CALCULATE OUTPUT BLOCK LENGTH     
CHKHD3    TNZ       LABSTD              . TEST IF 1ST INPUT 
          J         $+3               . CONTINUE  IS FIRST TIME
