          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          J         0,X11             . RETURN TO CALLER    
          L,U       R1,28               . 
          LXI,U     A1,1                . 
          L         A2,(1,LABSTD)       . 
          BT        A2,0,*A1            . TRANSFER LABEL    
          L         A1,A0               . 
          ER        TDATE$              . GET DATE    
          S         A0,LABSTD+4         . STORE DATE IN LABEL     
          DL        A0,TEMP1            . RESTORE A0 A1     
          LMJ       X9,OUTBCK         . GET OUTPUT SIZE     
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          J         0,X11              RETURN TO CALLER     
. IF NOT REEL 1, CHECK RECORD SIZE  
CHKSZ.
          TE        A4,RECSIZ           . TEST IF SAME RECORD SIZE
          J         BADH                . 
          J         0,X11              . YES, RETURN  
.     
. CHECK UNLABLED PARAMETERS   
. AND CALCULATE BLOCK SIZE IN WORDS 
.     
UCFHCK    TNZ       REQRSZ            . TEST IF RECSIZE GIVEN     
          TZ        REQBLK             TEST IF REQ BLK PARA GIVEN 
          J         REQE              . JUMP TO ERROR REQ PARA MISSING  
          DS        A0,TEMP1          . SAVE A0 A1    
          TNZ       BLIFLG            . TEST IF BLKSIZE IN WORDS  
          J         BINREC            . YES JUMP
BINLP     L         A1,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD  SIZE   
          L         A2,BLKSIZ         . LOAD BLOCKING FACTOR
          MSI       A1,A2             . FIND DATA WORDS PER BLK   
          TNZ       STDLBL            . TEST IF STANDARD LABEL    
          J         $+3               . JUMP  IF FORM01     
          A         A1,A2             . ADD RECORD CT WORDS 
          A,U       A1,1              . ADD BLOCK CT WORD   
          S         A1,BLKLGN         . STORE BLOCK SIZE    
          S,H1      A1,4,A0           . STORE IN READ PACKET
          J         OUTBCK            . JUMP TO CHECK OUTPUT
BINREC    L         A1,BLKLGN         .  LOAD BLOCK LENGTH  
          S,H1      A1,INPKT+4        . SET INPUT BLOCK SIZE
          SZ        A0                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          L         A2,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD SIZE    
          DI        A0,A2             . FIND BLOCKING FACTOR
          S         A0,BLKSIZ         . STORE BLOCKING FACTOR     
          TNZ       CICFLG            . TEST IF CONVERT PARA   ?  
          J         BINLP             . YES ADD CT WORDS    
OUTBCK    TNZ       OBKFLG            . TEST IF OUTPUT GIVEN
          J         $+7               . YES DO CALCULATIONS 
          L         A1,BLKSIZ         . LOAD BLOCKSIZE
          L         A2,BLKLGN         . LOAD INPUT BLOCK LENGTH   
          S         A1,OUTBLKSIZ      . STORE AS OUT BLOCKING FACTOR    
          S         A2,NEWBLKLN       . STORE BLOCK LENGTH  
          S,H1      A2,OUTPKT+4       . STORE OUTPUT BLOCK SIZE   
          J         BLKCKF            . GO TO END     
          L         A1,OUTLENG        . LOAD CONVERT LENGHT 
          TNZ       A1                . TEST IF GIVEN 
          L         A1,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD SIZE    
          TNZ       BLOFLG            . TEST IF FACTOR OR SIZE    
          J         BOUREC            . JUMP IT IS SIZE     
BOULP     L         A2,OUTBLKSIZ      . LOAD OUT BLOCK FACTOR     
          MSI       A1,A2             . FIND DATA WORDS PER BLOCK 
          TNZ       STDLBL            .  IS THIS UNLABELED  
          J         $+3               . YES  IGNORE CONTROL WORDS 
          A         A1,A2             . ADD  RECORD CONTROL WORDS 
          A,U       A1,1              . ADD BLOCK CT WORD   
          S         A1,NEWBLKLN       . STORE NEW BLOCK LENGTH    
          S,H1      A1,OUTPKT+4       . SET OUTPUT BLOCK SIZE     
          J         BLKCKF            . JUMP TO END   
BOUREC    L         A2,A1             . MOVE REC SIZE TO A2 
          SZ        A0                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          L         A1,NEWBLKLN       . LOAD BLOCK LENGTH   
          S,H1      A1,OUTPKT+4       . SET OUTPUT BLOCK SIZE     
          DI        A0,A2             . FIND BLOCKING FACTOR
          S         A0,OUTBLKSIZ      . STORE OUT BLOCKING FACTOR 
          L         A1,A2             . MOVE REC SIZE TO A1 
          TNZ       COCFLG            . TEST IF CONVERT PARA ?    
          J         BOULP             . JUMP IF PARA CONVERT
BLKCKF.     
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          L         A2,('CARDS ')     . CHECK IF CARD INPUT 
          TE        A2,INPKT          . TEST IF CARD INPUT  
          J         0,X9              . RETURN TO CALLER    
          TNZ       STDLBL              . NO    
           J          0,X9             . RETURN TO CALLER   
          L,U       A0,'S'              . S FOR STANDARD LABEL    
          S,S4      A0,LABSTD           . TO LABEL    
          ER        TDATE$              . GET DATE    
          S         A0,LABSTD+4         . STORE DATE IN LABEL     
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          J         0,X9              . RETURN TO CALLER    

.     
. WRONG REEL NUMBER. REWIND, SEND OPERATOR MESSAGE AND
. GO TO READ NEXT TAPE LABEL  
WRONGRL.    
          DL        A3,0,A0             . INPUT FILE NAME   
          DS        A3,WRLM+6           . TO ERROR MSG
          L         R2,A0               . SAVE INPUT PACKET ADDRESS     
          LMJ       X9,OTD             . CONVERT REEL NUMBER TO DECIMAL FD    
          S         A3,TEMP2          .   
          L         A3,WRLM+4         .   
          L         R2,(0,07777)      .   
          MLU       A3,TEMP2          .   
          S         A4,WRLM+4         .   
          L,U       A0,WCMP           . OUTPUT CONSOL MSG   
          ER        COM$                . 
          L         A0,R2               . RESTORE INPUT PACKET ADDRESS  
          L,U       A1,041            . REWIND INTERLOCK MSG
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          ER        IOW$              . REWIND TAPE   
          L,U       A1,020            . RESET FUNCTION
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          J         HEADCK             . CHECK HEADER ON NEW TAPE 
.     
. ERRORS    
BADH      L         A0,BADHP            . LABEL BLK OR RCD SIZE NO AGREE
          J         ERROR1              . 
WRSZ      L         A0,WRSZP            . PARM CARD AND HDR DO NOT AGREE
          J         ERROR1              . 
NOFIX     L         A0,NFIXP            . BLK LGTH NOT MULTIPLE OF RCD  
          J         ERROR1              . 
NOHD      L         A0,NOHP             . HDR LABEL MISSING FROM INPUT  
          J         ERROR1              . 
NOEOF     L         A0,NEOFP            . EOF DOES NOT FOLLOW LABEL BLK 
          J         ERROR1              . 
/.    
. *****************************************************************************     
WRITE.
.     
. WRITE A BLOCK     . IF FASTRAND FORMAT, UPDATE SECTOR ADDRESS   
.     
          . CALL VIA LMJ X11,WRITE  
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT           . 
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          TNZ       STDLBL             .  
          LA,U      A0,OUTPKT1         . ON MARTIME *** REPLACE  LA,U A0,OUTPKT     
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
          TZ,S6     OUTBKS    
          J         NOUSEOB           . NO  JUMP
          LMJ       X9,DOUSRB         . LET USER CHECK OUT BLOCK  
          J         0,X11             . DELETE BLOCK RETURN 
          J         WRITE1            . USR DID WRITE 
          J         $+1               . USER MODIFIED BLOCK 
NOUSEOB   L,U       A0,OUTPKT         . LOAD ADDR OF OUPUT IO PKT 
          ER        IOW$                . WRITE 1 BLOCK     
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       .   
          J         WRITE1              . NO    
          TZ        OHDFLG           . IS IT HEADING IO?    
          J         $+4               . YES JUMP OVER VAR LOAD BLKSZ    
          L,H2      A1,4,A0           . LOAD BUFFER AREA    
          L,H2      A2,0,A1           . LOAD BLOCK SIZE     
          TZ        VARFLG            . IS IT VARIABLE RECORDS ?  
          L,H1      A2,4,A0           . NO LOAD PKT SIZE AS BLKSIZE     
          SZ        A1                .  CLEAR FOR DIVIDE   
          DI,U      A1,28               . DIVIDE BY 28 TO GET NR SECTOS 
          TZ        A2                  . TEST IF PARTIAL SECTOR  
          A,U       A1,1                . YES, INCREMENT NUMBER BY 1    
          A         A1,OUTPKT+5         . UPDATE FASTRAND ADDRESS 
          S         A1,OUTPKT+5         . 
WRITE1    L,S1      A1,3,A0             . GET STATUS  
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
          ON        MARTIME            .  
WRITEAA   . 
          TS        BUFFDUB            .  
          LA,H2     A1,BUFFDUB         .  
          TNE,H2    A1,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          J         WAITWRT            .  
          SA,H2     A1,OUTPKT1+4       .  
          AU        A1,BLKLGN          .  
          TG,H1     A2,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          LA,U      A2,BUFFDUB+2       .  
          SA,H2     A2,BUFFDUB         .  
          SZ,S1     BUFFDUB            .  
          LMJ       X10,WRTIT          .  
          J         WRITEAA            .  
WAITWRT   . 
          SZ,S1     BUFFDUB            .  
          TZ,S2     BUFFDUB            .  
          J         EOFTIME            .  
          LA,U      A1,015             .  
          ER        TWAIT$             .  
          J         WRITEAA            .  
EOFTIME   . 
          SZ        NAMESD             .  
          ER        EXIT$              .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
/.    
. *************************************************************************   
READ. 
.     
. READ A BLOCK. IF FASTRAND FORMAT, UPDATE THE SECTOR ADDRESS     
.     
          . CALL VIA LMJ X10,READ      .  
          TZ        9,A0                . TEST IF EOF FOUND IN LAST READ
          J         READ1               . YES   
          ER        IOW$                . READ A BLOCK
          TZ        8,A0                . TEST IF FASTRAND FORMAT 
          J         READ0               . YES   
          L,U       A1,4                . 
          TE,S1     A1,3,A0             . TEST IF ABNORMAL FRAME COUNT STATUS 
          J         READ3               . NO    
          L,H2      A1,3,A0             . GET NR WORDS READ 
          AN,U      A1,1                . DECREMENT EVEN NR BY 1 TO MAKE ODD  
          S,H2      A1,3,A0   
          SZ,S1     3,A0                . CLEAR READ STATUS 
          J         READ3               . NO    
READ0     TNZ       CONVERTWORD        . TEST IF TAG OR SEL 
          J         $+3               . NO REG SORT JUMP    
          L         A1,5,A0           . LOAD DRUM ADDRESS   
          S         A1,ADRSAV         . STORE FOR RECORD    
          SZ        A1                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          TZ        HDGFLG            . NON ZEROREADING HEADING   
          J         $+7               . IF HDG JUMP OVER    
          TZ        VARFLG            . ZERO IS VARIABLE    
          J         $+5               . JUMP FOR FIXED LENGTH     
          L,H2      A1,4,A0           . LOAD BUFFER ADDRESS 
          L,H2      A2,0,A1           . LOAD BLOCK SIZE     
          S,H2      A2,3,A0           . STORE AS WORDS MOVED
          SZ        A1                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          L,H2      A2,3,A0             . GET NR WORDS READ 
          DI,U      A1,28               . DIVIDE BY 28 TO GET NR SECTORS
          TZ        A2                  . TEST IF PARTIAL SECTOR  
          A,U       A1,1                . YES, INCREMENT NR SECTORS READ BY 1 
          A         A1,5,A0             . UPDATE FASTRAND ADDRESS 
          S         A1,5,A0             . 
.     
.  IF LABELED FASTRAND FILE, TEST FOR EOF RECORD
          TNZ       STDLBL              . TEST FOR LABELED FILE   
          J         READ3               . NO    
          L,H2      A1,4,A0             . 
          L,H1      A2,0,A1             . GET 1ST WORD OF BUFFER  
          TE,U      A2,'EOF'            . TEST FOR EOF
          J         READ2               . 
READ1     L,U       A1,1                . SET I/O STATUS FOR EOF  
          S,S1      A1,3,A0             . 
          S         A1,HDGFLG         . SET HEADING FLAG    
          J         0,X10              . RETURN 
.     
. TEST FOR LAST PARTIAL BLOCK 
READ2     TNE,U     A2,'HDR'            . TEST IF HEADER    
          J         READ3               . YES   
          TNE       A2,BLKSIZ           . TEST IF PARTIAL BLOCK   
          J         READ3               . NO    
          TNZ       VARFLG             . VARIABLE LENGTH RECORDS  
          J         READ3              . YES    
          L,H2      A2,0,A1             . GET NR WORDS IN BLOCK   
          S,H2      A2,3,A0             . SET NR WORDS READ TO PARTIAL BLOCK  
          SZ        A1                  . 
          DI,U      A1,28               .  DIVIDE BY 28 TO GET NR SECTORS     
          TZ        A2                  . TEST IF PARTIAL SECTOR  
          A,U       A1,1                . YES, INCREMENT NR SECTORS BY 1
          MSI,U     A1,28               . 
          A,H2      A1,4,A0             . DETERMINE BUFFER ADDRESS OF EOF     
          S         A1,9,A0             . 
READ3     L,S1      A1,3,A0             . GET STATUS  
          TZ,S6     INPBLOKSW 
          J         0,X10             .   
          LMJ       X9,CKUSRB         . LET USER CK IN BLOCK
          J         READ              . DELETE BLOCK RETURN 
          J         $+1               . USER MODIFIED BLOCK RETURN
          L,S1      A1,3,A0           . NOR RETURN LOAD STATUS    
          J         0,X10             . RETURN TO CALLER    
          ON        MARTIME            .  
READAS    . 
          LA,U      A0,INPKT           .  
          LMJ       X11,READINS        .  
          J         READAS             .  
          SZ        NAMESA             .  
          ER        EXIT$              .  
READBS    . 
          LA,U      A0,INPKT1          .  
          LMJ       X11,READINS        .  
          J         READBS             .  
          TZ        FREEINF            .  
          J         CLRNAMEA           .  
          LA        A0,(3,FREE1)       .  
          ER        CSF$               .  
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       .  
CLRNAMEA  SZ        NAMESB  DD-2       .  
          ER        EXIT$              .  
READCS    . 
          LA,U      A0,INPKT2          .  
          LMJ       X11,READINS        .  
          J         READCS             .  
          TZ        FREEINF            .  
          J         CLRNAMEB           .  
          LA        A0,(3,FREE2)       .  
          ER        CSF$               .  
          JN        A0,CONTADD-2       .  
CLRNAMEB  SZ        NAMESC             .  
          ER        EXIT$     
READINS   . 
          TS        BUFFDUB            .  
          LA,H2     A1,BUFFDUB         .  
          AU        A1,BLKLGN          .  
          TLE,H2    A1,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          J         $+2                .  
          J         IOTODOOK           .  
          TG,H2     A2,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          J         WAITRD             .  
IOTODOOK  . 
          TG,H1     A2,BUFFDUB+1       .  
          LA,U      A2,BUFFDUB+2       .  
          TNE,H2    A2,BUFFDUB+1        . 
          J         WAITRD              . 
          SA,H2     A2,BUFFDUB         .  
          LA        A3,INCOMPB         .  
          SA        A3,0,A1            .  
          SZ,S1     BUFFDUB            .  
          SA,H2     A1,4,A0            .  
          ER        IOW$               .  
          TNZ,S1    3,A0               .  
          J         0,X11              .  
          LA,S1     A2,3,A0            .  
          TNE,U     A2,02              .  
          J         $+3                .  
          TE,U      A2,01              .  
          ER        ERR$               .  
          LA        A3,EOFSENT         .  
          LA,H2     A2,4,A0            .  
          SA        A3,0,A2            .  
          J         1,X11     
WAITRD    . 
          SZ,S1     BUFFDUB            .  
          TZ,S2     BUFFDUB            .  
          J         1,X11              .  
          LA,U      A1,020             .  
          ER        TWAIT$             .  
          J         READINS            .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
. LMJ X6 FROM FD GERT7A GETR7AB GETR7 CODE
. THIS CODE SKIPS CHECKPOINT BLOCKS (747574747474)-EOF
. AND RETURNS TO GENT NXT DATA BLOCK (0,X6) WHICH GOES TO   
. GETR1 FOR NEXT BLOCK  
CHECKPT.    
          L         A0,A1             . SET UP FOR IO 
          L,H1      A2,3,A0           . LOAD STATUS AND FUNCTION  
          S         A2,CKFUN          . SAVE    
          L,U       A2,052            . LOAD MOVE CSF$ FUNCTION CODE    
          S,S2      A2,3,A0           . MST BE 12/16 OR 20 TAPE UNIT    
          ER        IOW$              . DO MOVE 
          L         A2,CKA1           . LOAD STATUS AFTER IO
          TE,U      A2,2              . IS THIS AN END OF TAPE ?  
          J         $+5               . NO IT'S A CKPOINT EOF THEN
          L         A0,A1             . YES SET UP  THEN    
          ER        TSWAP$            . SWAP TO NXT TAPE OF MULTI REEL  
          L         A0,A1             . RESET   
          J         $-9               . GO DO MOVE PAST CKPT 'EOF'
          L         A2,CKFUN          . RETIEVE STATUS AND FUNCTION     
          S,H1      A2,3,A0           . FOR READING DATA BLKS AGAIN     
          SZ        9,A0              . THIS SAYS NO WE HAVE NOT FOUND EOF    
          L         A1,A0             . READYFOR NXT IOW$ OF DATA BLKS  
          J         0,X6              . RETURN TO GET (R7'S OR BLK) FOR FD    
/.    
. * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * *     
.  THIS  SECTION  HANDLES  ALL ASCII   INPUT AND  OUTPUT    
.     
$(1). 
AOTD . CALLED VIA X9 A2 IS VALUE TO CHANGE A2 A3 IS RESULT  
          DS        A4,A4SAV2         . SAVE A4 AND A5
          S         X11,X11SA2        . SAVE X11
          L         A0,A2             . LOAD VALUE TO A0    
          LMJ       X11,REBD$       .  USE SORT SUB TO CHANGE TO FLD DATA     
          DL        A1,A4            . MOVE CHANGED VALUE TO A1 AND A2  
          L         A3,AZEROS         . LOAD ASCII ZEROES TO A3   
          L         A4,AZEROS        .     LOAD ASCII ZEROES TO A4
          L,U       A5,05             . LOAD FLD DATA SPACE TO A5 
          L         A0,(-9,63)        . LOAD CHARACTER SHIFT INDEX
AOTD1     S,S6      A2,FBLNK          . STORE NEXT CHARACTER
          TNE       A5,FBLNK            . CHECK IF NEXT VALUE IS SPACE  
          J         AOTD2             . JUMP IF IT IS A SPACE     
          DSL       A3,6              . MOVE 6 BITS TO A4   
          LSSL      A3,6              . RESET A3
          DSL       A2,6              . MOVE ONE FLD DATA NUM TO A3     
          LSSL      A2,6              . RESET A2
          DSL       A1,6              . MOVE ONE FLD DATA NUM TO A2     
          DSL       A3,3              . INSERT ZERO MAKING ASCII  
          JMGI      A0,AOTD1          . SET SHIFT INDEX     
          J         $+2               . 8 CHARACTERS NO SHIFT     
AOTD2     DSC       A3,9,A0           . SHIFT ANSWER  
          DL        A2,A3             . PUT ANS IN A3 AND A3
          DL        A4,A4SAV2         . RESTORE A4 AND A5   
          L         X11,X11SA2        . RESTORE X11   
          J         0,X9              . RETURN  
AHTCOM.     
.   COMMMON CODE FOR ASCII HEAD AND TRAIL LABELS
          TZ,S6     OUTLBS            .TEST IF USER EXIT    
          J         NOUL              .NO JUMP OUT    
          LMJ       X9,DOUSRH         . LET USER CHECK OUT HEADERS
          J         0,X8              . RETURN DONE WITH HEADERS  
          J         NOUL              . PROCESS LABEL 
          J         ATWT              . DO EOF    F RET     
          L         A2,(0105117126061)    
          S         A2,BUFR           .   
NOUL. 
          TZ        A3                .   
          J         ATWT              .   
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD CUTOFF   
          TG        A2,STDLBL         . TEST TYPE OF LABEL  
          J         HTC1A             . JUMP IF OMITTED OR NONE   
          L,U       A2,1              .   
          S,S2      A2,PKTADD         .   
          S,S1      A2,PKTADD         .   
          L,U       A0,PKTADD         .   
          ER        LABEL$            .   
          S         A0,LBHOLD         . STORE LABEL$ STATUS 
          TZ,S1     LBHOLD            . TEST IF GOOD IO     
          J         CKLABL            . LABEL$ ERR CK IF 012 TYPE 
          J         HTC1A             .   
ATWT      L,U       A0,OUTPKT         .   
          L         A4,OUTPKT+4       . SAVE BUFFER INFOR   
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD CUTOFF
