          S         A1,MBLKHL         . STORE MAS STOR SIZ  
          L         A0,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD SIZE    
          LMJ       X6,CKOVFL         . GO CHECK OVERFLOW SECTOR  
          TZ        VARFLG            . TEST IF VARIBLE     
          S         A1,MBLKHL         . STORE BASIC MASS BLOCK SIZE     
          S         A2,MBKSIZ         . SET BLOCKING FACTOR 
NFAST3    L         A1,OUTLENG        . LOAD CONVERT RECORD LENGHT
          TNZ       A1                . TEST IF GIVEN 
          L         A1,RECSIZ         . NOT GIVE USE RECORD SIZE  
          TNZ       BLOFLG            . TEST CHARACTER OR FACTOR  
          J         NFAST4            . JUMP CHARACTER
NFAST7    L         A2,OUTBLKSIZ      . LOAD OUT FACTOR     
          MSI       A1,A2             . FIND DATA WORDS     
          A         A1,A2             . ADD RECORD CT WORDS 
          A,U       A1,1              . ADD 1 BLOCK CONTROL WORD  
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . TEST IF TP OR MASS ?
          A,U       A1,1              . TAPE HAS TWO CT WORDS     
          S         A1,NEWBLKLN       . STORE OUT LENGHT    
          J         NFAST5            . JUMP    
NFAST4    L         A2,A1             . MOVE RECSIZE TO A2  
          L         A1,NEWBLKLN       . LOAD OUT SIZE 
          SZ        A0                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          DI        A0,A2             . FIND BLOCKING FACTOR
          TZ        A1                . REMAINDER AFTER DIVIDE?   
          A,U       A0,1              . YES  INCREMENT OUTPUT BLOCKING FACTOR 
          S         A0,OUTBLKSIZ      . STORE BLOCKING FACTOR     
          TZ        VARFLG            . IS IT VARIABLE
          J         $+2               . JUMP NOW ASCII BG   
          J         NFAST5            . NO JUMP 
          L         A1,OUTLENG        . LOAD OUTPUT LENGTH IF GIVEN     
          TNZ       A1                . TEST IF ONE GIVEN   
          L         A1,RECSIZ         . LOAD RECORD SIZE    
          J         NFAST7            . LOOP FOR RCA CT WORDS     
NFAST5    TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . TEST TP OR MASS ?   
          J         NFAST6            . JUMP TAPE     
          L         A1,NEWBLKLN       . LOAD SIZE     
          SZ        A0                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          A,U       A1,27             . DO COVERED DIVIDE   
          DI,U      A0,28             . FIND SECTORS  
          MSI,U     A0,28             . CONVERT BACK TO WORDS     
          DSL       A0,36             . MOVE TO A1    
          L         A0,OUTLENG        . LOAD OUTPUT SIZE    
          TNZ       A0                . TEST IF GIVEN 
          L         A0,RECSIZ         . NO LOAD RECRD SIZE  
          S         A1,NEWBLKLN       . STORE   
          LMJ       X6,CKOVFL         . GO CHECK OVERFLOW SECTORS 
          TZ        VARFLG            . TEST IF VARIABLE    
          S         A1,NEWBLKLN       . STORE OUTPUT  
NFAST6.     
          L         A2,NEWBLKLN       . LOAD OUT BLK SIZE   
          S,H1      A2,OUTPKT+4       . STORE OUTBLK SIZE   
          J         0,X9              . RETURN TO CALLER    
CKOVFL    AN,U      A1,1              . SUB FOR CT WORD     
          A,U       A0,1              . ADD FOR REC CT WORD 
          DSL       A1,36             . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          DI        A1,A0             . FIND BLOCKING FACTOR
          L         A2,A1             . SAVE BLOCKING FACTOR
          MSI       A1,A0             . CONVERT BACK TO WORDS     
          A,U       A1,1              . ADD BLOCK CT WORD BACK    
          J         0,X6              . RETURN  
/.    
.   ASCII    OUTPUT     
.     
ANSWRITE.   
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT         . LOAD ADDR OUPUT PKT 
          TZ,S6     OUTBKS            . IS USER CHECKING OUTPUT BLKS    
          J         ANSWRI            . NO  CONTINUE  
          LMJ       X9,DOUSRB         . YES LET CHECK 
          J         0,X11             . DELETED BLOCK RETURN
          J         ANSWRI+2          . USER WROTE BLOCK    
          J         $+1               . USER MODIFIED BLOCK 
ANSWRI    L,U       A0,OUTPKT         . LOAD ADDR OUTPKT    
          ER        IOW$.     
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       .  TEST IF FASTRAND OUTPUT  
          J         ANSWRITE1          .NO JUMP 
          SZ        A1  
          L,H1      A2,OUTPKT+4           .GET NO. OF WORDS WRITTEN     
          A,U       A2,27             .   
          DI,U      A1,28             . TEST IF PARTIAL BLOCK     
          A         A1,OUTPKT+5       .UPDATE FASTR ADDR    
          S         A1,OUTPKT+5       .   
ANSWRITE1 L,S1      A1,3,A0             .LOAD WRITE STATUS  
          J         0,X11             .RETURN   
/.    
.  CONTROL COMES HERE FOR ASCII  RECORD  OUTPUT 
.   RECORD WILL BE INSERTED IN BUFFER IF ROOM   
.  ELSE BUFFER WILL BE WROTE AND REC MOVED IN   
.   AS FIRST REC OF NEXT BLOCK
OUT1      TNZ       FSTOUT           .TEST IF FIRST BLOCK   
          J         OUT2              . JUMP IF NOT FIRST REC     
          SZ        FSTOUT            . SET TO NOT FIRST RECORD   
          AX,U      X3,1              . ADD FOR ONE BLK CT WRD    
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . CHECK IF MASS OR TAPE     
          AX,U      X3,1              . ADD ONE MORE FOR TAPE     
OUT2      TZ        VARFLG            . ZERO IF VARIABLE    
          J         $+3               . JUMP NOT VAR SORT   
          L         A6,A0             . LOAD CURR REC SIZE IF VAR 
          SSL       A6,18             . STRIP FOR REC SIZ   
          L         R1,A6             . LOAD TO BT REC
          L         A5,X3           .TEST FOR FULL BLOCK    
          AN        A5,(1,0)  
          AN,H2     A5,OUTPKT+4     
          S         A5,TEST1  
          TZ        FASTFILEOUT       . MASS STOR IS NON ZERO     
          J         $+5               . JUMP MSS STR CK CR REC    
          TZ        VARFLG            . IS TPE VAR OR FIXED 
          J         $+3               .  FIXED IS NON ZERO JMP    
          A         A5,RECSIZ         . ADD MAX RED SIZ FOR CK    
          J         $+2               . HAVE ADDED    
          A         A5,A6     
          A,U       A5,1
           TNG       A5,NEWBLKLN   . WILL REC FIT IN BLK ?  
          J         FILL           .NO GO TO FILL     
         TNZ        FASTFILEOUT     
         J          CNVT
         L,H2       X3,OUTPKT+4     
         L          A2,TEST1  
         S          A2,0,X3   
          LXI,U     X3,1              .LOAD INCR TO X3
          S,H1      A2,OUTPKT+4       . FOR VAR STORE NUM WDS IN IO PKT 
         J          NCNVT     
CNVT. 
          DS        A0,BUFR           . SAVE REGISTERS
          DS        A2,BUFR+2         . SAVE REGISTERS
          L         A2,TEST1          .   
          S         A0,TEST1          .   
          TNZ       VARFLG            . ZERO IS VARIABLE    
          S,H1      A2,OUTPKT+4       . STORE WDS CUR BLK IN IO PKT     
          LMJ       X9,AOTD           . GET ASCII DECIMAL NUMBER  
          L,H2      X3,OUTPKT+4       .   
          S         A3,1,X3           . STORE BLOCK CONTROL WORD  
          S         A2,0,X3           . STORE FIRST BLK CT WORD   
          DL        A0,BUFR           . RESTORE REGISTERS   
          DL        A2,BUFR+2         . RESTORE REGISTERS   
          LXI,U     X3,1
NCNVT.
          S         A0,TEST1          .   
          LMJ       X11,ANSWRITE    .GO WRITE BLOCK   
          A,U       A8,1              .? BUMP BLOCK COUNT   
         TNZ        A1  
         J          AWROK     
         TE,U       A1,2
         LMJ        X9,IOERR  
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD LABEL CUTOFF   
          TLE       A2,STDLBL         . TEST LABELS   
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF        . FOR STANDARD WRITE EOF    
         SZ         A2  
         S          X7,A13    
         LMJ        X10,ATRLWT
         LX         X7,A13    
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD LABEL CUTOFF   
          TG        A2,STDLBL         . TEST TYPE OF LABELS 
          J         $+3               . JUMP OMITTED LABELS 
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF        . GO WRITE EOF MARK AFTER EOV BLK 
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF        . GO WRITE EOF MARK AGAIN   
         L,U        A0,OUTPKT 
         ER         TSWAP$    
         LMJ        X10,AHDWR 
AWROK.
          L         A0,TEST1          .   
          A         A11,A7        .ADD REC COUNT
          SZ        A7  
          AN        A5,A6     
          A,U       X3,2
           TZ        FASTFILEOUT      .  TYPE OF STORAGE    
         AN,U       X3,1
FILL      L         A5,ARCDC          . LOAD DECIMAL RCW    
          TZ        VARFLG            . ZERO IS VAR   
          J         NOTVAS            . JUMP IF FIXED 
          L         A5,A6             . LOAD VAR REC SIZE   
          A,U       A5,1              . ADD 1 FOR CT WORD   
          TZ        FASTFILEOUT       . ZERO IF TAPE  
          J         NOTVAS            .  JUMP IF MASS STOR  
          L         A2,A5             . CONVRT TPE CT TO DEC
          L         A5,A0             . SAVE A0 
          LMJ       X9,AOTD           . CONVRT NUM TO A DEC 
          L         A0,A5             . RESTORE A0 FOR BT   
          L         A5,A3             . LOAD TPE CT WD IN DEC     
NOTVAS    S         A5,0,X3           . STORE  CT WD  
          TZ        FASTFILEOUT       . TAPE IS ZERO  
          S,H1      A5,0,X3           . FOR MASS STOR STOR IN H1  
          A,U       X3,1          .FILL BLOCK WITH REC
          A,U       A7,1
          LXI,U     A0,1
          BT        X3,0,*A0  
          A,U       A9,1
          J         0,X7          .RETURN 
/.    
.  CONTROL IS TRANSFERRED HERE WHEN ALL ASCII RECORDS RETURNED    
ANSFIN    LXI,U       X3,1        .WRITE LAST BLOCK IF ANY  
          TNZ       POPFLG            . TEST IF PRINT ONLY  
          J         PRTOTRCD          . YES     
          AN,H2     X3,OUTPKT+4   .AND PRINT TOTAL NO. RECS 
          AN,U      X3,2
          TNZ       X3  
          J         PRTOTRCD  
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT 
          A,U       X3,2
          S,H1      X3,4,A0   
          L,H2      A1,OUTPKT+4     
          SZ        A2                . CLEAR A2
          LXM       A2,X3             . LOAD REC PTR  
          TZ        FASTFILEOUT       .  NON ZERO MASS STOR 
          J         $+6               . RESET TO CORRECT JUMP FAST
          LMJ       X9,AOTD           . CONVERT TO DECIMAL  
          L,H2      A1,OUTPKT+4       . RELOAD BUFFER ADDR A1 DESTRT    
          S         A3,1,A1           . STORE DEC CNT WDS IN BLK  
          S         A2,0,A1           . STORE FIRST BLOCK CT WORD 
          J         $+2               . JUMP OVER MSS STOR STOR   
           S         A2,0,A1          . STORE BLK CT WD     
          A,U       A8,1
          LMJ       X11,ANSWRITE    
         TNZ        A1  
         J          $+3 
          TG,U      A1,3              . TEST 0 1 2 OK 
         LMJ        X9,IOERR  
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . TEST IF TAPE OR MASS
          J         ACOND             . CONTINE PROCESS IF TAPE   
          TNZ       STDLBL            . TEST IF NON ZERO LABEL    
          J         ACOND             . CONTINUE PROCESSING IF ZERO     
          L         A2,INPKT          . LOAD INPUT NAME     
          TE        A2,('CARDS ')     . CHECK IF CARD INPUT 
          J         ACOND             . DONT DO NOT CARDS INPUT   
          LMJ       X9,MKAHRD         . IF SO GO MAKE HEADERS     
          L,U       A1,OUTPKT         . LOAD ADDR OF OUT NAME IN FD     
          L,U       A2,ENPKT1+1       . LOAD DESTINATION    
          L,U       A0,2              . LOAD NUMBER WORDS TO CONVERT    
          LMJ       X11,FDASC         . CONVERT TO ASCII CH 
          L         A1,(1,ENPKT1+1)   . LOAD ASCII NAME ADDR
          L         A2,(1,ENPKT2+1)   . LOAD WHERE TO MOVE TO     
          L,U       R1,5              . LOAD NUM WORDS TO MOVE    
          BT        A2,0,*A1          . MOVE ASCII NAME TO EOF1   
ACOND    A          A11,A7    
          L         A2,A8            .  LOAD BLOCK COUNT    
          LMJ       X9,AOTD          . CONVERT ASCII OCTAL TO DECIMAL   
          S,H2      A2,ENPKT1+13      . STORE UP 2 DIGITS BLK CT  
          S         A3,ENPKT1+14     . STORE BLK CNT IN LABEL     
         TZ         FASTFILEOUT     
         J          FASTEND   
          TNZ       STDLBL            .TEST IF LABELLED TAPE
          J         PRTOTRCD          .NO GO TO END   
          L         A2,STDLBL         . LOAD TYPE OF LABEL  
          TNE,U     A2,1              . TEST IF OMITTED     
          J         $+8               . FOR OMIT JUST WRITE EOFS  
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF        .   
         L          A1,AEOF   
         S          A1,ENPKT1 
         L          A1,AEOF2  
         S          A1,ENPKT2 
         S          A1,A2     
         LMJ        X10,ATRLWT
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF        . WRITE HARDWARE EOF  
          LMJ       X11,WRTEOF        . WRITE SECOND HARDWARE EOF 
         J          PRTOTRCD  
FASTEND   L,U       A1,28            .  LOAD 1 SECTOR LENGTH IN WDS     
         S,H1       A1,OUTPKT+4     
          L         A1,OUTPKT+5      . LOAD MASS STOR EOF ADDR    
         S          A1,ENPKT1+20    
         L          A1,AHDR   
         S          A1,ENPKT1 
         L,U        A1,ENPKT1 
         S,H2       A1,OUTPKT+4     
         SZ         OUTPKT+5  
         L,U        A0,OUTPKT 
         ER         IOW$
         TZ,S3      OUTPKT+3  
         LMJ        X9,IOERR           .  ERROR 
         L,U        A1,2
         S          A1,OUTPKT+5     
          L         A1,STDLBL        . LOAD TYPE OF LABEL   
          AN,U      A1,1             . SET TO ASCII  TYPE LABEL   
          S         A1,ENPKT1+24     . STORE TYPE OF FILE IN LABEL
          L,U       A1,28            . LOAD LENGTH OF SECTOR IN WORDS   
         S,H1       A1,OUTPKT+4     
         L          A1,AEOF   
         S          A1,ENPKT1 
         L,U        A0,OUTPKT 
         ER         IOW$
         TZ,S3      OUTPKT+3  
         LMJ        X9,IOERR  
         J          PRTOTRCD  
/.    
.   THIS  SECTION  HANDLES   ASCII INPUT OF RECORDS   
.     
GETARC.     
          LA,H2     A0,3,A1           .  GET NO. OF WORDS READ    
          AA,H2     A0,4,A1           .  ADD TO BUFFER
          TLE       A0,7,A1           .TEST IF BLOCK USED UP
          J         $+3               .  NO     
          TE        A0,7,A1           .   
          J         GETEND            .   
          L,H2      A0,4,A1           .  LOAD BUFFER ADDR   
          TZ        8,A1              . TEST FASTRAND OR TAPE INPUT     
         AN,U       A0,1
          AA,U      A0,2              .ADD FOR BLOCK CONTROL WD   
          S         A0,7,A1           .  RESET POINTER
GETBLK.     
          L         A0,A1             .  GO READ NEW BLOCK  
          LMJ       X10,ANSREAD       .   
          LA        A1,A0             .   
          TNZ,S1    3,A1              .  TEST IF GOOD READ  
          J         GETEND            .  YES    
          L,S1      A2,3,A1           .  RELOA ERR STATUS   
          TE,U      A2,1              .  TEST IF EOF? 
          LMJ       X9,IOERR          .  NO ERROR OFF 
          TZ,S3     3,A1              .   
          J         TEORF             .   
          TZ        8,A1              . TEST FASTRAND OR TAPE     
          J         MORFLS            . SEE IF MORE FILES   
         TNZ        STDLBL    
         J          AASK
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD CUTOFF   
          TNE       A2,STDLBL         . TEST TYPE OF LABEL  
          J         MORFLS            . JUMP IF OMITTED LABELS    
          L         A4,4,A0           . SAVE BUFFER INFO    
          L,U       A2,BUFR           . LOAD BUFFER FOR LABEL     
          LXI,U     A2,20             . LOAD LABEL SIZE     
          S         A2,4,A0           . STORE IN IO PACKET  
          SZ,S1     3,A0              . MAKE SURE STATUS CLEAR    
          ER        IOW$              . READ LABEL    
          S         A4,4,A0           . RESTORE IO PACKET   
          L,U       A2,2              . LOAD FOR CHECK
          TNZ,S1    3,A0              . CHECK STATUS  
          J         $+3               . GOOD STATUS   
          TLE,S1    A2,3,A0           . TEST IF END OF BLOCK
         LMJ        X9,IOERR  
         L          A3,BUFR   
         TNE        A3,AEOF   
          J         MORFLS            . CHECK IF MORE INPUT FILES 
         TE         A3,AEOV1  
          J         MORFLS            . CHECK FOR MORE FILES
          SZ,S1     3,A0              . MAKE SURE STATUS CLEAR NEXT READ
         J          RTCKAB    
         LMJ        X9,IOERR  
AASK. 
          DL        A2,,A1              ASK FOR EOF OR EOR ?
          DS        A2,CMOM+4         .   
          L,U       A0,CMPK           .   
          ER        COM$              .   
          L,S1      A2,CINB           .   
          TNE,U     A2,'F'            .   
          J         MORFLS           .    
          TE,U      A2,'R'            .   
          LMJ       X9,IOERR          .   
RTCKAB    TNZ       CKPFLG            .  CHECKPOINT NEEDED? 
          LMJ       X10,CKPNT         .   
          L         A0,A1             .   
          ER        TSWAP$            .  SWAP TAPES   
          J         GETBLK            .  GO READ NEW BLOCK  
TEORF     L,S3      A2,3,A1   
          TNE,U     A2,1              .   
          J         RTCKAB            .   
MORFLS.     
          TNE       A10,(' MERGE')    .   
          J         0,X8              .   
          TNZ       FILECNT           .   
          J         0,X8              .   
          LA        A14,FILECNT       .   
          AN,U      A14,1             .   
          S         A14,FILECNT       .   
          L,U       A15,FILNAMES      .   
          LSSL      A14,1             .   
          A         A15,A14           .   
          LA        A2,A15            .   
          DL        A14,0,A2          .   
          DS        A14,INPKT         .   
          DS        A14,FACPKT        .   
          L,U       A0,FACPKT         .   
          ER        FACIL$            .   
          L,S1      A0,FACPKT+6       .   
          SZ        INPKT+8           . SET TO ZERO BEFORE CHECK  
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD TO SET FLAG    
          S,S1      A2,INPKT+11       . SET FILE AS NOT CHECKED   
          TG,U      A0,020            .   
          S         A0,INPKT+8        .   
          SZ        FASTFILEIN        .   
          TG,U      A0,020            .   
          S         A0,FASTFILEIN     .   
          L         A0,TCORE$         . LOAD BEGINING ADDRESS     
          A,U       A0,1              . ADD FOR 1 ASCII CT WORD   
          TNZ       FASTFILEIN        . TEST IF TPE OR MASS 
          A,U       A0,1              . ADD 1 MORE CT WORD ASCII TAPE   
          S         A0,INPKT+7        . STORE RECORD POINTER
          L,U       A0,INPKT          .   
          SZ        5,A0              .   
          SZ        6,A0              .   
          SZ        9,A0              .   
          LMJ       X11,ACKHD         . GO CHECK HEADER LABELS    
          L,U       A1,INPKT          .   
          J         GETBLK            .   
GETA. 
          LA,H2     A0,4,A1           .   
          LA,H2     A2,0,A0           .   
          LA        A2,1,A0           .   
          SA        A2,QLPREC         .   
GETEND.     
          L         A0,7,A1           .   
          A,U       A0,1              .   
          TNZ       VARFLG            .   
          J         GETAB          .
          L         A2,A0     
          A         A2,RECSIZ         .   
          S         A2,7,A1           .   
          J         1,X8              . RETURN WITH REC     
GETAB.
          LA,H2     A2,7,A1           .   
          TNZ       FASTFILEIN        . ZERO IS TAPE  
          J         VRTAPE            . MUMP TO VARIABLE TAPE INPUT     
          LXI       A0,0,A2           .   
          AA,H2     A2,0,A2           .   
          AN        A0,(1,0)          . SUB 1 FOR CT WORD   
          SA        A2,7,A1           .   
          J         1,X8              . RETURN WITH RECORD  
VRTAPE    DL        A5,A0             . SAVE A0 AND A1
          L         A1,0,A2           . LOAD RECSZ IN DECIMAL     
          SZ        A2                . SET FOR 1 WD CONVERT
          LMJ       X6,ADTO           . CONVERT SZ TO OCTAL 
          NOP       . NOP IS ERRO RETURN  
          DL        A0,A5             . RESTORE A0 AND A1   
          LXI       A0,A4             . LOAD RECSZ IN A0 H1 
          AN        A0,(1,0)          . MINUS 1 FOR CT WORD 
          L         A2,7,A1           . RESET REC POINTER   
          A         A2,A4             . ADD RECSZ AND 1 CT WD     
          S         A2,7,A1           . RESTET REC PTR TO NXT RC  
             J         1,X8              . RETURN TO FPOCA CDE A0 HAS RC CT   
ANSREAD.    
          TZ        9,A0
          J         ANSRD1    
          ER        IOW$
          TZ        8,A0              . TEST IF FASTRAND FORMAT   
          J         ARDO              .   
          L,U       A1,4
          TE,S1     A1,3,A0           .TEST IF ABNORMAL FRAME COUNT     
          J         ANSRD3          .NO,JUMP    
          L,H2      A1,3,A0           .GET NO. OF WORDS READ
          AN,U      A1,1              .DECREMENT EVEN NO. TO MAKE ODD   
          S,H2      A1,3,A0           .STORE NO, OF WORDS READ    
          SZ,S1     3,A0              .CLEAR READ STATUS    
          J         ANSRD3    
ARDO      L,H2      A1,4,A0           . LOAD BUFFER ADDRESS 
          L         A2,0,A1           . LOAD NUM WORDS IN BLK     
          S,H2      A2,3,A0           . STORE BLKSZ IN WDS READ   
          TNZ       CONVERTWORD       . IS IT TAG OR SELECT 
          J         $+3               . NO RECORD SORT JUMP 
          L         A1,5,A0           . LOAD MASS STOR ADDR 
          S         A1,ADRSAV         . SAVE FOR TAG SORT   
          SZ        A1                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          DI,U      A1,28             . DIVIDE TO GET NR OF SECTORS     
          TZ        A2                . TEST IF PARTIAL SECTOR    
          A,U       A1,1              . YES, INCR NR OF SECTORS BY 1    
          A         A1,5,A0           . UPDATE FASTRAND ADDR
          S         A1,5,A0           .   
          TNE       A1,ENDADD        . IS ADDR EQUAL TO EOF ADDR ?
          J         $+4              .  YES  JUMP     
          TG        A1,ENDADD         .TEST IF LESS THAN ENDADD   
          J         $+2               .YES
          J         ANSRD3            .NO, GO AHEAD   
          L,H2      A1,4,A0           .GET BUFR ADDR  
          L         A1,0,A1           .GET 1ST WD OF BUFR   
          S,H2      A1,3,A0           . STORE BLK CNT AS WORDS MOVE     
          L,H2      A1,4,A0           .   
          S         A1,9,A0           .   
          J         ANSRD2            .   
ANSRD1    L,U       A1,1              .   
          S,S1      A1,3,A0           .   
          J         0,X10             .RETURN   
ANSRD2    TNE       A2,BSIZCK         . CHECK IF SAME 
          J         ANSRD3            .   
          TNZ       VARFLG            .   
          J         ANSRD3            .   
          L,H2      A2,0,A1           .   
          S,H2      A2,3,A0           .   
          SZ        A1                .   
          DI,U      A1,28             .   
          TZ        A2                .   
          A,U       A1,1              .   
          MSI,U     A1,28             .   
          A,H2      A1,4,A0           .   
          S         A1,9,A0           .SET STATUS     
ANSRD3.     
          TNZ,S1    3,A0              . TEST STATUS   
          J         ANSRD4            . JUMP GOOD STATUS    
          L,U       A2,2              . LOAD CUTOFF ON ERR STAUTS 
          TLE,S1    A2,3,A0           . TEST STATUS RANGE   
          J         ANSRD4            . JUMP BAD LET USER CHECK   
          TZ        8,A0              . TEST IF TAPE OR MASS STOR ?     
          J         ANSRD4            . JUMP MASS STORAGE   
          L         A1,STDLBL         . LOAD TYPE OF LABEL  
          TE,U      A1,1              . TEST IF OMIT LABEL  
          J         ANSRD4            . JUMP LABLED STANDARD
          L,H2      A1,6,A0           . LOAD CURRENT REEL # 
          TNZ       A1                . TEST IF 1ST REEL IN 
          L,U       A1,1              . LOAD 1 IF ZERO
          TG,H1     A1,6,A0           . TEST IF MAX STILL GREATER 
          J         ANSRD4            . JUMP LAST REEL
          A,U       A1,1              . BUMP REEL COUNT     
          S,H2      A1,6,A0           . STORE NEW CURRENT REEL COUNT    
          SZ        9,A0              . SET NOT END   
          L         A1,A0             . MOVE A0 TO A1 
          LXI,U     A0,0              . MAKE SURE 0 TSWAP CALL    
          ER        TSWAP$            . GET NEXT REEL 
          L         A0,A1              . RESTORE A0   
          SZ,S1     3,A0              . MAKE SURE STATUS CLEAR    
          J         ANSREAD           . GO READ FIRST BLK NEW TAPE
ANSRD4    TZ,S6     INPBLOKSW         . TEST USER CHECK IN BLKS   
          J         ANSRD5            . NO JUMP 
          LMJ       X9,CKUSRB         . LET USER CHECK BLOCK
          J         ANSREAD           . DELETE BLK RET GET NEXT BLOCK   
          J         $+1               . USER HAS MODIFIED BLOCK   
ANSRD5    L,S1      A1,3,A0           . LOAD STATUS OF READ 
          J         0,X10             . RETURN TO CALLER    
/.    
.    THIS ROUTINE BUILD ASCII HEADERS IF INPUT WAS CARDS    
. ENTER VIA X9    
.  USES  AOTD  FDASC REGISTER A0 THROUGH A4 X11 X9 R1 
MKAHRD    S         X9,MKEXT          . SAVE X9 EXIT  
          L         A1,ASPACE         . LOAD ASCII SPACES   
          L         A2,(1,ENPKT2)     . LOAD DESTINATION    
          L,U       A3,20             . LOAD MAX SPACES IN HEADING
          S         A1,0,*A2          . STORE SPACES  
          JGD       A3,$-1            .  SPACES FILL ENPKT2 
          L         A1,(1,ENPKT1)     . GET SET TO DO HDR1  
          L         A2,(1,AAHDR1)     . LOAD HARD CODE ADDR 
          L,U       R1,20             .   
          BT        A1,0,*A2          . MOVE HARD CODE IN   
          LMJ       X6,FJDATE         . GO GET JULIAN DATE  
          S         A0,YEARNM         . STORE NUM DAYS IN YEAR    
          S         A2,MKEYER         . STORE JULIAN  YEAR  
          S         A3,MKEDAY         . STORE DAYS SO FAR   
          LMJ       X9,AOTD           . CONVERT YEAR TO ASCII     
          S,H2      A3,ENPKT1+10      . STORE CREATE YEAR   
          S,H1      A3,ENPKT1+12      . STORE EXPIRE YEAR   
          L         A2,MKEDAY         . RELENTER TO GET DAYS IN ASCII   
          LMJ       X9,AOTD           . CONVERT DAYS TO ASCII     
          L         A4,ASPACE         . PUT SPACES IN A4    
          LDSL      A3,9              . MOVE SPACES TO Q4   
          S         A3,ENPKT1+11      . STORE ASCII CREATE DAYS   
          L         A0,YEARNM         . LOAD NUMBER DAYS IN YR    
          L         A2,MKEDAY         . LOAD DAYS     
          A,U       A2,60             . ADD SIXTY DAYS
          TLE       A0,A2             . IS IT THIS YEAR     
          J         $+2               . NO GET NEW YEAR     
          J         MKEEDY            . YES OLD YEAR OK     
          AN        A2,A0             . SUBTRACT FOR 1 YEAR 
          S         A2,MKEDAY         . STORE NEW DAYS NXT YR     
          L         A2,MKEYER         . LOAD YEAR     
          A,U       A2,1              . ADD 1   
          LMJ       X9,AOTD           . CONVERT NEXT YEAR   
          S,H1      A3,ENPKT1+12      . STORE NEW YEAR
          L         A2,MKEDAY         . LOAD NEW DAYS 
MKEEDY    LMJ       X9,AOTD           . CONVERT EXP DAYS TO ASCII 
          S,Q1      A3,ENPKT1+13      . STORE LOW ORDER CHARACTER 
          SSL       A3,9              . SHIFT OFF STORED CHARACTER
          S,H2      A3,ENPKT1+12      . STORE TWO HIGH ORDER DAYS 
          L,U       A0,2              . GET SET TO CONVERT QUALIFIER    
          L,U       A1,FACPKT+4       . ORIGIN OF QUALIFIER 
          L,U       A2,ENPKT1+15      . DESTINATION OF QUALIFIER  
          LMJ       X11,FDASC         . CONVERT TO ASCII    
          L         X9,MKEXT         .  RESTORE EXIT  
          J         0,X9              . EXIT TO CALLER
/.    
. ENTER VIA LMJ X6,FJDATE TO FIND JULIAN DATE   
. REGISTERS USED A0 THRU A4   
. EXIT A0 # DAYS IN YEAR A2 YEAR A3 DAYS SO FAR 
FJDATE    ER        TDATE$            . FIND TODAYS DATE    
          S         A0,JDATEA         . STORE RETURN INFO   
          L,S2      A4,JDATEA         . LOAD DAYS THIS MONTH
          L,S1      A0,JDATEA         . LOAD MONTHS THIS YEAR     
          AN,U      A0,1              . GET RID OF THIS MONTH     
          SZ        A2                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          L,S3      A3,JDATEA         . LOAD OFFSET OF 64 IN YEARS
          DI,U      A2,4              . DIVIDE BY 4   
          TZ        A3                .  ZERO MEANS LEAP YEAR     
          J         $+4               . JUMP NOT LEAP YEAR  
          L,H2      A3,JDATEB,A0      . LOAD LEAP DAYS IN MONTHS  
          L,U       A0,365            . LOAD DAYS IN LEAP YEAR    
          J        $+3                . JUMP NEXT INSTRUCTION     
          L,H1      A3,JDATEB,A0      . LOAD DAYS IN MONTHS NOT LEAP YEAR     
          L,U       A0,364            . LOAD DAYS IN YEAR NOT LEAP
          A         A3,A4             . ADD DAYS
          L,S3      A2,JDATEA         . LOAD YEAR OFFSET    
          A,U       A2,64             . ADD MODULO    
          J         0,X6              . RETURN TO CALLER    
/.        COMMON ROUTINES SORT ASCII CFH IO     
. ******************************************************************************    
.     
SKIPSOME  . CALL VIA LMJ X11,SKIPSOME     
. PURPOSE IS TO SKIP RECORDS TO SORT IN THE INPUT FILE
.     
          LA,U      A0,INPKT           . GET THE INPUT PACKET ADDRESS   
          LA        A2,SKIPSAVE        . GET THE NUMBER OF RECORDS PER BLOCK  
          SZ        A1                 .  
          DI        A1,BLKSIZ          . HOW MANY BLOCKS TO SKIP  
          TZ        A2                 . EVEN NUMBER  A1 = BLOCKS TO SKIP     
          J         SKIPERR            . NO ERROR OUT 
          TZ        8,A0               . TAPE INPUT FILE    
          J         FASTINPUT          . NO     
          LA,U      A2,050             .  
          SA,S2     A2,3,A0           . STORE IN A TAPE MOVE FUNCTION   
LOOPMV    ANA,U     A1,1               . DECREMENT COUNT    
          ER        IOW$               .  
          TZ,S1     3,A0               . I/O OK 
          LMJ       X9,IOERR           . NO     
          JNZ       A1,LOOPMV          .  
          LA,U      A2,020             .  
          SA,S2     A2,3,A0           . RESTORE THE READ FUNCTION FOR INPUT   
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
FASTINPUT . 
          LA,H1     A3,4,A0            . GET THE NUMBER OF WORDS PER BLOCK    
          SZ        A2                 .  
          DI,U      A2,28              .  
          TZ        A3                 .  
          AA,U      A2,1               . A2 = THE NUMBER OF SECTORS PER BLOCK 
          MSI       A2,A1              . A1 = THE NUMBER OF SECTORS TO SKIP   
          LA        A1,5,A0            .  
          AA        A2,A1              .  
          SA        A2,5,A0            . SET THE NEW SECTOR ADDRESS IN THE PKT
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
.     
. THE FOLLOWING CODE CHECKS USER EXIT OPTIONS   
.     
CHKOPT.     
          TZ,S1     CHKOPTWD          .   
          ER        EXIT$             .   
          TNZ,S2    CHKOPTWD          .   
          J         0,X6              .   
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,RESTSW         .   
          J         0,X6              .   
.     
. **************************************  
. COMMON ROUTINES FOR USER EXIT INTERFACE 
. ENTER ROUTINES VIA X9 GO TO USER VIA X6 
. SWITCH PACKET FOR EACH EXIT IS FLAG WORD 2 WORD NAME 1 WORD     
. ADDRESS OF EXIT PICKED UP  FROM ENTRY$ TABLE  
. FLAG DIVIDED INTO SIXTHS ONLY S1 S2 S6 USED   
. S1 = 0  NO VMOS TERM EXIT S2 = 0 NO VMOST CONT EXIT 
. S6 = 0  THIS USER EXIT BEING USED 
.     
. ********************************************* 
.     
.  DO USER HEADERS AND TRAILERS   OUPUT   
. ENTER VIA X9,DOUSRH  EXIT 0,X9  CONT WITH SORT
. EXIT 1,X9  PROCESS LABEL RET TO USER    
. EXIT 2,X9  WRITE EOF LABEL EOF MARK CONT PROCESSING 
. EXIT 3,X9  WRITE EOF MARK CONTINUE PROCESSING 
.     
DOUSRH    DS        A0,TEMP1          . SAVE A0 A1    
          TNZ       OLBLCK            . HAS THIS EXIT BEEN TESTED 
          J         JP5A              . YES JUMP
          L         A1,OUTLBS         . LOAD FLAG WORD
          S         A1,CHKOPTWD       . STORE FOR CHECK     
          SZ        OLBLCK            . SET AS TESTED 
          LMJ       X6,CHKOPT         . CHECK OPTIONS 
          TNZ       RESTSW            . IS IT VMOS CONT PARAM     
          J         $+4               . NO CONTINUE   
          L,U       A1,1              . SET FLAG
          S,S6      A1,OUTLBS         . SET NO EXIT   
          SZ        RESTSW            . CLEAR FOR USE OTHER EXITS 
JP5A      L         A1,OUTLBS+3       . LOAD ADDR OF EXIT   
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           . GO TO USER    
          S         A0,TSTA0X         . SAVE RESULTS  
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          L         A4,TSTA0X         . LOAD RESULTS  
          TNE,U     A4,'F'            . IS IT END OF FILE   
          J         2,X9              . YES RET TO DO EOF   
          TNE,U     A4,1              . IS IT 1 RET FROM USER     
          J         0,X9              . YES RET TO CONT PROCESSING
          TE,U      A4,2              .  IS  IT 2 RET 
          J         $+3               . NO
          SZ        MOREUWL           . CLEAR TO FORCE RETURN     
          J         1,X9              . RETURN 2 RET  
          TE,U      A4,'V'            . IS IT EOV V RET     
          J         3,X9              . NO TREAT AS F RET   
          J         2,X9              . YES PREP TO DO EOR  
.     
. *******************************************   
. DO USER CHECK OUTPUT BLOCK  
. ENTER  VIA  LMJ   X9,DOUSRB EXIT 0,X9 DELETE BLOCK  
. EXIT 1,X9 USER WROTE BLK  EXIT 2,X9 USER MODIFIED BLK     
. EXIT 3,X9  NO CHANGE TO BLOCK     
.     
DOUSRB    DS        A1,TEMP1          . SAVE A0 A1    
          TNZ       OBLKCK            . TEST IF TESTED
          J         JP3A              . YES JUMP
          L         A1,OUTBKS         . LOAD FLAG WORD
          S         A1,CHKOPTWD       . STORE FOR CHECK     
          SZ        OBLKCK            . SET TO TESTED 
          LMJ       X6,CHKOPT         . CHECK OPTIONS 
          TNZ       RESTSW            . TEST IF VMOS CONT   
          J         $+4               . NO JUMP 
          L,U       A1,1              . LOAD FOR FLAG 
          S,S6      A1,OUTBKS         . SET NO USER EXIT    
          SZ        RESTSW            . CLEAR  FOR  USER OTHER EXITS    
JP3A      L,H2      A0,OUTPKT+4       . LOAD BUFFER ADDR    
          L         A1,OUTBKS+3       . LOAD EXIT ADDRESS   
          L,H1      A2,OUTPKT+4       . LOAD BLCOK SIZE     
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           . GO TO USER    
          DS        A0,TSTA0X         . STORE A0 A1   
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          TZ        TSTA0X            . TEST IF DELETED BLOCK     
          J         $+3               . NO DO OTHER CHECKS  
          AN,U      A8,1              . SUB 1 FROM BLK COUNT
          J         0,X9              . RET DELETE BLOCK    
          TZ,H1     TSTA1X            . TEST IF USER WROTE BLCOK  
          J         1,X9              . YES RETURN    
          TNZ,H1    TSTA0X            . TEST IF BLK SIZE CHANGE   
          J         3,X9              . NO NO CHANGE RETURN 
          L,H1      A1,TSTA0X         . LOAD NEW END ADDR   
          L,H2      A0,TSTA0X         . LOAD BEGIN OF BLK   
          AN        A1,A0             . FIND DIFFERENCE     
          A,U       A1,1              . BUMP 1  
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT         . LOAD OUTPKT ADDR    
          S,H1      A1,4,A0           . STORE NEW BLOCK SIZE
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          J         2,X9              . RETURN WITHMODIFIED BLOCK 
.     
. *******************************************   
.     
. DO  USER RECORD OUTPUT CHECK
. ENTER VIA LMJ X9,DOUSRR EXIT 0,X9 DELETE RECORD     
. EXIT 1,X9 USER INSER RECS EXIT 2,X9 NO CHANGE 
.     
DOUSRR    DS        A0,TEMP1          . SAVE A0 A1    
          TNZ       ORECCK            . HAS THIS EXIT BEEN TESTED 
          J         JP1               . YES JUMP
          L         A1,OUTRECSW       . LOAD FLAG WORD
          S         A1,CHKOPTWD       . STORE IN TEMP WORD  
          SZ        ORECCK            . SET AS TESTED 
          LMJ       X6,CHKOPT         . GO TEST OPTIONS     
          TNZ       RESTSW            . WAS VMOS CONT PARAM SET   
          J         $+4               . NO JUMP 
          L,U       A1,1              . LOAD FLAG     
          S,S6      A1,OUTRECSW       . SET NO EXIT   
          SZ        RESTSW            . CLEAR FOR USER OTHER EXITS
JP1       S         A0,HDA0XO         . SAVE A0 POSS INSERT LATER 
          L         A1,OUTRECSW+3     . LOAD ADDRESS OF EXIT
          TZ        HDOREC            . TEST IF INSERTING   
          L         A0,HDOREC         . YES USE OLD REC FROM SORT 
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           . GO TO USER    
          DS        A0,TSTA0X         . SAVE RESULTS A0 A1  
          TZ        A0                . IS THIS DELETE RECORD     
          J         $+3               . NO JUMP 
          SZ        HDOREC            . MAKE SURE CLEAR     
          J         0,X9              . DELETE RETURN 
          TNZ       HDOREC            . IS INSERTION UNDER WAY    
          J         $+9               . NO JUMP 
          TNZ,H1    TSTA1X            . IS THIS END INSERTION     
          J         $+4               . NO JUMP 
          L         A0,HDOREC         . RESTORE A0 REC BEF INSERT 
          SZ        HDOREC            . CLEAR INSERT FLAG   
          J         2,X9              . CONTINUE NORMAL PROCESSING
          L         A0,A1             . LOAD NEW REC ADDR   
          LXI,H1    A0,TSTA0X         . LOAD REC SIZE 
          J         1,X9              . CONTINUE INSERTING  
          L,H2      A2,TSTA1X         . LOAD H2 A1 RETURN   
          TNE,U     A2,OUTRECSW+3     . HAS IT BEEN MODIFIED
          J         2,X9              . NO CONTINUE PROCESSING    
          TNZ       A2                . WAS A1 SET 0  
          J         2,X9              . YES CONTINUE PROCSSING    
          L         A2,HDA0XO         . LOAD A0 SAVED 
          S         A2,HDOREC         . SET IN INSERT SWTCH 
          L         A0,TSTA1X         . LOAD NEW REC ADDR   
          LXI,H1    A0,TSTA0X         . LOAD SIZE IF VAR    
          J         1,X9              .  JUMP  INSERT RECORDS     
.     
.     
. ********************************  
. CHECK USER RECORD INPUT     
. ENTER LMJ X9,CKUSRR   EXIT 0,X9  DELETE RECORD
. EXIT  1,X9   INSERTING EXIT 2,X9  NO CHANGE   
.     
CKUSRR    DS        A0,TEMP1          . SAVE A0 A1    
          TNZ       IRECCK            . HAS EXIT BEEN TESTED
          J         JPACI             . YES JUMP
          L         A1,INRCSW         . LOAD EXIT FLAG WORD 
          S         A1,CHKOPTWD       . STORE IN CHECK WORD 
          SZ        IRECCK            . SET EXIT TESTED     
          LMJ       X6,CHKOPT         . CHECK OPTIONS 
          TNZ       RESTSW            . WAS VMOS CONT SET   
          J         $+4               . NO JUMP 
          L,U       A1,1              . LOAD FLAG     
          S,S6      A1,INRCSW         . SET NO EXIT   
          SZ        RESTSW            . CLEAR FOR OTHER EXIT USE  
JPACI     S         A0,HDA0X          . SAVE A0 
          L         A1,INRCSW+3       . LOAD EXIT ADDRESS   
          TZ        HDINRC            . TEST IF INSERTING RECORDS 
          L         A0,HDINRC         . YES LOAD OLD REC INFO     
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           . GO TO USER    
          DS        A0,TSTA0X         . STORE RESULTS 
          TZ        A0                . IS THIS DELETE REC  
          J         $+3               . NO JUMP 
          SZ        HDINRC            . MAKE SURE INSERT FLAG CLEAR     
          J         0,X9              . RETURN TO USER
          TNZ       HDINRC            . IS INSERTION UNDER WAY    
          J         $+9               . NO  GO TO OTHER TEST
          TNZ,H1    TSTA1X            . IS THIS END OF INSERTION  
          J         $+4               . NO JUMP TO CONTINUE INSERTING   
          L         A0,HDINRC         . LOAD INPUT REC SIZE AND ADDR    
          SZ        HDINRC            . CLEAR INSERTION FLAG
          J         2,X9              . CONTINUE NORMAL PROCESSING
          L         A0,A1             . LOAD NEW REC ADDR   
          LXI,H1    A0,TSTA0X         . LOAD SIZE     
          J         1,X9              . CONTINUE INSERTING  
          L,H2      A2,TSTA1X         . LOAD H2 FROM A1 RETURN    
          TNE,U     A2,INRCSW+3       . TEST IF MODIFIED    
          J         2,X9              . NO  DO NORMAL PROCESSING  
          TNZ       A2                . WAS A1 SET TO 0     
          J         2,X9              . YES DO NORMAL PROCESSING  
          L         A2,HDA0X          . LOAD SAVED A0 INFO  
          S         A2,HDINRC         . SET INSERT FLAG     
          L         A0,TSTA1X         . LOAD NEW REC ADDR   
          LXI       A0,TSTA0X         . LOAD REC SIZE IF VAR
          J         1,X9              . START INSERTING RECORDS   
.     
. ******************************    
. CHECK USER HEADERS AND TRAILERS   
. ENTER VIA  LMJ X9,CKUSRH  EXIT 0,X9  CONTINUE PROCESSING  
. EXIT 1,X9 READ ANOTHER LABEL BLOCK  2 RETURN  
. EXIT 2,X9   IT WAS A END OF VOLUME LABEL
. EXIT 3,X9    IT WAS END OF FILE LABEL   
.     
CKUSRH    DS        A0,TEMP1          . SAVE A0 A1    
          TNZ       ILBLCK            . HAS THIS EXIT BEEN TESTED 
          J         JP7               . YES JUMP
          L         A1,INLBSW         . LOAD EXIT FLAG WORD 
          S         A1,CHKOPTWD       . STORE IN CHECK WORD 
          SZ        ILBLCK            . SET AS CHECKED
          LMJ       X6,CHKOPT         . CHECK OPTIONS 
          TNZ       RESTSW            . WAS VMOS CONT PARMA PRESENT     
          J         $+4               . NO  JUMP
          L,U       A1,1              . LOAD FLAG     
          S,S6      A1,INLBSW         . SET NO EXIT   
          SZ        RESTSW            . CLEAR FOR USER OTHER EXITS
JP7       L         A1,INLBSW+3       . LOAD EXIT ADDRESS   
          L,U       A0,INPKT          . LOAD ADDRESS  
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           . GO TO USER    
          S         A0,A2             . SAVE RESUTS IN A2   
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0A1  
          TNE,U     A2,'F'            . TEST IF END OF FILE 
          J         3,X9              . YES RETURN    
          TNE,U     A2,1              . TEST IF CONTINUE PROCESSING     
          J         0,X9              . YES CONTINUE PROCESSING   
          TNE,U     A2,2              . TEST IF 2 RET 
          J         1,X9              . RET IF 2 RET BY USER
          TE,U      A2,'V'            . TEST END OF VOLUME  
          J         3,X9              . NO MATCH TREAT AS EOF     
          J         2,X9              . RETURN AS V END OF VOLUME 
.     
. ************************************    
. CHECK USER INPUT BLOCK CODE 
. ENTER VIA  LMJ  X9,CKUSRB   
. EXIT 0,X9 DELETE BLOCK  1,X9 USER CHANGED BLOCK     
. EXIT 2,X9  NO CHANGE TO BLOCK     
.     
CKUSRB    DS        A0,TEMP1          . SAVE A0 A1    
          TNZ       IBLKCK            .  HAS THIS EXIT BEEN TESTED
          J         JP8               .   YES JUMP DO PROCESSING  
          L         A1,INPBLOKSW      .  NO LOAD SWITCH FLAGS     
          S         A1,CHKOPTWD       .  STORE IN CHECK WORD
          SZ        IBLKCK            .   CLEAR FLAG AS TESTED    
          LMJ       X6,CHKOPT         .  GO CHECK VMOS OPTIONS    
          TNZ       RESTSW            .   TEST IF VMOS CONT OPTION
          J         $+4               .    NO CONTINUE
          L,U       A1,1              .  YES LOAD FLAG
          S,S6      A1,INPBLOKSW      .    SET NO  USER EXIT
          SZ        RESTSW            .  CLEAR FOR USE OTHER EXITS
JP8       L         A1,INPBLOKSW+3    . LOAD EXIT ADDRESS   
          L,H2      A0,INPKT+4        .   LOAD BLOCK ADDR   
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           .   GO TO USER  
          DS        A0,TSTA0X         . STORE FOR TESTS     
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          TNZ       TSTA0X            . TEST IF DELETED BLOCK     
          J         0,X9              . YES RETURN TO USER  
          TNZ,H1    TSTA0X            . TEST IF CHANGED BLOCK     
          J         2,X9              . NO CHANGE IN BLOCK  
          L,H1      A0,TSTA0X         . LOAD NEW END ADDR   
          L,H2      A1,TSTA0X         . LOAD ORIGINAL BEGIN ADDR  
          AN        A0,A1             . FIND LENGTH   
          A,U       A0,1              . ADD ONE 
          L,U       A1,INPKT          . LOAD IOPKT ADDR     
          S,H1      A0,4,A1           . STORE IN IO PACKET  
          DL        A0,TEMP1          . RESTORE A0 A1 
          J         2,X9              . RETURN USER CHANGED BLOCK 
/.    
.     
. ***********************************************************************     
IOERR.
.     
. PRINT ERROR MESSAGE WITH I/O STATUS     
.      (A0 = I/O PACKET ADDRESS)    
          . CALL VIA LMJ X9,IOERR , NO RETURN   
          DS        A1,DSAVE            . SAVE RETISTERS A1 AND A2
          DL        A1,0,A0             . GET FILE NAME     
          DS        A1,IOEM+4           . TO MSG
          L,S1      A2,3,A0             . GET I/O STATUS    
          LSSL      A2,30               . POSITION STATUS   
          SZ        A1  
          LDSL      A1,3                . CONVERT TO FD     
          LSSL      A1,3                . 
          LDSL      A1,3                . 
          A,U       A1,'00'             . 
          S         A1,TEMP2          .   
          L         A3,IOEM+1         .   
          L         R2,(0,07777)      .   
          MLU       A3,TEMP2          .   
          S         A4,IOEM+1         .   
          DSL       A0,36               . SAVE A0     
          L         A0,IOEP             . 
          ER        PRINT$              . 
          LDSL      A0,36               . RESTORE A0  
          DL        A1,DSAVE            . RESTORE A1 AND A2 
          ER        ERR$                . 
.     
. **********************************************************************
.     
. PRINT ERROR MESSAGE SPECIFIED IN A0 AND CARD IMAGE  
.     
ERROR     ER        PRINT$              . 
          L         A0,CRCP             . ILLEGAL CARD IMAGE
ERROR1    ER        PRINT$              . 
          TZ        DMDFLG            . IS IT DEMAND MODE?  
          J         NXTPARAM-1        . YES  USER TRY AGAIN 
          ER        ERR$                . 
.     
. **********************************************************************
OTD.  
.     
. CONVERT OCTAL NUMBER IN A2 TO DECIMAL IN A3   
.     
          . CALL VIA LMJ X9,OTD     
          L         A3,('      ')      .  
          L         A0,(-6,30)         . SET FOR SIX CHARACTERS   
          SZ        A1                 .  
OTD1      DI,U      A1,10              . DIVIDE BY 10 
          A,U       A2,'0'             . CONVERT REMAINDER TO FD  
          DSL       A2,6               . SAVE   
          DSL       A1,36              .  
          JZ        A2,$+3              . TEST IF ALL OCTAL NRS PROCESSED     
          JMGI      A0,OTD1             . NO, GO PROCESS NEXT OCTAL NR  
          J         0,X9               . RETURN 
          SSC       A3,,A0             . NO, SHIFT FOR BLANKS     
          J         0,X9               . RETURN 
/.    
.     
. ******************************************************************************    
OPREQ.
.     
. ON ERROR RETURN FROM CSF$, REQUEST OPERATOR TO SPECIFY RETRY OR ERROR 
.     
          . CALL VIA LMJ X10,OPREQ  
          S         A3,CFSERPR2         . 
          L,H1      R1,CFSERPR2         . 
          L,H2      A0,CFSERPR2         . 
          LXI,U     A0,1                . 
          LX        X9,(1,CFSERM1A)    .  
          BT        X9,0,*A0           . TRANSFER ASSIGN IMAGE TO CONSOL MESSAGE    
          L,U       A0,CFSERRP1         . PRINT ASSIGN IMAGE
          ER        COM$                . 
OPREQ1    L,U       A0,CFSERRP2         . PRINT RETRY(R) OR ERROR (E)   
          ER        COM$                . 
          L,S1      A0,CFSERPR2         . GET OPERATOR ANSWER     
          TNE,U     A0,'R'              . RETRY?
          J         0,X10              . YES , RETURN 
          TNE,U     A0,'E'              . ERROR?
          ER        ERR$                . YES   
          J         OPREQ1              . ILLEGAL OPERATOR RESPONSE     
CONTADD   . 
          +         0                  .  
          +         0                  .  
          DS        A0,RSAVE          . SAVE A0 A1    
          DS        A2,RSAVE+2        . SAVE A2 A3    
          S         X11,RSAVE+4       . SAVE X11
          TNZ       QLPFLG             .  
          J         ENDCONT            .  
          LA,S3     A0,CONTADD         . GET THE CONTIHGENCY TYPE 
          TE,U      A0,06              . RESTART
          J          CORRAN              .
          ER        ERR$               .  
          ER        CEND$              . END CONTINGENCY    
          J         RETADDR            .  
CORRAN.     
          ER        CEND$                . END CONTINGENCY  
          S         A0,TEMP0             . SAVE REGISTER    
          L         A0,(WFORM1 0,2,5,MSG9) . LOAD PACKET    
          ER        PRINT$               .
          S         A1,TEMP3             . SAVE REGISTER    
          L,S3      A1,CONTADD           . LOAD CONTINGENCY ID    
          AN,U      A1,1                 .
          L         A0,MSGS,A1           . LOAD ADDRESS OF CONTINGENCY ID     
          S         A0,MSGX              . STORE ADDRESS    
          L         A0,(WFORM1 0,1,1,MSGX) . LOAD PACKET    
          ER        PRINT$               .
          A,U       A1,1                 .
          TNE,U     A1,010               .
          J         ALT1                 .
          TE,U      A1,012               .
          J         ERRFIN               .
          L,S1      A1,CONTADD         .  
          AN,U      A1,1                 .
          L         A0,ERRS+2,A1         .
          S         A0,MSGX              .
          L         A0,(WFORM1 0,1,1,MSGX) . LOAD PACKET    
          ER        PRINT$               .
          J         ERRFIN               . END ERROR HANDLING     
WFORM1    FORM      6,6,6,18            . 
ALT1      L,S1      A1,CONTADD           .
          AN,U      A1,1                 .
          L         A0,ERRS,A1           .
          S         A0,MSGX              .
          L         A0,(WFORM1 0,1,1,MSGX) .    
         ER         PRINT$             .  
ERRFIN.     
          S         A3,TEMP3            . SAVE REGISTER     
          S       X11,TEMPA1          . SAVE REGISTER 
          S         R1,TEMPA0           . SAVE REGISTER     
          E$DIT     PACKT               . BEGIN ADDRESS DUMP
          E$OCTF    12,CONTADD          . 
          E$CHAR    ' '                 . 
          E$OCTF    12,CONTADD+1        . 
          E$DITX                        . 
          L         A0,(WFORM1 0,1,5,MSGA) . LOAD PACKET    
          ER        PRINT$              . 
          L         A0,TEMP0            . RESTORE REGISTER  
          L         A3,TEMP3            . RESTORE REGISTER  
          L         X11,TEMPA1          . RESTORE REGISTER  
          L         R1,TEMPA0           . RESTORE REGISTER  
          TZ        DMDFLG              . DEMAND TERMINAL   
          J         QUEST               . YES, IT IS A DEMAND TERMINAL  
          L         A0,(01,0)           . LOAD PACKET 
          ER        IALL$               . REGISTER CONTINGENCY    
          ER        ERR$                . ERROR EXIT  
QUEST.
          L         A0,(01,0)           . LOAD CONTINGENCY REGISTRATION PACKET
          ER        IALL$               . REGISTER CONTINGENCY    
          ER        ERR$                . ERROR EXIT  
ENDCONT   . 
          ER        CEND$              . END CONTINGENCY    
          LA,U      A0,ERRIOP          .  
          ER        IOW$               . WRITE BAD STATUS   
          TNZ,S1    3,A0               . GOOD I/O STATUS    
          ER        EXIT$              . YES , EXIT   
          LA        A0,(01,CONTADD)    . END CONTINGENCY    
          ER        IALL$              .  
          ER        EXIT$              . EXIT RATHER THAN ERROR   
/.    
. ***********************************************************************     
WRTEOF.     
.     
. WRITE END OF FILE     
.     
          . CALL VIA LMJ X11,WRTEOF 
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . FASTRAND OUTPUT FILE?     
          TNZ       TAPEDRUM            . TEST IF TAPE OR DRUM OUTPUT FILE    
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
          L,U       A0,OUTPKT           . 
          L,U       A1,011            . END FILE FUNCTION   
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          ER        IOW$              . WRITE END OF FILE   
          L,S1      A1,3,A0             . GET STATUS  
          TNZ       A1                  . 
          J         WEOF1               . 
          TE,U      A1,2                . IGNORE IF EOF     
          LMJ       X9,IOERR            . BAD   
          SZ,S1     3,A0              . RESET STATUS CLEARS IO PKT
WEOF1     L,U       A1,010              . REPLACE WRITE FUNCTION  
          S,S2      A1,3,A0           .   
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
.     
. ***********************************************************************     
CPTAOF.     
.     
. CLOSE PARAMETER TABLE AND ASSIGN OUTPUT FILE IF NONE SPECIFIED  
.     
          . CALL VIA  LMJ X11,CPTAOF
          L         A1,(  PA1 071,PARAM)  
          L         A2,PARPNT 
          S         A1,0,A2           . END OF PARAM TABLE  
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
OUTASGT   . 
          TNZ       POPFLG            . IS PRINT ONLY FLAG SET    
          J         0,X11             . RETURN IMMEDIATELY TO USER
          DL        A1,OUTPKT 
          DTE       A1,BLANKS           . OUTPUT FILE ASSIGNED?   
          J         CPTAOF1             . YES   
CPTAOF0   L         A0,(CSFL,CSFM)      . ASSIGN ONE  
          ER        CSF$
          JN        A0,CPTERR           . TEST IF GOOD ASSIGN     
          L,U       A1,1              . SET NON ZERO  
          S         A1,OUTCHANGESW    . SET NON ZERO  
          S         A1,OUTCLOSESW     . SET NON ZERO  
          L         A0,OTPMEP         . LOAD  PRINT MESSAGE 
          ER        PRINT$            .   
          DL        A1,('OUTTAPE     ')   
          DS        A1,OUTPKT 
.     
. DETERMINE IF DRUM OR TAPE OUTPUT (FOR END OF FILES) 
. IF LABELED OUTPUT     
CPTAOF1   DL        A1,OUTPKT           . OUTPUT FILE NAME  
          DS        A1,FACPKT           . 
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . 
          ER        FACIL$              . GET OUTPUT FILE FACILITY INFO 
          L,S1      A1,FACPKT+6         . EQUIPMENT TYPE    
          TLE,U     A1,030              . TEST IF FASTRAND FORMAT 
CPTAOF2   S         A1,TAPEDRUM         . SET INDICATOR FOR TAPE  
          J         0,X11              . RETURN 
CPTERR    L         A3,(CSFL,CSFM)      . 
          LMJ       X10,OPREQ          . ASK OPERATOR TO RETRY    
          J         CPTAOF0             . RETRY RETURN
. CHECK ILLEGAL USE FORM01    
CKFRM1    TZ        STDLBL            . CHECK IF FORM 01    
          J         0,X11             . NOT FORM01 JUMP     
          TNZ       FASTFILEIN        . CHECK INPUT FILE    
          J         $+6               .  NOT MASS GO CHECK OUTPUT 
          L         A0,INPKT          . CHECK INPUT FOR CARDS     
          TNE       A0,('CARDS ')     . IS IT CARDS   
          J         $+3               . YES NO WORRY  
          TZ        INLBSW            .  CHECK USER INPUT SWITCH  
          J         FRME              . ERROR NO SWITCH MASS AND FORM01 
          TNZ       FASTFILEOUT       . IS OUTPUT MASS STOR ?     
          J         $+6               . NO JUMP OK    
          L         A0,OUTPKT         . LOAD  OUTPUT PACKET 
          TNE       A0,('CARDS ')     . CARDS TEST    
          J         $+3               . JUMP OK 
          TZ        OUTLBS            . TEST OUTPUT USER SWITCH   
          J         FRME              . ERROR  IF NO SWITCH 
          J         0,X11             . RETURN CHECK OK     
FRME      L         A0,FRMEP          . LOAD ERR MSG  
          J         ERROR1            . MSG AND ERR OUT     
.     
/.    
.  ALL CODE FROM I BANK FROM HERE IS
.    OPTIONS OR PARAMETER DEFINITION
INITCD    LA        A0,PRTPKT          .  
          . 
          TE,U      A4,4                . DEMAND TERMINAL   
          J         NODMD               . NOT A DEMAND TERMINAL   
          L,U       A4,1                . 
          S         A4,DMDFLG           . YES, SET DEMAND FLAG    
          L,U       A4,4                . RESET REGISTER    
NODMD.
          LA        A0,(0177701,CONTADD) .
          ER        IALL$              .  
          LA       A0,(CEOF,CRDBUF)    .  
          ER        READ$              . DISPOSE OF PROCESSOR CALL CARD 
          LMJ       X11,IDONLY$       .   
          L         A0,PRTPKT         . GO TO PRINT ID LINE 
          ER        PRINT$            .  LEVEL AND ID 
          LA,U      A0,CRDBUF          . GET THE INPUT BUFFER ADDRESS   
          LA        A1,(0,01000)       . SET MASK FOR Q-OPTION    
          AND       A1,0,A0            . IS THERE A Q OPTION
          JZ        A2,NOQOPT          . NO , NOQOPT  
          SZ        QLPFLG             . CLEAR QLP ENTRANCE 
NOQOPT    . 
          LA        A1,(0,04)          . MASK THE X OPTION  
          AND       A1,0,A0            .  
          JZ        A2,YOPT            . AN X OPTION  
          SZ        XOPTFL             . YES INDICATE IT    
YOPT .
          L         A1,(0,02)         . LOAD FOR MASKING  OPTION  
          AND       A1,0,A0           . MASK    
          JZ        A2,NOXOPT         .   
          SZ        YOPTFL            .   
NOXOPT    . 
          L         A1,(0,020000)     . LOAD M OPTION 
          AND       A1,0,A0           . MASK FOR M OPTION   
          TNZ       A2                . TES FOR M OPTION    
          J         $+3               . NO JUMP TO TEST NEXT
          SZ        MIXFLG            . SET MIX MODE FLG TO ZERO  
          J         MIXASC            . JUMP TO MIXED ASCIII
          L         A1,(0200,0)         . MASK A OPTION     
          AND       A1,0,A0             . 
          JNZ       A2,AOPT             . A OPTION?   
          J         NOAOPT              . NO A OPTION 
AOPT      L,U       A1,4                . 
          S         A1,TYPECONST1       . STORE CHARACTERS/WORD   
          L,U       A1,9              .   
          S         A1,TYPECONST2     .   
MIXASC    SZ        A1                . SET FILE TYPE FOR ASCII FILES   
          S         A1,FILTYP           . SET FILTYPE FOR ASCII FILES   
          L,U       A1,3              .   
          S         A1,TGCNT            . 
          S         A0,TEST1          .   
          L         A0,(0400001)      .LOAD FOR Q MODE
          ER        PSR$              . CHANGE TO QUARTER WORD MODE     
          L         A0,TEST1          .RESTORE A0     
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,ANSFLG         .   
NOAOPT.     
          L         A1,(0,0200)         . MASK S OPTION     
          AND       A1,0,A0             . 
          JNZ       A2,SOPT             . S OPTION?   
          J         NOSOPT              . NO S OPTION 
SOPT      L,U       A1,2              .   
          S         A1,FILTYP           . SET FILTYPE FOR SDF FILES     
NOSOPT.     
          L         A1,(0,040000)     .   
          AND       A1,0,A0           .   
          JZ        A2,NOLOPT 
          L,U       A1,1
          S         A1,ZOP            .   
          SZ        POZFLG            . SET L OR P OPTION FLAG    
NOLOPT.     
          L         A1,(0,0100000)     .  LOAD MASK FOR K OPTION  
          AND       A1,0,A0           . CHECK FOR P OPTION  
          JZ        A2,NOPOPT         . JUMP NOT P OPTION   
          SZ        POPFLG            . SET P OPTION TO ZERO
          SZ        POZFLG            . SET L OR P OPTION FLAG    
NOPOPT.     
          SZ        A11                 . CLEAR TOTAL RECORDS OUTPUT CTR
          L         A10,('  SORT')      . SORT INDICATOR    
          L,U       A0,LASTD$         . LOAD LAST D BANK ADDR     
          A,U       A0,1              . BUMP TO NXT AVAIL ADDR    
          S         A0,TCORE$         . SAVE ADDRESS  
          S         A0,STBUF          . SAVE FOR TAG OR REL SORT  
          S,H2      A0,INPKT+4        . STORE IN INPUT PKT  
          S,H2      A0,OUTPKT+4       . STORE IN OUPUT PKT  
          S,H2      A0,MERGEPKT+4     .   
          S,H2      A0,OUTPKT1+4      . STORE IN AUX PKT    
          LMJ       X11,CARDIN        . READ PARAMETER CARD 
NXTPARAM  LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET ITEM
          J         $-2                 . END OF CARD 
          DL        A1,GITV           . GET ITEM
          L,U       R1,NUMCRD           . NUMBER OF POSSIBLE ITEMS
          L         A0,(1,0)  
          SE        A1,CARDS,*A0      . SEARCH FOR VALID ENTRY    
          J         ILLEGAL           . BAD ENTRY     
          J         *CRDJMP-1,A0      . GO HANDLE ITEM
ILLEGAL   L         A0,ILLP             . ILLEGAL INPUT PARAMETER 
          J         ERROR               . 
$(0). 
CARDS.                                . VALID ITEMS   
          'RSZ   '                    .   
          'RSZW  '  . REC IN WORDS IND    
          'KEY   '                    .   
          'KEYW  '  . KEY IN WODS INDICATOR     
          'BLOCK '                    .   
          'BLOCKO'  . OUTPUT BLOCKING FACTOR    
          'BLKCHI'  . INPUTBLK  IN CHARACTERS   
          'BLKCHO'  . OUTPUT BLK IN CHARACTERS  
          'FILEIN'                    .   
          'FILEOU'                    .   
          'TAPES '                      . 
          'CORE  '                    .   
          'LABEL '
          'MERGE'                       . 
          'SEQUEN'                     .  
          'CHECK '                      . 
          'VOLUME'
          'BIAS'                        . 
          'RECORD'                      . 
          'LIMDRM'  .   
          'LIMFST'  .   
          'SKIP  '                     .  
          'NUMREC'                     .  
          'CKPTRS'                     .  
          'FILESI'                    .   
          'OWN'                       .   
          'LINKSZ'                     .  
          'MOVE  '  .   
          'CONVER'                      . 
          'END'                         . 
NUMCRD    EQU       $-CARDS           .   
$(1). 
CRDJMP.     
          +         RSZ               .   
          +         RSZW              . ADDR OF REC IN WDS CODE   
          +         KEY               .   
          +         KEYW              . ADDR OF KEY IN WDS CODE

. ***********************************************************************     
CARDIN.     
.     
. READ PARAMETER CARD   
.     
          L         A0,BLANKS           . 
          L,U       A1,13     
          S         A0,CRDBUF,A1      . BLANK INPUT AREA    
          JGD       A1,$-1    
          L         A0,(CEOF,CRDBUF)
          ER        READ$             . READ PARAMETER CARD 
          L         A0,( PF 1,14,CRDBUF ) 
          ER        PRINT$            . PRINT PARAMETER CARD
          L,U       A0,CRDBUF 
          S,H2      A0,INIMGE         . INITIALIZE GIT ROUTINE    
          SZ,H2     CHRPOS    
          J         0,X11     
/.    
.     
. **********************************************************************
GIT.  
.     
.     GETS NEXT SYNTACTICAL ITEM FROM INPUT CARD PUTTING IT IN GITV     
.         ENTRY     LMJ   X11,GIT   
.                   END OF STATEMENT RETURN     
.                   STANDARD RETURN 
.     
          L         X7,CHRPOS         . GET CURRENT CHARACTER     
          L         X8,INIMGE         . GET CURRENT WORD    
          DL        A0,BLANKS           . 
          DS        A0,GITV             . 
          L         A1,(1,0)          . INDEX INTO GITV FOR STORE 
GIT0      EX        GX,*X7              . GET NEXT CHARACTER
. IF PROCESSING FOR SEQUENCE PARAMETER, RETURN WITH 1 CHARACTER   
          TNZ       INDSEQ              . TEST IF SEQUENCE PARAMETER    
          J         GIT1               . NO, CONTINUE 
          S,S1      A2,GITV             . STORE 1 CHARACTER FOR SEQUENC(
          J         GITG+1              . YES, ONLY RETURN 1 CHARACTER  
GIT1      TE,U      A2,' '              . BLANK?
          J         GITCONT             . 
          L         A0,RETBLANK         . HAS IMAGE BEEN STARTED? 
          TNZ       A0                .   
          J         GIT0              .   
          TZ        INDSEQ            .   
          SZ        RETBLANK          .   
          J         GITG                . RETURN WITH BLANK AS NEXT CHARACTER 
GITCONT   TNE,U     A2,','              . COMMA?
          J         GITG              . YES, THEN HAVE NUL(BLANK) FIELD 
          TNE,U     A2,'['            . IS IT EBCDIC LEFT PARENS  
          L,U       A2,'('            . YES CHANGE    
          TNE,U     A2,']'            . IS IT EBCDIC RIGHT PARENS 
          L,U       A2,')'            . YES CHANGE    
          TNE,U     A2,'('              . TEST FOR LEFT PAREN     
          J         GITG                . 
          TNE,U     A2,')'              . TEST FOR RIGHT PAREN    
          J         GITG                . 
          TE,U      A2,'.'            . IS IT PERIOD  
. GET AND STORE DATA UNTIL BLANK, EQUAL, COMMA OR END OF CARD IS FOUND  
GITLP.
          EX        STRX,*A1          . STORE CHAR IN ITEM  
          EX        GX,*X7            . GET NEXT CHAR 
          TNE,U     A2,'.'            . IS IT PERIOD ?
          J         $-2               . YES IGNORE    
          TNE,U     A2,'['            . IS IT EBCDIC LEFT PARENS  
          L,U       A2,'('            . YES CHANGE    
          TNE,U     A2,']'            . IS IT EBCDIC RIGHT PARENS 
          L,U       A2,')'            . YES CHANGE    
          TNE,U     A2,076            . IS IT EBCDIC EQUAL SIGN   
          L,U       A2,'='            . YES CHANGE    
          TE,U      A2,'='            . END OF PARAMETER    
          TNE,U     A2,','            .  OR END OF FIELD    
          J         GITG              . YES     
          TNE,U     A2,' '              . TEST FOR END OF LIST    
          J         GITG                . 
          TNE,U     A2,'('              . TEST FOR LEFT PAREN     
          J         GITG                . 
          TE,U      A2,';'            . CONTINUATION CHARACTER?   
          J         $+3               .   
          TNZ       FORMSW            .   
          J         CONTCARD          .   
          TE,U      A2,')'              . TEST FOR RIGHT PAREN    
          J         GITLP             . NO
GITG      S         A2,NGX            . SAVE NEXT CHAR
          S,H2      X7,CHRPOS         . SAVE POINTERS 
          S,H2      X8,INIMGE         .   
          J         1,X11             . NORMAL RETURN 
CONTCARD  S         X11,TEMP           . STORE X11    
          LMJ       X11,CARDIN        . READ NEXT CARD
          L         X11,TEMP           . RESTORE X11  
          J         1,X11             . NORMAL RETURN 
. GET NEXT CHARACTER UNTIL END OF CARD IS DETECTED    
GX.   
          L,S1      A2,0,X8             . 
          L,S2      A2,0,X8           .   
          L,S3      A2,0,X8           .   
          L,S4      A2,0,X8           .   
          L,S5      A2,0,X8           .   
          L,S6      A2,0,*X8          .   
          LMJ       X6,$+1            .   
          L,U       A5,0,X8           .   
          TG,U      A5,CRDBUF+13      . OUT OF BUFFER 
          J         EOS                 . YES, END OF CARD  
          L         X7,(1,0)          . RESET CHAR POSITION 
          ANX,U     X6,1              .   
          J         0,X6              . TRY AGAIN     
. END OF CARD. SET POINTER SO EOC WILL BE FIRST UNLESS CARD READ IS GIVEN     
EOS.  
          L,U       A2,' '            . END OF CARD SET CHAR AS BLANK   
          S         A2,NGX            . SAVE NEXT CHAR
          ANX,U     X8,1              .   
          S         X8,INIMGE         .   
          LX        X7,(1,6)            . 
          S         X7,CHRPOS         . SET CHRPOS AND INIMGE     
          DL        A4,BLANKS           . 
          DTE       A4,GITV           . DID WE HAVE ITEM    
          J         1,X11             . YES, RETURN IT
          J         0,X11             . NO, RETURN EOS
. STORE MAXIMUM OF 12 CHARACTERS INTO GITV
STRX. 
          S,S1      A2,GITV             . 
          S,S2      A2,GITV           .   
          S,S3      A2,GITV           .   
          S,S4      A2,GITV           .   
          S,S5      A2,GITV           .   
          S,S6      A2,GITV           .   
          S,S1      A2,GITV+1         .   
          S,S2      A2,GITV+1         .   
          S,S3      A2,GITV+1         .   
          S,S4      A2,GITV+1         .   
          S,S5      A2,GITV+1         .   
          S,S6      A2,GITV+1         .   
          J         $+1               . TOO MANY CHARACTERS 
          L         A0,TOLC           .   
          J         ERROR               . 
/.    
. ***********************************************************************     
DTO.                                  .   
.     
.     CONVERTS FIELD DATA DECIMAL NUMBER IN A1,A2 TO OCTAL IN A4  
.     
          SZ        A4                .   
          L,U       R1,5              .   
DTOL      SZ        A0                  . 
          LDSL      A0,6              . GET FIRST NUM 
          TNE,U     A0,' '            . END OF NUMBER 
          J         1,X11             . YES     
          AN,U      A0,'0'            . FD TO BCD     
          TN        A0                . CHECK FOR NUMERIC   
          TG,U      A0,10             .   
          J         0,X11             . ILLEGAL CHAR RETURN 
          MSI,U     A4,10             .   
          A         A4,A0             .   
          JGD       R1,DTOL           .   
          TNZ       A2                . HAVE WE DONE BOTH WORDS   
          J         1,X11             . YES, RETURN   
          LDSL      A1,36             . MOVE A2 TO A1 
          J         DTOL-1            . REPEAT  
. ******************************************************************************    
.     
. HANDLES CHECKPOINT/RSTART PARAMETERS    
.         CKPTRS=FILE,X,#     
.         X=DEVICE TYPE 'T'=TAPE,'D'=DISK,'F'=FASTRAN 
.         #=NUMBER OF CKPTS POSSIBLE
.     
.     
CKPTRS    LX        X3,GITV            .  
          LA        A2,NGX             . GET THE NEXT CHARACTER   
          TE,U      A2,'='             . AN EQUAL?    
          J         FIE1               . NO , BAD PARAMETER 
          SZ        CKPFLG             . CLEAR THE CKPTRS FLAG    
          LMJ       X11,GIT            . GET FILENAME 
          J         FIE1A              . ERROR  
          DL        A1,GITV            . GET THE FILENAME   
          DS        A1,CKFLNM          . STORE THE FILENAME IN CKPT IMAGE     
          LA        A2,NGX             . GET THE NEXT CHARACTER   
          TE,U      A2,','             . A COMMA
          J         FIE1A              . NO , ERROR   
          LMJ       X11,GIT            . GET THE PARAMETER  
          J         FIE1A              . ERROR  
          LA,S1     A1,GITV            .  
          TNE,U     A1,'T'             .  
          J         STORET             .  
          TNE,U     A1,'D'             .  
          J         STORET             .  
          TE,U      A1,'F'             .  
          J         FIE1A              . NO LEGAL DEVICE TYPE     
STORET    SA        A1,DEVTYPE         . STORE THE DEVICE TYPE    
          LA        A2,NGX             .  
          TE,U      A2,','             .  
          J         FIE1A              .  
          LMJ       X11,GIT            . GET THE NUMBER OF CKPTS POSSIBLE     
          J         FIE1A              . ERROR  
          LA        A1,GITV            .  
          LMJ       X11,DTO            . CONVERT FD TO OCTAL
          J         FIE1A     
          TG,U      A4,10              .  
          J         FIE1A              .  
          SA        A4,NUMCKS          .  
          J         NXTPARAM           .  
          LA        A2,NGX             .  
          TE,U      A2,' '             .  
          J         FIE1A              .  
FIE1A     . 
          LX        X3,('CKPTRS      ') . 
          J         FIE1               .  
          . 
          . 
          . 
          . 
          . 
          . 
/.    
. ***********************************************************************     
. FILEOUT AND FILEIN    
.     
. HANDLES INPUT AND OUTPUT FILE NAMES     
.      FILEOUT=N  
.         WHERE N IS OUTPUT FILE NAME     
.         (IF PARAMETER IS NOT INPUT, 'OUTTAPE' IS ASSIGNED)
.      FILEIN=N   
.         WHERE N IS INPUT FILE NAME OR 'CARDS' 
.         (ALWAYS REQUIRED FOR SORT, NOT USED FOR MERGE)    
.     
FILEOUT.    
          L,U       X2,OUTPKT 
          TE        A2,('T     ')     . VALID PARAMETER     
          J         ILLEGAL           . NO
          J         FIE 
FILEIN.     
          L,U       X2,INPKT  
          SZ        REQFIN            .   
          TE        A2,BLANKS         . IS ITEM CORRECT     
          J         ILLEGAL           . NO
. IF TAPE, STORE FILE NAME IN TAPE I/O PACKET   
. IF CARD INPUT, CHANGE PARAMETER TABLE ADDRESS FOR OPEN RETURN   
FIE.  
          L         X3,GITV             . NAME FOR ERROR MSG
          L         A2,NGX    
          TE,U      A2,'='            . ITEM FOLLOWED BY =  
          J         FIE1              . NO
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET FILE NAME 
          J         FIE1                . 
          DL        A1,GITV   
          DS        A1,0,X2             . INPUT OR OUTPUT FILE NAME     
          LA        A0,X2              .  
          TNE,U     A0,OUTPKT          . FILEOUT CARD 
          J         FLCRDO             . YES    
          TE        A1,('CARDS ')     . INPUT FROM CARDS    
          J         FIE0                . 
          L,U       A0,FCARD          . ADDRESS OF CARD INPUT ROUTINE TO
          S         A0,FASTFILEIN     .   
          S         A1,INPKT          . SET TO USE AS FLAG  
          J         NXTPARAM          . GET NEXT PARAMETER  
.  CHECK FOR NO OUTPUT FILE WITH FILEIN=CARDS   
.  IF FILEOUT=CARDS BUT NO @ASG  CARDS CREATE PUNCH$ FILE @SYM,C  
.  PUNCH$   
FLCRDO .    
          TE        A1,('CARDS ')     . IS FILEOUT=CARDS WITH NONE ASGND
          J         FIE0              . NO  FIND IT   
          L,U       A0,CARDCK         . LOAD OUTPUT CARD CODE ADDRESS   
          S         A0,FASTFILEOUT    . SET FASTRAND TYPE   
          S         A1,OUTPKT         . SET OUTPKT TO CARDS FOR TEST PURPOSES 
          J         NXTPARAM          . CONTINUE, GETTING NEXT PARAMETER CARD 
. TEST INPUT FILE FOR FASTRAND FORMAT     
FIE0. 
          L         A0,X2               . 
          TNE,U     A0,OUTPKT           . OUTPUT FILE?
          J         FIE2                . YES   
          L         A0,X2               . 
          ON        MARTIME            .  
          TNE,U     A0,OUTPKT          .  
          DS        A1,OUTPKT1         .  
          OFF       MARTIME            .  
          TE,U      A0,INPKT            . TEST IF INPUT FILE
          J         NXTPARAM            . NO    
          DS        A1,FACPKT           . 
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . 
          ER        FACIL$              . TEST IF FILE IS FASTRAND
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          . IS IT ASSIGNED ?    
          J         FIE3              . NO GO ERR OUT 
          L,S1      A0,FACPKT+6         . 
          TG,U      A0,020              . 
          S         A0,INPKT+8          . 
          TG,U      A0,020            . FASTRAND OR DRUM FILE?    
          S         A0,FASTFILEIN     . YES     
          TZ        FASTFILEIN        . TEST TAPE OF MASS   
          J         $+3               . JUMP NOT TAPE 
          L,S1      A2,FACPKT+8       . LOAD NUM REELS
          S,H1      A2,INPKT+6        . STORE FOR INPKT CHECKS    
OUT1CK    LA        A2,NGX            . GET NEXT CHARACTER  
          TE,U      A2,','            . MORE PARAMETERS ?   
          J         NXTPARAM          . JUMP NEXT PARAMETER
