          L         A0,X2             . LOAD ID 
          TNE,U     A0,OUTPKT         . TEST IF OUTPKT
          J         FIE1              . YES JUMP
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT FIELD
          J         FIE1              . JUMP TO ERROR NO FIELD RET
          L         A1,GITV           . LOAD FIELD    
          TE        A1,('F     ')     . IS IT FRE OPTION    
          J         $+3               . JUMP NOT F    
          SZ        FREEINF           . SET SW TO FREE INPUT FILE 
          J         NXTPARAM          . JUMPTO NEXT PARAMERTER    
          J         FIE1              . ERROR RETURN  
.     
. ERROR     
FIE1      S         X3,FIE1M            . 
          L         A0,FIE1P            . PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY
          J         ERROR     
FIE2      DS        A1,FACPKT           . LOAD FILE NAME INTO FACPKT    
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . LOAD ADDRESS OF PACKET  
          ER        FACIL$              . 
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          . IS IT ASSIGNED  ?   
          J         FIE3              .  NO JUMP TO ERR 3   
          L,S1      A0,FACPKT+6         . LOAD EQUIPMENT CODE     
          TG,U      A0,020            . FASTRAND OR DRUM FILE?    
          S         A0,FASTFILEOUT    . YES     
          TZ        FASTFILEOUT       . TAPE OR MASS S ?    
          J         OUT1CK            . CHECK FOR MORE PARAMETERS 
          L,S1      A2,FACPKT+8       . LOAD NUM REELS
          S,H1      A2,OUTPKT+6       . STORE FOR OUTPUT    
          J         OUT1CK            . DO OTHER CHECKS     
FIE3      DL        A1,FACPKT         . LOAD FILE NAME
          DS        A1,FIE3M          . STORE IN MESSAGE    
          L         A0,FIE3P          . LOAD MESSAGE THING  
          ER        PRINT$            . PRINT MESSAGE 
          ER        ERR$              .   
. ********************************************************  
. THE FOLLOWING CODING INTERPRETS THE MULTIPLE INPUT FILES  
. SPECIFICATION CARD.   
.     
FILESIN.    
          L         A0,(2,0)          .   
          S         A0,TEMP1          .   
NXTFIL    LMJ       X11,GIT           .   
          J         FIE1              .   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD FILE NAME
          DS        A1,FACPKT         . STORE IN FACPKT     
          L,U       A0,FACPKT         . LOAD PKT ADDR 
          ER        FACIL$            .   
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          .  IS FILE ASSIGNED ? 
          J         FIE3              . NO ERR  
          L         A0,TEMP1          .   
          DS        A1,TEMPSPOT,*A0   .   
          S         A0,TEMP1          .   
          L         A2,NGX            .   
          TNE,U     A2,' '            .   
          J         NAMEFL            .   
          L         A1,FILECNT        .   
          A,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,FILECNT        .   
          J         NXTFIL            .   
NAMEFL    S         X5,TEMP1          .   
          L         X5,(2,0)          .   
NAM       AN,U      A0,2              .   
          DL        A1,TEMPSPOT,A0    .   
          DS        A1,FILNAMES,*X5   .   
          SZ        A1                .   
          LXM       A1,A0             .   
          TZ        A1                .   
          J         NAM               .   
          L         X5,TEMP1          .   
          SZ        REQFIN            .   
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,INPFILFLG      .   
          L,U       X2,INPKT          .   
          DL        A1,TEMPSPOT       . LOAD FIRST FILE NAME
          DS        A1,0,X2           .   
          DS        A1,FACPKT         .   
          L,U       A0,FACPKT         .   
          ER        FACIL$            .   
          L,S1      A0,FACPKT+6       .   
          TG,U      A0,020            .   
          S         A0,INPKT+8        .   
          TLE,U     A0,030            .   
          S         A0,TAPEDRUM       .   
          TG,U      A0,020            .   
          S         A0,FASTFILEIN     .   
          TZ        FASTFILEIN        . TAPE OR MAS ? 
          J         NXTPARAM          .   
          L,S1      A2,FACPKT+8       . LOAD NUMBER OF REELS
          S,H1      A2,INPKT+6        . STORE FOR INPUT CHECK     
          J         NXTPARAM          . CONTINUE PROCESSING CARDS 
/.    
. ***********************************************************************     
. MERGE     
. HANDLES MERGE PARAMETER CARDS     
.      MERGE=X OR MERGE=X,Y,...,Z   
.         WHERE X,Y,...,Z ARE INPUT FILE NAMES  
.     
MERGE.
          L         A2,NGX              . GET NEXT CHARACTER
          TE,U      A2,'='              . IS 'MERGE' FOLLOWED BY EQUAL  
          J         ME1                 . NO, ERROR   
          SZ        REQFIN              . SET INPUT REQUIRED SW OFF     
          L         A10,(' MERGE')      . SET INDICATOR TO MERGE  
. BUILD I/O PACKETS FOR MERGE FILES 
MOPN. 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET INPUT FILE NAME     
          J         ME1                 . IF BLANK, ERROR   
          L         A0,MERGEPT          . MERGE INPUT FILE POINTER
          DL        A2,GITV             . GET FILE NAME     
          DS        A2,MERGEPKT,A0      . 
          DS        A2,FACPKT           . 
          L,U       A2,020              . READ FUNCTION TO  
          S,S2      A2,MERGEPKT+3,A0    . I/O PACKET  
          L         A2,TCORE$           . SORT CORE ADDRESS 
          S,H2      A2,MERGEPKT+4,A0    . FOR INITIAL LABELED TAPE READ 
.     
. TEST INPUT FILE FOR FASTRAND FORMAT     
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . 
          ER        FACIL$              . TEST IF FILE IS FASTRAND
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          . IS FILE ASSIGNED    
          J         FIE3              . NO JUMP TO ERR
          L         A0,MERGEPT          . 
          L,S1      A1,FACPKT+6         . 
          TG,U      A1,020              . 
          S         A1,FASTFILEIN     .  STORE SO NON ZERO  
          TZ        FASTFILEIN        . TAPE OR MASS STOR ? 
          J         $+3               . JUMP MASS STORAGE   
          L,S1      A2,FACPKT+8       . LOAD NUMBER REELS   
          S,H1      A2,MERGEPKT+6,A0  . STORE FOR MERGE INFO
          TG,U      A1,020            . RETEST IF TPE OR MASS     
          S         A1,MERGEPKT+8,A0    . 
          A,U       A0,PACKSIZE         . UPDATE POINTER    
          S         A0,MERGEPT          . 
          L         A2,NGX              . GET NEXT CHAR PARAMETER CARD  
          TE,U      A2,','              . IS IT A COMMA     
          J         NXTPARAM            . NO, GET NEXT ITEM 
          J         MOPN                . YES, GO GET NEXT FILE   
.     
. ERRORS    
ME1       L         X3,('MERGE ')       . MERGE PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY
          J         FIE1                . 
/.    
.     
. *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  * *  *  *  *  *  * 
.  BLOCKO=N    N = NUM RECS IN OUTPUT BLOCK     
.     
BLOCKO.     
          L         A2,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TE,U      A2,'='            . IS IT EQUAL   
          J         BE1               . NO ERROR
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT FIELD
          J         BE2               . ERROR RETURN NO FIELD     
          DL        A1,GITV           .  LOAD FIELD   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT FIELD TO OCTAL    
          J         BE2               . ERROR RETURN NOT NUMERIC  
          S         A4,OUTBLKSIZ      . STORE BLOCKING FACTOR     
          SZ        OBKFLG            . SET OUT BLK PARA GIVEN    
          J         NXTPARAM          . EXIT TO NEXT PARAMETER    
.     
.     
.  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *     
.     
.       BLKCHI=N       N= NUMBER CHARACTERS IN BLOCK  
.     
BLKCHI.     
          L         A2,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TE,U      A2,'='            . IS IT EQUAL SIGN    
          J         BE1               . ERROR IF IT ISNT    
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT FILELD     
          J         BE2               . ERROR RETURN IF NO B?FIELD
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD NEXT FIELD     
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . GO CONVERT TO OCTAL 
          J         BE2               . ERROR RETURN NON NUMERIC  
          L         A2,TYPECONST1     . LOAD NUM CHARACTERS PER WORD    
          SZ        A3                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          DI        A3,A2             . FIND NUM WORDS PER BLOCK  
          TZ        A4                . TEST IF REMAINDER   
          A,U       A3,1     [        . ADD 1 WORD IF REMAINDER   
          S         A3,BLKLGN         . STORE BLKLENGTH IN WDS    
          SZ        REQBLK            . SET REQ PARA FLG TO ZERO  
          SZ        BLIFLG            . SET BLOCK FLAG IN TO ZERO 
          J         NXTPARAM          . JUMP TO NEXT PARAMETER    
.     
.     
.  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  
.     
.   BLKCHO=N      N= NUMBER CHARACTER IN OUTPUT BLOCK SIZE  
.     
BLKCHO.     
          L         A2,NGX            .  LOAD NEXT CHARACTER
          TE,U      A2,'='            . IS IT EQUAL SIGN    
          J         BE1               . ERROR IF IT IS NOT  
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GO GET NEXT FIELD   
          J         BE2               . ERROR RETURN NO FIELD     
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD FIELD    
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         BE2               . ERROR RETURN IF NOT OCTAL NUM   
          L         A2,TYPECONST1     . LOAD NUM CHARACTERS PER WORD    
          SZ        A3                . CLEAR FOR DIVIDE    
          DI        A3,A2             . FIND WORDS PER BLOCK
          TZ        A4                . IS THERE REMAINDER  
          A,U       A3,1              . YES ADD 1 WORD
          S         A3,NEWBLKLN       . STORE OUT BLK LENGTH
          SZ        OBKFLG            . SET OUTPUT FLAG GIVEN     
          SZ        BLOFLG            . SET CHARACTER BLOCK BFLAG TO ZERO     
          J         NXTPARAM          .  EXIT TO GET NEXT PARAMETER     
/.    
. ***********************************************************************     
BLOCK.
.     
. HANDLES NUMBER OF RECORDS PER BLOCK     
.      BLOCK=N    
.         WHERE N IS NUMBER OF RECORDS PER BLOCK
.         (OPTIONAL FOR LABELED TAPES OR CARD INPUT)  
.     
          L         A2,NGX            . GET NEXT CHAR 
          TE,U      A2,'='            . IS IT = 
          J         BE1               .NO 
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET ITEM
          J         BE2                 . 
          DL        A1,GITV           .   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT BLOCK SIZE TO OCTAL     
          J         BE2               . NON-NUMERIC   
          S         A4,BLKSIZ         . SAVE BLOCK SIZE     
          SZ        REQBLK            .   
          J         NXTPARAM          .   
.     
. ERRORS    
BE1       L         X3,(' BLOCK')       . BLOCK PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY
          J         FIE1
BE2       L         A0,BE2P             . NON-NUMERIC BLOCKING FACTOR   
          J         ERROR               . 
/.    
. ****************************************************************************
BIAS. 
.     
.     
. SPECIFIES THE BIAS FACTOR (THE RATIO OF THE NUMBER OF INPUT RECORDS TO
. THE NUMBER OF NATURAL SEQUENCES EXISTING IN THE DATA)     
.      BIAS=XXXX  
.         WHERE XXXX IS A DECIMAL NUMBER OF UP TO 4 DIGITS WHICH INDICATES    
.         THE BIAS.  A DECIMAL POINT IS IMPLIED ONE DIGIT FROM THE RIGHT
.         OF THE NUMBER.
.     
          L,U       A6,6                . PARAMETER TABLE ENTRY FOR BIAS
          L         X3,('  BIAS')       . 
          J         DECIMAL             . 
.     
.     
. ****************************************************************************
.     
RECORD.     
.     
. SPECIFIES THE VOLUME TO BE SORTED IN THOUSANDS OF RECORDS 
.      RECORD=N   
.         WHERE N IS THE NUMBER OF THOUSANDS OF INPUT RECORDS     
.     
.     
          L         X3,('RECORD')       . 
.     
          L,U       A6,064              . PARAMETER TABLE ENTRY FOR RECORD    
          J         DECIMAL             . 
. ****************************************************************************
.     
LIMDRM.     
.     
. SPECIFIES THE MINIMUM DRUM BLOCK SIZE IN WORDS
.      LIMDRM=N   
.         WHERE N IS THE NUMBER OF WORDS  
.     
          L         X3,('LIMDRM')       . 
          L,U       A6,061              . PARAMETER TABLE ENTRY FOR RECORD    
          J         DECIMAL             . 
.     
.     
. ****************************************************************************
.     
LIMFST.     
.     
. SPECIFIES THE MINIMUM FASTRAND BLOCK SIZE TO BE USED IN WORDS   
.      LIMFST=N   
.         WHERE N IS THE NUMBER OF WORDS  
.     
          L         X3,('LIMFST')       . 
          L,U       A6,062              . PARAMETER TABLE ENTRY FOR LIMFST    
          J         DECIMAL             . 
. ******************************************************************************    
LINKSZ    . 
. HANDLES THE LINK-SIZE FOR VARIABLE LENGTH RECORDS   
. LINKSZ=N  
 . WHERE N IS THE RECORD SIZE IN WORDS OF THE SMALLEST RECORD TO GET LINK SZ  
.    ( NOT AVAILABLE FOR NON-LABELED FILES)     
.     
          LX        X3,('LINKSZ')      . SET PARAMETER TYPE 
          LA,U      A6,02              . PARAMETER TABLE ENTRY FOR VRSZ LINKSIZE    
          SZ        VARFLG             . CLEAR VARIABLE LENGTH FLAG     
          J         DECIMAL            .  
.     
.     
. *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *   
.  RSZW=N    N=NUMBER WORDS IN RECORD     
.     
RSZW. 
          L         X3,('  RSZW')     . LOAD IN WORDS INDICATOR   
          L,U       A6,1              . SET FOR SPT   
          J         DECIMAL           . JUMP TO CALCULATE   
.     
. ***********************************************************************     
RSZ.  
.     
. HANDLES RECORD SIZE VERIFICATION  
.      RSZ=N
.         WHERE N IS RECORD SIZE IN CHARACTERS  
.         (OPTIONAL FOR LABELED TAPES, OTHERWISE REQUIRED)  
.     
          L         X3,('   RSZ')       . 
          L,U       A6,1                . PARAMETER TABLE ENTRY FOR RSZ 
.     
.     
. *************************************************************************** 
.     
. COMVERT DECIMAL VALUE TO OCTAL AND STORE IN THE PARAMETER TABLE 
.     
.      X3 = PARAMETER CARD TYPE     
.      A6 = PARAMETER TABLE ENTRY TYPE    
.     
DECIMAL.    
          L,U       A2,'='            . IS ITEM FOLLOWED BY EQUAL 
          TE        A2,NGX    
          J         FIE1                . NO    
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NUMBER OF CHARACTERS
          J         RSZE2               . 
          DL        A1,GITV   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         RSZE2             . NON NUMERIC   
.     
. IF RSZ INPUT, CALCULATE RECORD SIZE IN WORDS  
          L         A0,('  RSZW')     . LOAD RESZ IN WDS INDICATOR
          TNE       A0,X3             . IS THIS THE PARAMETER     
          J         RSZW1             . YES JUMP
          L         A0,('   RSZ')       . TEST IF RSZ 
          TE        A0,X3               . 
          J         DECIMAL1            . NO    
          SSL       A3,36     
          L         A1,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A3,A1               . CONVERT NUM CHARS TO WORDS    
          TZ        A4                . REMAINDER?    
          A,U       A3,1
          S         A3,RECSIZ         . SAVE NUM WORDS IN RECORD  
          L         A4,A3     
          SZ        REQRSZ              . 
          J         DECIMAL1          . JUMP AROUND RSZW CODE     
RSZW1     S         A4,RECSIZ         . STORE RECORD SIZE   
          SZ        REQRSZ            . SET REQ PARA FLG TO ZERO  
.     
. STORE VALUE IN PARAMETER TABLE    
DECIMAL1  L         A1,PARPNT           . POINTER INTO PARAMETER TABLE  
          S         A4,0,A1             . PUT DECIMAL VALUE IN TABLE    
          S,S1      A6,0,A1             . ENTRY TYPE TO TABLE     
          A,U       A1,1              . INCREMENT POINTER   
          S         A1,PARPNT 
          J         NXTPARAM  
.     
. ERRORS    
RSZE2     DL        A1,GITV   
          DS        A1,RE2M+4 
          L         A0,RE2P             . ILLEGAL DECIMAL VALUE   
          J         ERROR               . 
.     
.  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *     
. KEYW=A,B,C,E,F   A= WORD KEY BEGINS B= START BIT    
.      C = NUMBER BITS  E =TYPE  F= ORDER 
.     
KEYW      L         A2,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TE,U      A2,'='            . IS IT EQUAL SIGN    
          J         KEYE1             . NO ERROR
.  GET STARTING WORD    
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT FIELD
          J         KEYE4             . ERROR IF NO FIELD   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD FIELD    
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         KEYE2             . ERROR RETURN NON NUMERIC  
          AN,U      A4,1              . SUBTRACT ONE FOR ZERO WORD
          L         X1,PARPNT         . LOAD SPT POINTER    
          S,H1      A4,1,X1           . STORE STARTING WORD 
. GET STARTING BIT
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT FIELD
          J         KEYE4             . ERROR IF NO FIELD   
          DL        A1,GITV            . LOAD FIELD   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         KEYE2             . ERROR RETURN NON NUMERIC  
          L,U       A3,044            . LOAD CONSTANT 
          AN        A3,A4             . FIND BIT MASK 
          S,S4      A3,0,X1           .  STORE BIT MASK     
. GET NUMBER OF BITS    
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT FIELD
          J         KEYE4             . ERROR IF NO FIELD   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD NEXT FIELD     
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         KEYE2             . ERROR RETURN NON NUMERIC  
          S,H2      A4,1,X1           , STORE NUMBER BITS   
          J         RESTKY            . USE SAME CODE AS KEY FR TYPE ORDER    
/.    
. **********************************************************************
KEY.  
.     
. HANDLES DECODE OF THE KEY PARAMETER CARD
.      KEY=A,B,C,D,E    
.      WHERE A IS STARTING CHARACTER POSITION OF KEY FIELD  
.            B IS NUMBER OF CHARACTERS IN FIELD 
.            C IS TYPE OF KEY FIELD 
.            D IS ORDER OF SORTING SEQUENCE (OPTIONAL)
.            E IS FIELD NUMBER (OPTIONAL) 
.      (REQUIRED FOR EACH KEY FIELD)
.     
          L         A2,NGX            .   
          TE,U      A2,'='            . FOLLOWED BY = 
          J         KEYE1             . NO
. CHARACTER POSITION OF KEY FIELD   
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET CHAR POSITION ITEM    
          J         KEYE4               . 
          DL        A1,GITV   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT 
          J         KEYE2             . NON NUMERIC   
          AN,U      A4,1
          SSL       A3,36     
          L         A1,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A3,A1               . CONVERT CHAR TO WORDS   
          L         X1,PARPNT         . GET POINTER INTO PARAM TABLE    
          S,H1      A3,1,X1           . STORE NUM WORDS     
          L         A1,TYPECONST2       . LOAD BITS/CHAR    
          MSI       A4,A1               . GET STARTING BIT POSITION     
          A,U       A4,1
          S,S4      A4,0,X1           . TO TABLE
. NUMBER OF CHARACTERS IN KEY FIELD 
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NUMBER OF CHAR IN KEY 
          J         KEYE4               . 
          DL        A1,GITV   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         KEYE2             . NON NUMERIC   
          L         A1,TYPECONST2       . LOAD BITS/CHAR    
          MSI       A4,A1               . CHARS TO BITS     
          S,H2      A4,1,X1           . NUM BITS IN KEY TO TABLE  
RESTKY    L,U       A4,077            . ENTRY CONTINUATION TYPE   
          S,S1      A4,1,X1   
. TYPE OF KEY FIELD FORMAT    
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET RECORD TYPE     
          J         KEYE4               . 
          L         A1,GITV   
          SSL       A1,30     
          TE,U      A1,'A'            . VALID TYPE    
          TNE,U     A1,'B'    
          J         KOK               . YES     
          TE,U      A1,'D'    
          TNE,U     A1,'M'    
          J         KOK 
          TE,U      A1,'U'    
          TNE,U     A1,'P'            .   
          J         KOK               .   
          TE,U      A1,'Q'            .   
          TNE,U     A1,'R'            .   
          J         KOK               .   
          TE,U      A1,'S'            .   
          J         KEYE2             . BAD ENTRY     
KOK       S,S2      A1,0,X1             . RECORD TYPE TO TABLE    
. ORDER     
          DL        A1,BLANKS         .   
          DS        A1,GITV   
          SZ,S3     0,X1                . SET TO ASCENDING FIELD  
          L         A1,NGX            . GET NEXT CHAR 
          TNE,U     A1,' '            . IF BLANK DO NOT SCAN CRAD 
          J         KOK2                . IF BLANK, ASSUME ASCENDING    
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET ORDER OF SORT( ASCEND OR)   
          J         KEYE4               . 
          L         A1,GITV   
          SSL       A1,30     
          TE,U      A1,'A'            . VALID ORDER   
          TNE,U     A1,'D'              . 
          J         KOK1              . YES     
          J         KEYE2             . BAD     
KOK1      S,S3      A1,0,X1           . ORDER TO TABLE
. FIELD NUMBER    
KOK2      SZ        A2                .   
          L         A1,0,X1           .   
          L         R2,(0,07777)      .   
          S         A2,TEMP2          .   
          MLU       A1,TEMP2          .   
          S         A2,0,X1           .   
          L         A1,NGX    
          TNE,U     A1,' '            . IF NEXT CHAR BLANK, DO NOT
          J         KFIRST              . SCAN CARD   
          LMJ       X11,GIT   
          J         KEYE4               . 
          L         A1,GITV             . GET FIELD ORDER ENTRY   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         KEYE2             . NON NUMERIC   
          S         A4,TEMP2          .   
          L         A1,0,X1           .   
          L         R2,(0,07777)      .   
          MLU       A1,TEMP2          .   
          S         A2,0,X1           .   
KFIRST    L         A1,GITV             . 
          TZ,H1     KEYTYP            . FIRST KEY HANDLED   
          J         KTFIRST           . YES     
          L,H2      A2,KEYTYP         . GET PREVIOUS KEY ORDER    
          TE        A2,(0,'   ')      . WAS OTHER KEYS WITH NO ORDER    
          J         KS2               . NO
          TE        A1,BLANKS         . YES THEN THIS ONE MUST NOT HAVE ORDER 
          J         KEYE3             . IT DOES SO ERROR    
          J         KTOK
KS2       TNE       A1,BLANKS         . OTHER KEYS HAD ORDER SO THIS MUST     
          J         KEYE3             . IT DOES NOT SO ERROR
KTOK      L,U       A1,3
          S,S1      A1,0,X1           . ENTRY TYPE TO TABLE 
          A,U       X1,2              . INCREMENT POINTER   
          S         X1,PARPNT 
          SZ        REQKEY    
          J         NXTPARAM  
KTFIRST   SZ,H1     KEYTYP            . FIRST KEY HANDLED   
          SSL       A1,18             . SAVE ORDER    
          S,H2      A1,KEYTYP 
          J         KTOK
.     
. ERRORS    
KEYE1     L         X3,('   KEY')       . KEY PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY  
          J         FIE1
KEYE2     DL        A1,GITV   
          DS        A1,KE2M+4 
          L         A0,KE2P             . ILLEGAL KEY PARAMETER   
          J         ERROR     
KEYE3     L         A0,KE3P             . FIELD ORDER DOES NOT AGREE    
          J         ERROR     
KEYE4     L         A0,KE4P             . REQUIRED KEY PARAMETER MISSING
          J         ERROR               . 
/.    
. **********************************************************************
TAPES.
.     
. HANDLES SCRATCH TAPE FILES FOR THE SORT 
.      TAPES=NN,TTT     
.      WHERE NN IS THE NUMBER OF SCRATCH TAPES  
.            WHERE TTT IS THE EQUIPMENT TYPE (OPTIONAL, DEFAULT IS 'T') 
.      (MAXIMUM OF 24 TAPES, MINIMUM OF 2)
.      (OPTIONAL) 
.     
          L,U       A2,'='              . 
          TE        A2,NGX              . IS 'TAPE' FOLLOWED BY EQUAL   
          J         TAPERR1             . NO, ERROR   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NUMBER OF SCRATCH TAPES   
          J         TAPERR1             . IF BLANK, ERROR   
          L         A1,GITV             . 
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT NR OF TAPES TO OCTAL  
          J         TAPERR1             . ERROR IF NON NUMERIC    
          TG,U      A4,25               . TEST IF WITHIN MAXIMUM OF 24  
          J         TAPERR1             . NO, ERROR   
.     
. ASSIGN EQUIPMENT TYPE, IF SPECIFIED     
          L         A2,NGX              . 
          TNE,U     A2,' '              . TEST IF EQUIPMENT TYPE IS SPECIFIED 
          J         TAPE0               . NO    
          TE,U      A2,','              . 
          J         TAPERR1             . 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET EQUIPMENT TYPE
          J         TAPERR1             . IF BLANK, ERROR   
          L         A1,GITV             . 
          LSSC      A1,18               . MAX OF 3 CHAR EQUIPMENT TYPE  
TAPE0A    S,H2      A1,TAPASGM+2        . 
          L,U       A2,' '              . 
          TE,S6     A2,TAPASGM+2        . LOOP IF LESS THAN 3 CHAR EQUIP TYPE 
          J         TAPE0               . 
          SSL       A1,6                . 
          J         TAPE0A              . 
.     
. ASSIGN TAPE AND INCLUDE IN SORT PARAMETER TABLE     
TAPE0     L         X1,PARPNT           . POINTER TO SORT PARAM TABLE   
          LXI,U     X1,1                . ADDRESS OF TAPE FILE NAMES    
          L         X2,(2,TAPTAB)       . ADDRESS OF TAPE FILE NAMES    
          TE,U      A4,1                . IF ONLY 1 TAPE, USE MIN OF 2  
          AN,U      A4,1                . NUMBER OF TAPES TO BE ASSIGN  
          S,H2      A4,SCRTPC         . STORE # SCR TP FILES ASSIGNED   
          L,U       X3,023              . EXTERNAL FILE INDICATOR FOR TABLE   
TAPE1     DL        A0,0,X2             . GET TAPE FILE NAME
          DS        A0,FACPKT           . STORE FILE NAME IN FACILITIES PACKET
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . 
          ER        FACIL$              . SEE IF FILE IS ASSIGNED 
          TZ,S1     FACPKT+6            . 
          J         TAPE1A              . ALREADY ASSIGNED  
          L         A0,0,X2           . GET TAPE FILE NAME  
          SSL       A0,24             .   
          S         A0,TEMP2          .   
          L         R2,(0,07777)      .   
          L         A0,TAPASGM+1      .   
          MLU       A0,TEMP2          .   
          S         A1,TAPASGM+1      .   
          L         A0,(TAPASGL,TAPASGM) .
          ER        CSF$                . ASSIGN TAPE 
          TP        A0                  . GOOD ASSIGN?
          J         TAPE2               . NO    
          L,U       A0,0,X2             . ADDRESS OF TAPE FILE NAME     
          S         A0,0,X1             . STORE IN SORT PARAMETERFTABLE 
          S,S1      X3,0,*X1            . SET EXTERNAL FILE INDICATOR   
TAPE1A    NOP       0,0,*X2             . 
          JGD       A4,TAPE1            . 
          S,H2      X1,PARPNT           . SAVE INCREMENTED POINTER
          J         NXTPARAM            . 
.     
. IF REQUEST REJECTED, SEND MESSAGE TO OPERATOR 
TAPE2     L         A3,(TAPASGL,TAPASGM)  
          LMJ       X10,OPREQ          . SEND MESSAGE TO CONSOL   
          J         TAPE1               . RETURN IF OPERATOR SPECIFIES CONTINUE     
.     
. ERRORS    
TAPERR1   L         X3,('  TAPE')       . TAPE PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY 
          J         FIE1                . 
/.    
. **********************************************************************
LABEL.
.     
. HANDLES LABEL SPECIFICATION 
.      LABEL=X    
.         WHERE X IS 'FORM01' OR 'MODE1'  
.         STANDARD  OR  OMITTED  STANDARD IS DEFAULT  
.     
          L,U       A2,'='              . 
          TE        A2,NGX              . IS 'LABEL' FOLLOWED BY EQUAL  
          J         LABERR1             . NO, ERROR   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET LABEL TYPE    
          J         LABERR1             . IF BLANK, ERROR   
          L         A0,GITV           . LOAD TYPE OF LABEL  
          L,U       A2,3              . LOAD STANDARD FLAG  
          TE        A0,('STANDA')     . TEST IF STANDARD    
          J         $+3               . NO JUMP 
          S         A2,STDLBL         . STORE TYPE OF LABEL 
          J         NXTPARAM          . EXIT    
          L,U       A2,1              . LOAD OMIITED FLAG   
          TE        A0,('OMITTE')     . TEST IF OMITTED     
          J         $+3               . NO JUMP 
          S         A2,STDLBL         . STORE OMITTED FLAG  
          J         NXTPARAM          . EXIT    
          SZ        STDLBL              . SET STDLBL TO 0 FOR NON-LABELED     
          TE        A0,('FORM01')       . TEST FOR FORM01   
          TNE       A0,('MODE1 ')       . TEST FOR MODE1    
          J         NXTPARAM            . 
.     
. ERRORS    
LABERR1   L         X3,(' LABEL')       . LABEL PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY
          J         FIE1
/.    
. **********************************************************************
CORE. 
.     
. HANDLES CORE REQUEST FOR SORT SCRATCH AREA    
.      CORE=X     
.         (OPTIONAL, STANDARD REQUESTED IF CORE PARAMETER NOT INPUT)    
.     
          L,U       A2,'='              . 
          TE        A2,NGX              . IS 'CORE' FOLLOWED BY EQUAL   
          J         CORERR1             . NO, ERROR   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET REQUESTED CORE SIZE 
          J         CORERR1             . IF BLANK, ERROR   
          DL        A1,GITV             . 
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT CORE SIZE TO OCTAL    
          J         CORERR1             . IF NON-NUMERIC, ERROR   
          S         A4,CORESAVE         . SAVE CORE SIZE    
          J         NXTPARAM            . 
.     
. ERRORS    
CORERR1   L         X3,('  CORE')       . CORE PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY 
          J         FIE1                . 
/.    
. ************************************************************************    
.     
SEQUENCE.   
.     
. HANDLES CHANGE IN COLLATING SEQUENCE    
.      SEQUENCE=X 
.         WHERE X IS THE NEW 64-CHARACTER SET   
.      (OPTIONAL, MUST BE ON ONE CARD)    
.     
          TE        A2,('CE    ')     . IS IT CFH SEQ 
          J         $+5               . NO JUMP 
          L,U       A1,1              . LOAD CFH SEQ TYPE   
          S         A1,SEQTYP         . STORE TYPE    
          L,U       A1,64             . LOAD MAX CHAR 
          J         $+6               . JUMP    
          TE        A2,('CEA   ')     . IS IT ASCII ? 
          J         ILLEGAL           . ERROR IF NOT ASCII OR CFH 
          L,U       A1,4              . LOAD ASCII TRANS TYP
          S         A1,SEQTYP         . STORE FILE TYPE     
          L,U       A1,128            . LOAD  MAX ASCII CHAR
          S         A1,SEQMAX         . SET MAX TRANS POSS  
  SZ        SEQIDX   !  
SEQ1      LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT CHARACTER  
          J         SEQCK             . JUMP IF NONE  
          L         A1,GITV           . LOAD NEXT FIELD     
          TNE       A1,('UPTO  ')     . TEST IF UPTO  
          J         SEQ2              . JUMP IF UPTO  
          TNE       A1,('ALL   ')     . TEST IF ALL   
          J         SEQALL            . JUMP IF ALL   
          LMJ       X11,FDTOC         . GO STRIP FOR OCTAL  
          L         A3,SEQIDX         . LOAD INDEX POINTER  
          TNZ,H1    SEQTAB,A2         . TEST IF ALREADY     
          J         SEQERR1           . ERROR IF ALREADY    
          TG        A2,SEQMAX         . TEST IF TOO BIG     
          J         SEQERR1           . IF IF TOO BIG 
          SZ,H1     SEQTAB,A2         . ZERO VALUE    
          S,H2      A2,SEQTAB,A3      . STORE IN TABLE
          A,U       A3,1              . BUMP INDEX    
          S         A3,SEQIDX         . STORE IN INDEX
          TE        A3,SEQMAX         . TEST IF LIMIT 
          J         SEQ1              . NO CNTINUE TO LOOP  
          J         SEQCK             . LIMIT REACHED DO CHECK    
SEQ2      LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT FOR UPTO   
          J         SEQERR1           . ERROR IF NONE 
          L         A1,GITV           . LOAD FIELD    
          TNE       A1,('ALL   ')     . TEST IF ALL   
          J         SEQALL            . JUMP IF IT IS 
          LMJ       X11,FDTOC         . CONVER STRIP  
          L         A3,SEQIDX         . LOAD INDEX    
          AN,U      A3,1              . SUBTRACT ONE  
          L,H2      A1,SEQTAB,A3      . LOAD LAST VALUE     
          A,U       A3,1              . REST TO RIGHT POINTER     
SEQ3      A,U       A1,1              . BUMP ONE TO NEXT VALUE    
          TNZ,H1    SEQTAB,A1         . TEST IF EMPTY 
          J         SEQERR            . ERROR IF NOT ZERO   
          SZ,H1     SEQTAB,A1         . SET TO USED   
          S,H2      A1,SEQTAB,A3      . STORE VALUE   
          A,U       A3,1              . BUMP INDEX    
          S         A3,SEQIDX         . STORE NEW INDEX     
          TNE       A3,SEQMAX         . TEST IF LIMIT 
          J         SEQCK             . YES GO CHECK  
          TNE       A1,A2             . TST FOR EQUAL 
          J         SEQ3              . LOOP ON EQUAL 
          TLE       A1,A2             . TEST IF LIMIT REACHED     
          J         SEQ3              . NO CONTINUE LOOP    
          J         SEQ1              . YES BACK TO NORMAL  
SEQALL    L,U       A1,0              . RESET   
          L         A3,SEQIDX         . LOAD INDEX    
          TN        SEQTAB,A1         .FIRST WORD IN TABLE(00)USED?     
          J         $+4               . YES     
          SSL       A2,36             . NO DELETE NEG SIGNS 
          L,U       A2,0              . LOAD ZERO     
          J         SEQ5              . JUMP TO PUT IN TABLE
SEQ4      TZ,H1     SEQTAB,A1         . TES IF DONE YET     
          J         $+5               . NO GO MOVE VALUE    
          A,U       A1,1              . BUMP BY 1     
          TE        A1,SEQMAX         . HAS LIMIT BEEN REACH
          J         SEQ4              . NO CONTINUE LOOP    
          J         SEQCK             . YES DO FINAL CHECK  
          L,H1      A2,SEQTAB,A1      . LOAD VALUE    
SEQ5      SZ,H1     SEQTAB,A1         . SET TO ZERO   
          S,H2      A2,SEQTAB,A3      . SET IN TABLE  
          A,U       A3,1              . ADD 1 TO INDEX
          TNE       A3,SEQMAX         . TEST IF INDEX FULL  
          J         SEQCK             . YES GO DO CK  
          A,U       A1,1              . ADD ONE TO BUMPER   
          TE        A1,SEQMAX         . TEST FOR MAX  
          J         SEQ4              . JUMP LIMIT NOT READED     
SEQCK     L,U       A3,0              . RESET POINTER 
          TZ,H1     SEQTAB,A3         . TEST IF ZEROES
          J         SEQERR1           . NOT ZERO IS ERROR   
          A,U       A3,1              . ADD 1 TO BUMPER     
          TE        A3,SEQMAX         . HAS MAX BEEN REACHED
          J         SEQCK+1           . NO CONTINUE CHECK   
SEQDON    L         A2,SEQTYP         . LOAD TYPE     
          A,U       A2,3              . ADD TO MAKE 4 OR 7  
          LSSL      A2,30             . MOVE SO S1    
          LXM,U     A2,SEQTAB         . LOAD TABLE ADDRESS  
          L         X11,PARPNT        . LOAD SPT POINTER    
          S         A2,0,X11          . STORE IN SPT  
          A,U       X11,1               . UPDATE PARAM TBLE POINTER     
          S         X11,PARPNT          . 
          SZ        INDSEQ              . SET SEQUENCE IND OFF FOR GIT  
          J         NXTPARAM            . 
.     
. ERRORS    
SEQERR1   L         X3,('SEQNCE')       . SEQUENCE PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY   
          J         FIE1                . 
SEQERR2   L         A0,SEQMP            . SEQUENCE DATA MUST BE ON 1 CARD     
          J         ERROR     
/.    
.     
. THIS ROUTINE CONVERTS OCTAL IN FD TO OCTAL    
.     
.  ENTER VIA LMJ X11 EXIT 0,X11     
.  REGISTERS USED A0 A1 A2 A3A
FDTOC     L,U       A0,0              . INITIALIZE    
          L,U       A2,0              . INITIALIZE    
          L,U       A3,05             . LOAD FD SPACE 
CONFDA    TNZ       A1                . TEST IF END   
          J         ENDFDA            . FINISHED JUMP 
          S,S6      A1,FDAWD          . SET FOR SPACE CHECK 
          TNE,S6    A3,FDAWD          . TEST IF SPACE 
          J         $+5               . JUMP IF SPACE 
          DSL       A1,3              . MOVE DIGIT TO A3    
          SSL       A1,3              . SHIFT OFF HIGH  DIGIT     
          A,U       A0,3              . INCREMENT FOR BLANKS
          J         CONFDA            . LOOP FOR NEXT CHARACTER   
          A,U       A0,6              . INCREMENT INDEX     
          SSL       A1,6              . SHIFT OFF BLANK     
          J         CONFDA            . CONTINUE LOOP 
ENDFDA    SSL       A2,0,A0           .  RIGHT JUSTIFY
          J         0,X11             . RETURN TO CALLER    
/.    
. ********************************************************  
.     
OWN.  
.     
. HANDLES USER EXIT SPECIFICATIONS AND NAMES    
.     
NXTOWN    LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET EXIT NUMBER     
          J         OWNERR            . ERROR   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD NUMBER   
          S         A1,TEMP           .   
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET ROUTINE ENTRY POINT NAME    
          J         OWNERR            . ERROR   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD ENTRY POINT NAME     
          DS        A1,TEMP1          .   
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,OWNFLG         . SET OWN FLAG  
          L,U       A1,'T'            .   
          S         A1,OPSYSTEM       .   
          L         A1,TEMP           . LOAD EXIT NUMBER    
          LMJ       X6,VMOSMOD+2      . JUMP TO HANDLE EXIT 
          L         A1,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TE,U      A1,' '            . BLANK?  
          J         NXTOWN            . GET NEXT EXIT 
          SZ        OPSYSTEM          . RESTORE OP SYSTEM   
          SZ        RETBLANK          . SET RETURN SWITCH ZERO    
          TZ        USRCSW            . TEST IF SWITCH 01 SET     
          J         NXTPARAM-1        . NO CONTINUE PARAMETER PROCESSING
          L         A1,USRCSW+3       . LOAD ADDRESS USER 01 CODE 
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           . LMJ TO USER 01 CODE 
          J         NXTPARAM-1        . CONTINUE PARAMETER PROCESSING   
/.    
. ******************************************************************************    
.     
CHECK.
.     
. SPECIFIES THE TYPE OF HARDWARE FOR WHICH THE CHECKSUM OPTION IS TO    
. BE OMITTED.     
.      CHECK=D,F,T
.         WHERE D IS TO OMIT DRUM CHECKSUM
.               F IS TO OMIT FASTRAND DRUM CHECKSUM   
.               T IS TO OMIT TAPE CHECKSUM
          . S IS TO OMIT MULTIREEL SEQUENCE CHECK     
.      (OPTIONAL) 
.      (ANY ONE OR ANY COMBINATION MAY BE SPECIFIED)  
.     
          L,U       A2,'='              . 
          TE        A2,NGX              . IS 'CHECK' FOLLOWED BY EQUAL  
          J         CHCKERR1            . NO, ERROR   
CHECK1    LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET FIRST HARDWARE TYPE 
          J         CHCKERR1            . IF BLANK, ERROR   
          L         A1,GITV             . 
          SSL       A1,30               . 
          TE,U      A1,'D'              . TEST IF DRUM
          J         CHECK2              . 
          S,S2      A1,PARAM+5          . YES, SET TO OMIT DRUM CHECKSUM
          J         CHECK4              . 
CHECK2    TE,U      A1,'F'              . TEST IF FASTRAND  
          J         CHECK3              . 
          S,S4      A1,PARAM+5          . YES, SET TO OMIT FASTRAND CHECKSUM  
          J         CHECK4              . 
CHECK3    TE,U      A1,'T'              . TEST IF TAPE
          J         CHECK4A            . NO     
          S,S3      A1,PARAM+5          . YES, SET TO OMIT TAPE CHECKSUM
          J         CHECK4             .  
CHECK4A   TE,U      A1,'S'             . AN S   
          J         CHCKERR1           . NO     
          SZ        SEQCKS             .  
CHECK4    L         A2,NGX              . GET NEXT CHAR ON PARAM CARD   
          TE,U      A2,','              . IS IT A COMMA     
          J         NXTPARAM            . NO, GET NEXT ITEM 
          J         CHECK1              . YES, GET NEXT HARDWARE TYPE   
.     
. ERRORS    
CHCKERR1  L         X3,(' CHECK')       . CHECK PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY
          J         FIE1                . 
/.    
. **************************************************************************  
.     
VOLUME.     
.     
. SPECIFIES IF SMALL, MEDIUM OR LARGE SORT
.      VOLUME=X   
.         WHERE X IS 'SMALL', 'MEDIUM' OR 'LARGE'     
.      (OPTIONAL, IF OMITTED A SMALL VOLUME IS ASSUMED)     
.      (OVERIDDEN BY XA AND XB ASSIGN CARDS)    
.     
          L,U       A2,'='              . 
          TE        A2,NGX              . TEST IF 'VOLUME' IS FOLLOWED BY =   
          J         VOLERR1             . NO, ERROR   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET VOLUME SIZE   
          J         VOLERR1             . IF BLANK, ERROR   
          L         A1,GITV             . 
          L,U       A3,VOLSML           . 
          TNE       A1,SMALL            . TEST IF SMALL VOLUME    
          J         VOLUME3             . SMALL 
          TE        A1,MEDIUM           . TEST IFMMEDIUM VOLUME   
          J         VOLUME1             . NO    
          L         A1,(XAASGML,XAASGMM)  
          L         A2,(XBASGML,XBASGMM)  
          L,U       A3,VOLMED           . SET XA, XB AND SCRATCH CORE   
          J         VOLUME2             . FOR MEDIUM VOLUME 
VOLUME1   TE        A1,LARGE            . TEST IF LARGE VOLUME    
          J         VOLERR1             . NO, ERROR   
          L         A1,(XAASGLL,XAASGLM)  
          L         A2,(XBASGLL,XBASGLM)  
          L,U       A3,VOLLRG           . SET FOR LARGE VOLUME    
VOLUME2   S         A1,XAASGP           . 
          S         A2,XBASGP           . 
VOLUME3   S,H2      A3,PARAM+1          . NO, SET SCRATCH CORE SIZE     
          J         NXTPARAM            . 
.     
. ERRORS    
VOLERR1   L         X3,('VOLUME')       . VOLUME PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY     
          J         FIE1                . 
/.    
. ******************************************************************************    
.     
. SPECIFIES THE NUMBER OF DATA RECORDS TO SKIP IN THE INPUT FILE  
SKIP      . 
.     
.     
          LA,U      A2,'='             .  
          TE        A2,NGX             . TEST IF PARAMETER FOLLOWED BY AN EQUAL     
          J         SKIPERR            . IF BLANK , ERROR   
          LMJ       X11,GIT            . GET THE SKIP COUNT 
          J         SKIPERR            . NO , ERROR   
          DL        A1,GITV            .  
          LMJ       X11,DTO            . CONVERT SKIP COUNT TO OCTAL    
          J         SKIPERR            . ERROR IF NON-NUMERIC     
          SA        A4,SKIPSAVE        . SAVE SKIP COUNT    
          SZ        SKIPFLG            . CLEAR SKIP FLAG    
          J         NXTPARAM           . GET THE NEXT PARAMETER   
. ERRORS    
SKIPERR   LX        X3,('  SKIP')      . SKIP PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY  
          J         FIE1               .  
.     
/.    
. ******************************************************************************    
.     
.     
NUMREC    . 
.     
. SPECIFIES THE NUMBER OF RECORDS TO SORT IN THE INPUT FILE 
.     
          LA,U      A2,'='             .  
          TE        A2,NGX             . TEST IF PARAMETER FOLLOWED BY AN EQUAL     
          J         NUMRECERR          . NO , ERROR   
          LMJ       X11,GIT            . GET THE NUMBER OF RECORDS TO SORT    
          J         NUMRECERR          . ERROR IF BLANK     
          DL        A1,GITV            .  
          LMJ       X11,DTO            . CONVERT THE NUMBER TO OCTAL    
          J         NUMRECERR          . ERROR IF NON-NUMERIC     
          SA        A4,RECSAVE         . SAVE THE NUMBER    
          SZ        NUMFLG             . CLEAR THE FLAG     
          J         NXTPARAM           . GET THE NEXT PARAMETER   
NUMRECERR . 
          LX        X3,('NUMREC')      . NUMREC PARAMETER USED ILLEGALLY
          J         FIE1               .  
.     
.     
.     
. *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *  *   
. THIS CODE HANDLES MOVE PARAMETER  
.  MOVE=N   N= NUMBER TAPEMARKS TO MOVE   
.     
MOVE      LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NUM TAPEMARKS   
          J         ILLEGAL           . ERROR IF NONE 
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD NUMBER   
          LMJ       X11,DTO           . CONVERT TO OCTAL    
          J         DTOERR            . NON NUMERIC IS ERROR
          S         A4,MRKCTRVAL      . STORE NUMBER TAPE MARKS   
          SZ        INPOPENSW         . SET TO NO REWIND    
          J         NXTPARAM          . JUMPT TO NEXT PARAMETER   
/.    
.     
. **********************************************************
. THE FOLLOWING CODING IS EXECUTED WHEN AN END CONVERT
. PARAMETER CARD IS ENCOUNTERED.    
. THE CODING STORES BYTE INFORMATION FOR TAG LIST SORTS     
. AND LOADS INPUT AND OUTPUT FILE NAMES.  
END.  
          L         A0,CONVERTWORD      . LOAD TYPE OF SORT 
          TNZ       A0                  . TAG OR SELECT SORT?     
          J         FILELOAD            . NO, LOAD FILE NAMES     
          L         A1,FILTYP           . LOAD FILE TYPE    
          L,U       A2,1                . 
          TE        A1,A2               . CFH FILE?   
          J         TAGSELCONT          . NO CONTINUE 
          S         X1,TEMP1            . SAVE REGISTERS    
          S         X2,TEMP2            . 
         S         X3,TEMP3            .  
         S         X4,TEMP4            .  
         LXI,U     X1,1                . INITIALIZE REGISTERS     
         LXI,U     X2,1                .  
         LXI,U     X3,1                .  
         LXI,U     X4,1                .  
          LXM,U      X1,TAGLD             . LOAD DESTINATION LOAD 
          LXM,U    X2,TAGST            . LOAD DESTINATION STORE   
            LXM,U      X3,LDINST(1) 
            LXM,U      X4,STINST(1) 
         L,U       R1,4                .  
        BT       X1,0,*X3     
        L,U      R1,4   
        BT       X2,0,*X4     
         L         X1,TEMP1            .  
         L         X2,TEMP2            . RELOAD REGISTERS   
         L         X3,TEMP3            .  
         L         X4,TEMP4            .  
TAGSELCONT. 
          L         A1,FLDLENG          . LOAD FIELD LENGTH 
          L         A0,CONVERTWORD      . LOAD TYPE OF SORT 
          L,U       A2,2                . 
          TNE       A0,A2               . TAG LIST SORT?    
          A,U       A1,1              . YES, ADD ONE WORD FOR ADDRESS   
          S         A1,FLDLENG        .   
          S         A1,OUTLENG          . STORE OUTPUT RECORD LENGTH    
          L         A0,ORDERCNT2        . LOAD NUMBER OF FIELDS   
          L         A1,(1,0)            . 
BYTECONT  AN,U      A0,1                . DECREMENT INDEX   
          L         A3,TEMPBWORD,A0     . LOAD LAST FIELD POSITION
          SZ        A2                  . 
          L         A4,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
         DI        A2,A4               . GET BYTES/WORD     
          A,U       A3,1                . 
          S         A3,QBYTE,*A1        . 
          TZ        A0                  . 
          J         BYTECONT            . CONTINUE    
          L         A1,(1,0)            . 
          L         A0,ORDERCNT2        . LOAD NUMBER OF FIELDS   
NBYTELOOP AN,U      A0,1                . DECREMENT INDEX   
          L         A2,TEMPNBYTE,A0     . LOAD FIELD LENGTH 
          AN,U      A2,1              .   
          S         A2,NBYTE,*A1        . STORE NBYTE 
          TZ        A0                  . 
          J         NBYTELOOP           . CONTINUE    
          L         A0,ORDERCNT2      .   
          SZ        TEMP1             . CLEAR FOR CALCULATIONS    
          AN,U      A0,1              .   
          S         A0,R1     
          L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          L         A4,TYPECONST1   
BWORD1    L         A2,TEMPBWORD,A0   .   
          L         A3,TEMPBWORD,A0   .   
          SZ        A1                .   
          DI        A1,A4             .   
          L         A2,TEMP1          .   
          AN        A1,A2     
          S         A1,TEMPBWORD,A0 
BWORD3    L         A2,TEMPNBYTE,*A0
          A         A3,A2     
          AN,U      A3,1
          SZ        A2                .   
          DI        A2,A4             .   
          S         A2,TEMP1          .   
          JGD       R1,BWORD1         .   
BWORD4    L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          L         A1,ORDERCNT2    
BWORD5    AN,U      A1,1
          L         A2,TEMPBWORD,A1   .   
          S         A2,BWORD,*A0      .   
          TZ        A1                  . 
          J         BWORD5            .   
FILELOAD  L,U       X2,INPKT            . LOAD INPUT PACKET ADDRESS     
          TNZ       OUTLENG           .   
          J         $+4               .   
          L         A1,OUTLENG        .   
          S         A1,FLDLENG        .   
          J         $+5               .   
          TZ        CONVERTWORD       .   
          J         $+3               .   
          L         A1,RECSIZ         .   
          S         A1,FLDLENG        .   
          L         A1,ORDERCNT2      .   
          AN,U      A1,1              .   
          S         A1,ORDERCNT2      .STORE ADJUSTED NO. OF FIELDS     
          DL        A1,TEMPSPOT         . LOAD FIRST INPUT FILE   
          L         A0,INPFILFLG        . INPUT FILE NAME GIVEN?  
          TNZ       A0                  . 
          J         OUTFILENAME         . NO    
          DS        A1,0,X2             . YES, STORE FILE NAME    
          DS        A1,FACPKT           . 
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . 
          ER        FACIL$              . TEST IF FILE IS FASTRAND
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          . IS  FILE ASSIGNED ? 
          J         FIE3              . NO JUMP TO ERR
          L,S1      A0,FACPKT+6         . 
          TG,U      A0,020              . 
          S         A0,INPKT+8          . 
          TLE,U     A0,030            .   
          S         A0,TAPEDRUM       .   
          TG,U      A0,020            . FASTRAND FILE?
          S         A0,FASTFILEIN     . YES, SET FLAG 
          TZ        FASTFILEIN        . TAPE OR MASS STORAGE ?    
          J         $+3               . JUMP MASS STORAGE   
          L,S1      A2,FACPKT+8       . LOAD NUMBER REELS   
          S,H1      A2,INPKT+6        . STORE NUMBER REELS  
OUTFILENAME L,U     X2,OUTPKT           . 
          DL        A1,OUTNAMESAVE      . LOAD OUTPUT FILE NAME   
          L         A0,OUTFILFLG        . OUTPUT FILE NAME GIVEN? 
          TNZ       A0                  . 
          J         NXTPARAM-1          . NO    
          DS        A1,0,X2             . STORE FILE NAME   
          DS        A1,FACPKT           . STORE FILE NAME INTO FACPKT   
          L,U       A0,FACPKT           . LOAD PACKET ADDRESS     
          ER        FACIL$              . 
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          . IS FILE ASSIGNED ?  
          J         FIE3              . NO JUMP TO ERR
          L,S1      A0,FACPKT+6         . LOAD EQUIPMENT CODE     
          TG,U      A0,020            . FASTRAND OR DRUM FILE?    
          S         A0,FASTFILEOUT    . STORE MASS STOR INDICATOR 
          TZ        FASTFILEOUT       . TAPE OR MASS STORAGE
          J         NXTPARAM-1           . GET NEXT CARD    
          L,S1      A2,FACPKT+8       . LOAD NUMBER REELS   
          S,H1      A2,OUTPKT+6       . STORE NUMBER REELS FOR OUTPUT   
          J         NXTPARAM-1        . JUMP DO NEXT CARD   
/.    
. **********************************************************************
CONVERT.    
.     
. STARTING POINT FOR CONVERSION FROM SERIES 70 TO SORT/MERGE 1100 
. ORIENTED PARAMETERS   
.     
          TE        A2,('T     ')       . VALID CONVERT STATEMENT?
          J         ILLEGAL             . NO, SO ERROR
          L,U       A2,' '              . 
          TE        A2,NGX              . COMPARE CHARACTER TO BLANK    
          J         ILLEGAL             . NO SO FORMAT IS WRONG   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET THE REST OF CONVERT STMT  
          J         CNVERR            .   
          DL        A1,GITV             . PUT REST OF CONVERT STMT IN A1
CVRTEST   S         A1,SAVEVAL          . STORE A1    
          TE,S1     A0,SAVEVAL          . TEST FOR BLANK    
          J         CVRTLTRS            . CHECK OUT REMAINDER OF STMT   
          LDSC      A1,6                . SHIFT A1 & A2 1 DIGIT   
          J         CVRTEST             . TEST NEXT BYTE    
CVRTLTRS  L,U       A0,'R'              . TEST TO SEE IF RCA SORT IS    
          S         A1,SAVEVAL           . SET A1 FOR TEST  
          TE,S1     A0,SAVEVAL          . BEING HANDLED     
          J         CNVERR            .   
          LDSC      A1,6                . CHECK FOR VALID OPERATIN G    
          L,U       A0,'D'              .      SYSTEM - ONLY T, D, OR   
          S         A1,SAVEVAL          .      V QUALIFIES FOR RCA
          TE,S1     A0,SAVEVAL          . 
          J         NOTDOS              . NOT DOS - CHECK VMOS & TDOS   
          S         A0,OPSYSTEM         . IT IS DOS SO STORE A D IN     
          J         GETNEXTONE          .     OPSYSTEM, READ NEXT CARD  
NOTDOS    L,U       A0,'T'              . SET A0 TO T FOR TDOS    
          S         A1,SAVEVAL          . 
          TE,S1     A0,SAVEVAL          . CHECK FOR TDOS INDICATOR
          J         NOTTDOS             . NOT DOS OR TDOS   
          S         A0,OPSYSTEM         . IT IS TDOS SO STORE A T IN    
          J         GETNEXTONE          .      OPSYSTEM, READ NEXT CARD 
NOTTDOS   L,U       A0,'V'              . SET A0 TO V FOR VMOS    
          S         A1,SAVEVAL          . SET A1 FOR TEST   
          TE,S1     A0,SAVEVAL          . CHECK FOR VMOS INDICATOR
          J         CNVERR            .   
          S         A0,OPSYSTEM         . IT IS VMOS STORE T IN OPSYSTEM
.     
. AT THIS POINT, THE OPERATING SYSTEM HAS BEEN DETERMINED AND STORED    
. IN THE LOCATION OPSYSTEM    
.     
GETNEXTONE. 
          LMJ       X11,CARDIN          . READ SERIES 70 PARAMETER CARD 
          SZ        A0                  . 
          S         A0,RETBLANK         . RESET FLAG FOR START OF IMAGE 
NEWITEM   LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET A PARAMETER FROM THE CARD 
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          L,U       A0,1                . 
          S         A0,RETBLANK         . SET FLAG FOR START OF IMAGE   
          DL        A1,GITV             . PUT PARAMETER IN A1 & A2
          L,U       R1,NORCACRDS        . COUNTER OF POSSIBLE PARAMETERS
          L         A0,(1,0)            . SET COUNTER IN A0 
          SE        A1,RCACARDS,*A0     . SEARCH FOR VALID ENTRY  
          J         ILLEGAL           .   
          J         *RCAJUMP-1,A0       . GO HANDLE STATEMENT     
$(0). 
RCACARDS.   
          'SORT  '                      . VALID RCA PARAMETERS    
          'MERGE '                      . 
          'RECORD'                      . 
          'INPFIL'                      . 
          'OUTFIL'                      . 
          'WORKFI'                      . 
          'MODS  '                      . 
          'OPTION'                      . 
          'ALLOC '                      . 
          'END   '  .   
NORCACRDS EQU       $-RCACARDS          . 
$(1). 
RCAJUMP.    
          +         RCASORT             . 
          +         RCAMERGE            . 
          +         RCARECORD           . 
          +         RCAINPFIL           . 
          +         RCAOUTFIL           . 
          +         RCAWORKFIL          . 
          +         RCAMODS             . 
          +         RCAOPTION           . 
          +         RCAALLOC            . 
          +         RCAENDCVRT          . 
/.    
. **********************************************************************
.     
. RCA SORT COMMANDS WILL START BEING HANDLED AT THIS POINT. 
. RCA MERGE COMMANDS WILL GO FIRST TO RCAMERGE TO SET 
. SWITCHES AND THEN THRU THIS POINT.
.     
. THE PARAGRAPHS THRU KEYEND WILL BE USED TO HANDLE THE     
. KEY FIELDS.  GETSORT THRU SORTSIZE WILL HANDLE THE  
. OPTIONS.  
.     
. *******************************************************************   
RCASORT.    
          SZ        REQKEY              . REQUIRED PARAMETER
          SZ        A0                  . 
          S         A0,KEEPFORM     
          L,U       A0,1                . 
          S         A0,ORDERCNT         . INITIALIZE ORDERCNT     
          SZ        ORDERCNT2           . INITIALIZE ORDERCNT2    
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,' '              . BLANK?
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          J         LISTCON4            . 
ENDCK1    L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,' '              . BLANK?
          J         REPROCESS           . YES, REREAD SORT CARD   
LISTCON4  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD WORD   
          L         A2,('FORMAT')        .
          TE        A1,A2                . FORMAT= ?  
          J         ENDCK1              . NO FIND     
          J         SORTFORMAT          . GO PROCESS FORMAT CARD  
SORTFORMAT. 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET FORMAT TYPE   
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD TYPE   
          S         A1,KEEPFORM         . KEEP FORMAT TYPE  
REPROCESS L,U       A0,CRDBUF           . LOAD ADDRESS OF CARD BUFFER   
          SZ        FORMSW            .   
          S,H2      A0,INIMGE           . REINITIALIZE GIT ROUTINE
          SZ,H2     CHRPOS              . 
          SZ        A0                  . 
          S         A0,RETBLANK         . RESET IMAGE START FLAG  
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET SORT    
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          L,U       A0,1                . 
          S         A0,RETBLANK         . SET IMAGE START FLAG    
          J         LISTCON5            . 
KEYEND    A,U       X1,2              .INCR POINTER TO NEXT TABLE 
          S         X1,PARPNT         .STORE POINTER  
          L         A2,FLDLENG          . LOAD FIELD LENGTH 
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A4,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A1,A4               . CALCULATE LENGTH IN WORDS     
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . ADD ONE WORD
          S         A1,FLDLENG          . STORE FIELD LENGTH IN WORDS   
CONTROLOOP. 
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,' '            .BLANK    
          J         SELECTFIX           . 
LISTCON5  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          L,U       R1,NOSORTCRDS       . LOAD NUMBER OF SORT IDS 
          L         A0,(1,0)            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . 
          SE        A1,RCASORTCRDS,*A0  . SORT VALID IDS    
          J         CONTROLOOP          . INVALID ID  
          J         *SORTCRDS-1,A0      . HANDLE ID   
$(0). 
RCASORTCRDS.
          'FIELDS'                      . 
          'SIZE'                        . 
          'SKIP'                        . 
          'OPT'                         . 
          'TYPE'                        . 
NOSORTCRDS EQU      $-RCASORTCRDS       . 
$(1). 
SORTCRDS.   
          +         SORTFIELDS          . 
          +         SORTSIZE            . 
          +         SORTSKIP            . 
          +         SORTOPT             . 
          +         SORTTYPE            . 
SORTFIELDS. 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET LEFT PAREN    
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          L         X1,PARPNT           . LOAD PARAMETER TABLE POINTER  
          S         X1,POINTERKEEP      . STORE POINTER     
KEYSTART  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET KEY FIELD POSITION  
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD POSITION     
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT TO OCTAL  
          J         KEYE2               . 
KEYSTART2 L,U       A3,3                . 
          S,S1      A3,0,X1             . STORE TYPE OF ENTRY     
          L         A3,ORDERCNT         . LOAD ORDERCNT     
          S,S6      A3,0,X1           .   
          A,U       A3,1                . INCREMENT KEY COUNTER   
          S         A3,ORDERCNT         . 
          L         A3,ORDERCNT2        . 
          A,U       A3,1                . 
          S         A3,ORDERCNT2        . 
          AN,U      A4,1                . CORRECT VALUE OF POSITION     
          SSL       A3,36               . 
          L         A0,TEMPKEEP         . 
          S         A4,TEMPBWORD,A0     . 
          L         A2,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A3,A2               . CONVERT TO UNIVAC WORDS 
          S,H1      A3,1,X1             . STORE # OF WORDS  
          L         A2,TYPECONST2       . LOAD BITS/CHAR    
          MSI       A4,A2               . CALCULATE STARTING BIT  
          A,U       A4,1                . 
          S,S4      A4,0,X1             . STORE INTO TABLE  
.     
. GET LENGTH OF KEY FIELD     
.     
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET LENGTH OF FIELD     
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD LENGTH 
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT TO OCTAL  
          J         KEYE2               . 
          L         A0,FLDLENG          . LOAD FIELD LENGTH 
          A         A0,A4               . ADD NEXT FIELD LENGTH   
          S         A0,FLDLENG          . 
          L         A0,TEMPKEEP         . 
          S         A4,TEMPNBYTE,*A0    . 
          S         A0,TEMPKEEP         . 
          L         A2,TYPECONST2       . LOAD BITS/CHAR    
          MSI       A4,A2               . GET LENGTH IN BITS
          S,H2      A4,1,X1             . PUT INTO TABLE    
          L,U       A4,077              . 
          S,S1      A4,1,X1             . STORE TYPE OF ENTRY     
          L         A1,OPSYSTEM         . LOAD TYPE OF OPERATING SYSTEM 
          TNE,U     A1,'T'              . TDOS? 
          J         TDOSKEY             . YES   
          TNE,U     A1,'V'              . VMOX? 
          J         VMOSKEY             . YES   
DOSKEY.     
. THE FOLLOWING HANDLES PRIORITY FIELD    
. FIELD IS REDUNDANT - ONLY INFORMATION IS
. N INDICATING A NON SORT FIELD     
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET PRIORITY
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PRIORITY     
          L         A2,('N     ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . NON SORT FIELD?   
          J         SORTIT              . A SORT FIELD
          L         A1,ORDERCNT         . A NON SORT FIELD  
          AN,U      A1,1                . DECREMENT ORDER COUNT   
          S         A1,ORDERCNT         . STORE ORDERCNT    
TRYAGAIN  L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT PARAMETER     
          TE,U      A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         TRYCONT1            . 
          SZ        0,X1                . 
          SZ        1,X1                . 
          J         KEYEND+2            . 
TRYCONT1  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD NEXT WORD    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT TO OCTAL  
          J         TRYAGAIN            . NON NUMERIC, NOT KEY FIELD YET
          J         KEYSTART2           . START NEXT KEY FIELD    
SORTIT    LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET SEQUENCE PARAMETER  
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD SEQUENCE     
          LSSC      A1,6                . RIGHT JUSTIFY SEQUENCE  
          S,S3      A1,0,X1             . STORE INTO TABLE  
          L         A1,KEEPFORM         . LOAD FORMAT= FLAG 
          TNZ       A1                  . WAS FORMAT= GIVEN?
          J         GETFORMAT           . NO, GET FORMAT    
          S         A1,GITV             . 
          LMJ       X11,KEYFORM         . HANDLE FORMAT TYPE
          J         KEYFORMERR          . 
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         KEYEND    
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . IGNORE FORMAT FIELD IF PRESENT
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         KEYEND+2            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          A,U       X1,2                . INCREMENT POINTER 
          S         X1,PARPNT         .STORE POINTER  
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . 
          J         KEYSTART            . 
          J         KEYSTART2           . 
GETFORMAT.  
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET FORMAT TYPE   
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          LMJ       X11,KEYFORM         . 
          J         KEYFORMERR          . 
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         KEYEND    
          A,U       X1,2                . INCREMENT POINTER 
          S         X1,PARPNT 
          J         KEYSTART            . 
TDOSKEY   L         A1,KEEPFORM         . WAS FORMAT= GIVEN?
          TNZ       A1                  . 
          J         GETFORM2            . NO    
          S         A1,GITV             . YES, STORE TYPE INTO GITV     
          LMJ       X11,KEYFORM         . 
          J         KEYFORMERR          . 
TRY2      LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD WORD   
          L         A2,('A     ')       . 
          L         A0,('D     ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . ASCENDING?  
          TNE       A1,A0               . DECENDING?  
          J         STORESEQ            . STORE SEQUENCE    
          L         A2,('E     ')        .
          TE        A1,A2                . USER EXIT? 
          J         TRY2                . 
STORESEQ  LSSC      A1,6                . RIGHT JUSTIFY SEQUENCE  
          S,S3      A1,0,X1             . STORE SEQUENCE    
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         KEYEND    
          A,U       X1,2                . INCREMENT POINTER 
          S         X1,PARPNT         .STORE POINTER  
          J         KEYSTART            . 
GETFORM2  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET FORMAT TYPE   
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          LMJ       X11,KEYFORM         . HANDLE FORMAT TYPE
          J         KEYFORMERR          . 
          J         TRY2                . GET SEQUENCE PARAMETER  
VMOSKEY   LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET SEQUENCE PARAMETER  
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          L         A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('A     ')       . 
          L         A0,('D     ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . ASCENDING?  
          TNE       A1,A0               . DESCENDING? 
          J         STOREVSEQ           . 
          L         A2,('E     ')       . USER EXIT?  
          TNE       A1,A2               . 
          J         STOREVSEQ           . 
          L         A1,ORDERCNT         . OTHERWISE, NON SORT FIELD     
          AN,U      A1,1                . 
          S         A1,ORDERCNT         . DECREMENT ORDER COUNT   
TRY3      L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         TRYCONT2            . 
          SZ        0,X1                . 
          SZ        1,X1                . 
          J         KEYEND+2            . 
TRYCONT2  LMJ       X11,GIT             . 
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . GET START OF KEY TO OCTAL     
          J         TRY3                . FORMAT FIELD - IGNORE   
          J         KEYSTART2           . 
STOREVSEQ LSSC      A1,6                . RIGHT JUSTIFY SEQUENCE  
          S,S3      A1,0,X1             . STORE SEQUENCE    
          L         A1,KEEPFORM         . LOAD FORMAT= FLAG 
          TNZ       A1                  . WAS FORMAT= GIVEN 
          J         GETFORM3            . NO, GET FORMAT    
          S         A1,GITV             . YES   
          LMJ       X11,KEYFORM         . HANDLE FORMAT TYPE
          J         KEYFORMERR          . 
TRY4      L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         KEYEND    
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD WORD   
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT TO OCTAL  
          J         TRY4                . 
          A,U       X1,2                . INCREMENT PARAMETER TABLE POINTER   
          S         X1,PARPNT         .STORE POINTER  
          J         KEYSTART2           . 
GETFORM3  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET FORMAT TYPE   
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          LMJ       X11,KEYFORM         . HANDLE FORMAT TYPE
          J         KEYFORMERR          . 
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         KEYEND    
          A,U       X1,2                . INCREMENT PARAMETER TABLE POINTER   
          S         X1,PARPNT         .   
          J         KEYSTART            . 
SORTSIZE.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET SIZE    
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD SIZE   
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT TO OCTAL  
          J         DTOERR              . 
          SSL       A3,36               . 
          DI,U      A3,1000             . 
          L         X1,PARPNT           . LOAD PARAMETER TABLE POINTER  
          L,U       A6,064              . 
          S         A3,0,X1             . STORE SIZE  
          S,S1      A6,0,X1             . STORE TYPE OF ENTRY     
          A,U       X1,1              .INCREMENT POINTER    
          S         X1,PARPNT         .STORE POINTER  
          J         CONTROLOOP          . 
SORTSKIP.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET SKIP NUMBER   
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD NUMBER 
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT TO OCTAL  
          J         DTOERR              . 
          S         A4,SKIPSAVE         . STORE NUMBER TO SKIP    
          SZ        SKIPFLG             . RESET FLAG  
          J         CONTROLOOP          . 
SORTOPT.    
SORTTYPE.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET TYPE OF SORT  
          J         SORTERR1            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD TYPE OF SORT 
          TE        A1,('V     ')       . RECORD SORT?
          TNE       A1,('REC   ')       . 
          J         CONTROLOOP          . 
          TE        A1,('A     ')       . SELECT SORT?
          TNE       A1,('SELECT')     . SELECT SORT ? 
          J         RECSELTYP           . 
          L,U       A1,2                . 
          S         A1,CONVERTWORD      . STORE FOR TAG SORT
          J         CONTROLOOP          . 
RECSELTYP L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,CONVERTWORD      . STORE FOR SELECT SORT   
          J         CONTROLOOP          . 
.     
. THE FOLLOWING HANDLES THE FORMAT TYPE INFO    
.     
KEYFORM.    
          L,U       A1,'B'            .   
          TE,S1     A1,GITV           . BINARY? 
          J         TESTCHAR          . NO
          L,U       A1,'U'            .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
TESTCHAR  L,U       A1,'C'            .   
          TE,S1     A1,GITV           . CHARACTER?    
          J         TESTPACK          . NO
          TNZ       ANSFLG            . ASCII FILES?  
          J         $+3               . NO
          L,U       A1,'S'            . YES, LOAD S KEY TYPE
          J         KEYFIX            .   
          L,U       A1,'A'            .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
TESTPACK  L,U       A1,'P'            .   
          TE,S1     A1,GITV           . PACKED? 
          J         TESTZONE          . NO
          L,U       A1,'U'            .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
TESTZONE  L,U       A1,'Z'            .   
          TE,S1     A1,GITV           . ZONED?  
          J         TESTINTG          . NO
          L,U       A1,'A'            .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
TESTINTG  L,U       A1,'F'            .   
          TE,S1     A1,GITV           . FLOATING OR FIXED?  
          J         TSTQRS    
          L,U       A1,'I'            .   
          TE,S2     A1,GITV           . FIXED POINT?  
          J         TESTFLOAT         . NO
          L,U       A1,'B'            .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
TESTFLOAT L,U       A1,'L'            .   
          TE,S2     A1,GITV           . FLOATING POINT?     
          J         0,X11             . NO, ERROR     
          L,S4      A1,0,X1           . LOAD STARTING BIT   
          TE,U      A1,1              . FIRST BIT?    
          J         FLOATERR          . NO, ERROR     
          L,H2      A1,1,X1           . LOAD LENGTH IN BITS 
          SZ        A0                .   
          L         A2,TYPECONST2     . LOAD BITS/CHAR
          DI        A0,A2             . CALCULATE CHARACTERS
          L         A1,A0             .   
          SZ        A0                .   
          L         A2,TYPECONST1     . LOAD CHAR/WORD
          DI        A0,A2             .   
          TZ        A1                .   
          J         FLOATERR2         . NOT EVEN DIVISION, ERROR  
          L,U       A1,'U'            .   
KEYFIX    S,S2      A1,0,X1           . STORE INTO TABLE    
          J         1,X11             . RETURN  
TSTQRS    L,U       A1,'Q'            .   
          TNE,S1    A1,GITV           .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
          L,U       A1,'R'            .   
          TNE,S1    A1,GITV           .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
          L,U       A1,'S'            .   
          TNE,S1    A1,GITV           .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
          L,U       A1,'M'            .   
          TNE,S1    A1,GITV           .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
          L,U       A1,'D'            .   
          TNE,S1    A1,GITV           .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
          L,U       A1,'I'            .   
          TE,S1     A1,GITV           .   
          J         $+3               .   
          L,U       A1,'B'            .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
          L,U       A1,'L'            .   
          TE,S1     A1,GITV           .   
          J         0,X11             .   
          L,U       A1,'U'            .   
          J         KEYFIX            .   
. CORRECTS PARAMETER TABLE ENTRIES IF TAG OR  SELECT SORT   
SELECTFIX.  
          L         A0,CONVERTWORD      . LOAD SORT TYPE    
          TNZ       A0                  . TAG OR SELECT?    
          J         GETNEXTONE          . 
          L         X1,POINTERKEEP      . LOAD POINTER
          L         A0,ORDERCNT2        . NUMBER OF FIELDS  
          L,H2      A2,1,X1             . LOAD FIELD LENGTH 
          S         A2,TOTLNG           . TOTAL LENGTH
          L         A4,CONVERTWORD      . LOAD SORT TYPE    
          TE,U      A4,2                . TAG LIST SORT?    
          J         SELECT1             . NO
