          L         A4,TOTLNG           . 
          A,U       A4,36               . ADD ONE WORD
          S         A4,TOTLNG           . 
SELECT1   L,U       A1,1                . 
          S,S4      A1,0,X1             . STORE STARTING BIT
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A4,CONVERTWORD      . LOAD SORT TYPE    
          TE,U      A4,2                . TAG LIST SORT?    
          J         SELECT2             . NO    
          L,U       A1,1                . 
SELECT2   S,S3      A1,1,X1             . STORE STARTING WORD     
FIXCONT   A,U       A2,1                . 
          SZ        A1                .   
          DI,U      A1,36               . CONVERT TO WORDS  
          AN,U      A0,1                . DECREMENT ORDERCNT2     
          TNZ       A0                  . 
          J         GETNEXTONE          . 
          A,U       X1,2                . INCREMENT POINTER 
          S,H1      A1,1,X1             . STORE WORD POSITION     
          L,U       A1,077              . 
          S,S1      A1,1,X1             . 
          S,S4      A2,0,X1             . STORE BIT POSITION
          L,H2      A2,1,X1             . LOAD FIELD LENGTH 
          L         A1,TOTLNG           . 
          A         A2,A1               . ADD LENGTHS 
          S         A2,TOTLNG           . 
          J         FIXCONT             . REPEAT LOOP 
/.    
.     
. *******************************************************************   
RCAMERGE.   
        L,U       A1,1                . PUT 1 IN A1   
        S         A1,SRTMRGTYP        . STORE MERGE INDICATOR IN SWITCH 
          L         A10,(' MERGE')      . LOAD INDICATOR FOR GSM  
        J         RCASORT             . GO HANDLE MERGE PARAM WITH SORT 
/.    
.     
. *******************************************************************   
RCARECORD.  
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,' '              . BLANK?
          J         RECERR1             . NO, RECORD ERROR  
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         RECERR1             . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET LEFT PAREN    
          J         RECERR1             . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A0,('      ')       . 
          TE        A0,A1               . BLANK FIELD?
          J         RECRECORD           . 
          L         A0,(1,0)            . 
.     
. THIS LOOP READS RECORD SIZES IN THE FOLLOWING ORDER:
.  RECSIZ,SORTLENG,OUTLENG,VMINLENG,VAVELENG.   
.     
RECLOOP.    
          S         A0,KEPSAFE          . SAVE A0 CONTENTS  
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET RECORD SIZE PARAMETER     
          J         RECERR1             . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD RECSIZ PARAMETER   
           LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT TO OCTAL 
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          L         A0,KEPSAFE          . RESTORE A0 CONTENTS     
          L         A2,A4               . 
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A4,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A1,A4               . DIVIDE BY CHAR/WORD     
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . NO, ADD ONE MORE WORD   
          L,U       A2,1                . 
          S         A2,RCZSW            . SET RECORD FLAG   
          S         A1,RECSIZ,*A0       . STORE RECORD SIZES
          L         A2,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A2,')'            .RIGHT PAREN    
          J         RECLOOP           .NO, CONTINUE GETTING LENGTHS     
          L         A2,VMINLENG       .LOAD MIN LENGTH
          TNZ       A2                .VAR LENG RECORDS     
          J         RECTYP            .NO,FIXED LENG  
          L         A1,PARPNT         . LOAD SPT INDEX
          S         A2,0,A1           . STORE LINKSZ IN SPT 
          L,U       A2,2              . LOAD LINKSZ INDICATOR     
          S,S1      A2,0,A1           . STORE VAR INDICATOR IN SPT
          A,U       A1,1              . BUMP INDEX    
          S         A1,PARPNT         . STORE NEW INDEX     
          SZ        A2                . VAR FLG IS ZERO     
          S         A2,VARFLG         .SET VAR REC FLAG     
          J         RECTYP    
RECRECORD.  
          S         A0,JMPFORTYP    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT @ TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          L         A2,A4               . 
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A4,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A1,A4               . DIVIDE BY CHAR/WORD     
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . NO, ADD ONE MORE WORD   
          S         A1,RECSIZ           . STORE RECORD SIZE 
RECTYP.     
.     
. PUT RECORD SIZE INTO PARAMETER TABLE    
.     
          L         A2,RECSIZ           . LOAD RECORD SIZE  
          L         A1,CONVERTWORD    .LOAD SORT TYPE 
          TNZ       A1                .TAG OR SELECT SORT?  
          J         RECCONT1          .NO 
          L         A2,FLDLENG        .LOAD FIELD LENGTH    
          TNE,U     A1,2
          A,U       A2,1              .ADD 1 IF TAG SORT    
RECCONT1.   
          TNZ       SORTLENG          .   
          J         $+2               .   
          L         A2,SORTLENG       .   
          L         A1,PARPNT           . LOAD PARAMETER TABLE POINTER  
          S         A2,0,A1             . PUT DECIMAL VALUE IN TABLE    
          L,U       A2,1                . 
          S,S1      A2,0,A1             . STORE ENTRY TYPE IN TABLE     
          A,U       A1,1                . INCREMENT POINTER 
          S         A1,PARPNT           . 
          SZ        REQRSZ              . REQUIRED PARAMETER GIVEN
          TZ        SORTLENG            . SORTLENG GIVEN?   
          J         RECCONT             . YES   
          L         A1,RECSIZ           . NO, LOAD RECSIZ   
          S         A1,SORTLENG         . STORE RECSIZ IN SORTLENG
.     
. GET RECORD TYPE 
.     
RECCONT   L         A1,OPSYSTEM         . TYPE OF OP SYSTEM 
          TNE,U     A1,'T'              . TDOS? 
          J         GETNEXTONE          . GET NEXT PARAMETER CARD 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET COMMA   
          J         RECERR1             . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          L         A1,JMPFORTYP    
          TZ       A1   
          J         TYPJMP    
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         RECERR1             . ERROR - END OF CARD     
TYPJMP    LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET RECTYPE 
          J         RECERR1             . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD RECORD TYPE  
          S         A1,RECTYPE          . STORE RECTYPE     
          J         GETNEXTONE          . GET NEXT PARAMETER CARD 
/.    
$(0). 
RCAINPCRDS .
          'INPUT '                      . 
          'BLKSIZ'                      . 
          'OPEN  '                      . 
          'CLOSE '                      . 
          'LABEL '                      . 
          'BYPASS'                      . 
          'FILES '                      . 
          'VOLUME'                      . 
          'NOKEY '                      . 
          'CHANGE'                      . 
NOINPCRDS  EQU      $-RCAINPCRDS        . NUMBER OF INPUT COMMANDS
. *******************************************************************   
. THE FOLLOWING CONVERTS THE INPFIL PARAMETER CARD    
.     
$(1). 
RCAINPFIL . 
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,' '              . BLANK?
          J         INPERR              . NO, INPFIL ERROR  
          SZ        RETBLANK          . SET TO NO IMAGE CNT 
          J         LISTCON1             . SKIP END OF LIST CHECK 
TDOSINPUT.  
          L         A1,NGX    
          TE,U      A1,' '            . IS NEXT CHARACTER BLANK   
          J         LISTCON1          . NO CONTINUE PROCESSING    
          L,U       A1,'U'            . LOAD  1100 INDICATOR
          TNE       A1,OPSYSTEM       . IS IT 1100 SYSTEM   
          J         1,X10             . YES RETURN TO FILEIN CODE 
           J         GETNEXTONE     
LISTCON1  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         INPERR              . INPFIL ERROR
          L,U       R1,NOINPCRDS        . 
          L         A0,(1,0)            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . NO, LOAD NEXT WORD
          SE        A1,RCAINPCRDS,*A0   . SEARCH COMMANDS   
          J         TDOSINPUT           . NOT A COMMAND - CONTINUE SEARCH     
          J         *INPCRDJMP-1,A0       . HANDLE COMMAND  
INPCRDJMP.  
          +         INPINPUT            . 
          +         INPBLKSIZ           . 
          +         INPOPEN             . 
          +         INPCLOSE            . 
          +         INPLABEL            . 
          +         INPBYPASS           . 
          +         INPFILES            . 
          +         INPVOLUME           . 
          +         INPNOKEY            . 
          +         INPCHANGE           . 
INPINPUT.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         INPERR              . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('USER  ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . INPUT=USER? 
          J         INPNAME             . 
          L,U       A2,1                . 
          S         A2,USERINPUT        . SET SWITCH  
          J         GETNEXTONE          . GET NEXT PARAMETER CARD 
INPNAME   L,U       A1,'T'              . 
          L         A2,OPSYSTEM         . LOAD TYPE OF OPERATING SYSTEM 
          TE        A1,A2                 . TDOS?     
          J         TDOSINPUT           . 
          L,U       A1,'('              . 
          L         A2,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE       A1,A2               . LEFT PAREN? 
          J         NAMECONT            . YES   
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD FILE NAME    
          DS        A1,FILNAMES         . STORE FILE NAME   
          DS        A1,TEMPSPOT         . 
          SZ        REQFIN              . REQUIRED PARAMETER
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,INPFILFLG        . SET INPUT FILE NAME FLAG
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING CARD
NAMECONT  L         A0,(2,0)            . 
          S         A0,TEMP1            . SAVE A0     
NEXTNAME  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT FILE NAME
          J         INPERR              . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD FILE NAME    
          DS        A1,FACPKT         . STORE FILE NAME     
          L,U       A0,FACPKT         . LOAD PKT ADD  
          ER        FACIL$            . CHECK FILE    
          TNZ,S1    FACPKT+6          . IS FILE ASSIGNED    
          J         FIE3              . NO ERR  
           L         A0,TEMP1           . RESTORE A0  
          DS        A1,TEMPSPOT,*A0     . STORE FIRST FILE NAME   
            S         A0,TEMP1           . SAVE A0    
          TE        A10,(' MERGE')    .   
          J         NOMRG             .   
          L         A0,MERGEPT        .   
          DS        A1,MERGEPKT,A0    .   
          DS        A1,FACPKT         .   
          L,U       A2,020            .   
          S,S2      A2,MERGEPKT+3,A0  .   
          L         A2,TCORE$         .   
          S,H2      A2,MERGEPKT+4,A0  .   
          L,U       A0,FACPKT         .   
          ER        FACIL$            .   
          L         A0,MERGEPT        .   
          L,S1      A1,FACPKT+6       .   
          TG,U      A1,020            .   
          S         A1,MERGEPKT+8,A0  .   
          TZ        MERGEPKT,A0       . TAPE OR MASS STORAGE ?    
          J         $+3               . MASS STORAGE JUMP   
          L,S1      A1,FACPKT+8       . LOAD NUMBER REELS   
          S,H1      A1,MERGEPKT+6,A0  . STORE FOR MERGE     
          A,U       A0,PACKSIZE       .   
          S         A0,MERGEPT        .   
NOMRG.
          L,U       A1,')'              . 
          L         A2,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE       A1,A2               . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         NAMETHEM1           . 
          L         A1,FILECNT        .   
          A,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,FILECNT        .   
          J         NEXTNAME            . GET NEXT FILE NAME
NAMETHEM1 S         X5,TEMP1            . SAVE X5 REGISTER  
          L         X5,(2,0)            . 
NAMETHEM  AN,U      A0,2                . 
          DL        A1,TEMPSPOT,A0      . LOAD LAST FILE NAME     
          DS        A1,FILNAMES,*X5     . STORE IN FIRST POSITION OF ARRAY FILNAMES 
          SZ        A1                  . INITIALIZE A1     
          LXM       A1,A0               . 
          TZ        A1                  . HAVE ALL FILE NAMES BEEN STORED?    
          J         NAMETHEM            . NO, CONTINUE STORING    
          L         X5,TEMP1            . RESTORE REGISTER  
          SZ        REQFIN               . REQUIRED PARAMETER GIVEN     
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,INPFILFLG        . SET INPUT FILE NAME FLAG
          J         TDOSINPUT           . YES, CONTINUE PROCESSING CARD 
INPBLKSIZ.  
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET BLOCK SIZE IN BYTES 
          J         INPERR              . INPFIL ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD BLKSIZ 
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT # TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          L         A2,A4               . 
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A0,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A1,A0               . DIVIDE BY CHAR/WORD     
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . ADD ONE     
          S         A1,BLKLGN         . YES STORE BLKLGN    
          SZ        CICFLG            . SET IN SIZE CONVERT FLG   
          SZ        BLIFLG            . SET PARA IN SIZE FLAG     
          SZ        REQBLK              . GIVEN REQUIRED PARAMETER
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PARAMETER CARD PROCESSING  
INPOPEN.    
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         INPERR              . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('NORWD ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . NO REWIND?  
          J         TDOSINPUT           . NO, CONTINUE PROCESSING 
          SZ        INPOPENSW         .   
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
INPCLOSE.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         INPERR              . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('NORWD ')       . 
          TNE       A1,A2               . NO REWIND?  
          J         NOREWIND            . 
          L         A2,('UNLD  ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . REWIND AND UNLOAD?
          J         TDOSINPUT           . NO, CONTINUE PROCESSING 
          L,U       A1,02               . YES, SET SWITCH   
          S         A1,INPCLOSESW       . 
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
NOREWIND.   
          SZ        INPCLOSESW        .   
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
INPLABEL.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         INPERR              . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          S         A1,LBLTYPE          . STORE LABEL TYPE  
          L         A2,('U     ')       . 
          TNE       A1,A2               . U?    
          J         LBL1                . 
          L         A2,('S     ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . S?    
          J         LBL2                . 
          L,U       A2,3                . USE 3 STD LBL LEVEL 2   
LBL       S         A2,STDLBL           . STORE INDICATOR IN STDLBL     
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING CARD
LBL1      L,U       A2,1              . LOAD OMITTED LABEL  
          J         LBL               . JUMP TO STORE AND EXIT    
LBL2      L,U       A2,2                . USE 2 NON STD LEVEL 2   
          J         LBL                 . 
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
INPBYPASS.  
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,INPBYPASSW       . SET SWITCH  
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
INPNOKEY.   
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,INPNOKEYSW       . SET SWITCH  
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
INPCHANGE.  
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,INPCHANGESW      . SET SWITCH  
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
INPFILES.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         INPERR              . ERROR - END OF CARD     
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT # TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          S         A4,FILECNT          . STORE FILECNT     
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
INPVOLUME.  
          L         A0,(1,0)            . 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET VOLUME PARAMETER    
          J         INPERR              . INPFIL ERROR
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,'('              . LEFT PAREN? 
          J         VOLLOOP             . YES   
          DL        A1,GITV             . NO, LOAD VOLUME   
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . YES, CONVERT # TO OCTAL 
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          S         A4,VOLCNT1          . STORE VOLUME
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
VOLLOOP   LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         INPERR              . INPFIL ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT # TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          S         A4,VOLCNT1,*A0      . STORE VOLUME
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         VOLLOOP             . NO, CONTINUE WITH VOLUME PROCESSING 
          J         TDOSINPUT           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
/.    
$(0). 
RCAOUTCRDS. 
          'OUTPUT'                      . 
          'BLKSIZ'                      . 
          'LABEL '                      . 
          'OPEN  '                      . 
          'CLOSE '                      . 
          'CHANGE'                      . 
          'NOTPMK'                      . 
NOOUTCRDS EQU       $-RCAOUTCRDS        . 
$(1). 
. ********************************************************************  
.     
. THE FOLLOWING CONVERTS THE OUTFIL PARAMETER CARD    
.     
RCAOUTFIL.  
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,' '              . BLANK?
          J         OUTERR              . NO, BAD OUTFIL CARD     
          SZ        RETBLANK          . SET TO NO IMAGE CNT 
          J         LISTCON2  
TDOSOUTPUT. 
          L         A1,NGX    
          TE,U      A1,' '            . IS NEXT CHARACTER BLANK ? 
          J         LISTCON2          . NO CONTINUE PROCESSING    
          L,U       A1,'U'            . LOAD 1100 INDICATOR 
          TNE       A1,OPSYSTEM       . 1100 OR RCA   
          J         1,X10             . 1100 RETURN TO FILEOUT CODE     
          J         GETNEXTONE
LISTCON2  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          L,U       R1,NOOUTCRDS        . 
          L         A0,(1,0)            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          SE        A1,RCAOUTCRDS,*A0   . SEARCH VALID COMMANDS   
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . NOT A COMMAND - CONTINUE SEARCH     
          J         *OUTCRDJMP-1,A0       . HANDLE COMMAND  
OUTCRDJMP.  
          +         OUTOUTPUT           . 
          +         OUTBLKSIZE          . 
          +         OUTLABEL            . 
          +         OUTOPEN             . 
          +         OUTCLOSE            . 
          +         OUTCHANGE           . 
          +         OUTNOTPMK           . 
OUTOUTPUT.  
          L         A0,(2,0)            . 
          S         A0,TEMP1            . SAVE A0     
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,'('              . LEFT PAREN? 
          J         OUTNAME             . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('USER  ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . INPUT=USER? 
          J         NOUSEROUT           . NO    
          L,U       A1,1                . YES, SET SWITCH   
          S         A1,USEROUTPUT       . 
          J         GETNEXTONE          . GET NEXT PARAMETER CARD 
NOUSEROUT L         A2,('W     ')       . 
          TNE       A1,A2               . W?    
          J         WRITOUT             . YES, STORE  
          L         A2,('D     ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . D?    
          J         OUTNAME3            . 
WRITOUT   S         A1,OUTPUTWD         . STORE OUTPUTWD    
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OUTNAME   L,U       A1,'T'              . 
          L         A2,OPSYSTEM         . LOAD TYPE OF OPERATING SYSTEM 
          TE        A1,A2               . TDOS? 
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . NO, CONTINUE PROCESSING CARD  
OUTNAME2  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         OUTERR              . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . NO, LOAD FILE NAME
          L         A0,TEMP1            . RESTORE A0  
          DS        A1,OUTNAMESAVE,*A0  . STORE OUTPUT FILE NAME  
          S         A0,TEMP1            . SAVE A0     
          L,U       A0,1                . 
          S         A0,OUTFILFLG        . SET OUTPUT FILE NAME FLAG     
          L,U       A1,')'              . 
          L         A2,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE        A1,A2               . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         OUTNAME2            . NO CONTINUE READING LIST
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING CARD
OUTNAME3  L,U       A1,'T'              . 
          L         A2,OPSYSTEM         . LOAD TYPE OF OPERATING SYSTEM 
          TE        A1,A2               . TDOS? 
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . NO    
          DL        A1,GITV             . YES, LOAD FILE NAME     
          DS        A1,OUTNAMESAVE      . SAVE FILE NAME    
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,OUTFILFLG        . SET OUTPUT FILE NAME FLAG     
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING CARD
OUTBLKSIZE. 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TNE,U     A1,'('              . LEFT PAREN? 
          J         BLKPROC             . YES   
          DL        A1,GITV             . NO, LOAD PARAMETER
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . YES, CONVERT # TO OCTAL 
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A2,A4               . 
RCA1IN    L         A4,TYPECONST1     . LOAD CHARACTERS PER WORD  
           DI        A1,A4              . DIVIDE BY CHAR/WORD     
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . NO, ADD ONE MORE WORD   
          S         A1,NEWBLKLN       .NEW WORDS/BLOCK
          SZ        BLOFLG            . SET  OUTPUT IN SIZE 
          SZ        COCFLG            . SET OUT SIZE CONVERT FLG  
          SZ        OBKFLG            . SET  OUTPUT PARA GIVEN    
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
BLKPROC   LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          L         A1,GITV           .   
          TNE,U     A1,' '              . BLANK?
          J         BLK1                . YES   
          DL        A1,GITV             . NO, LOAD PARAMETER
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . YES, CONVERT # TO OCTAL 
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          L         A2,A4               . 
           SZ        A1                 . 
           L         A4,TYPECONST1      . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
           DI        A1,A4              . DIVIDE BY CHAR/WORD     
           TZ        A2                 . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
           A,U       A1,1               . ADD ONE     
           L         A2,A1              . 
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A4,OUTLENG          . 
          TNZ       A4                  . IS OUTLENG GIVEN? 
          L         A4,RECSIZ           . NO, USE RECSIZ INSTEAD  
          DI        A1,A4               . DIVIDE BLKSIZE BY OUTLENG     
          S         A4,TEMP4            . SAVE A4 CONTENTS  
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . ADD ONE MORE BLOCK
          S         A1,OUTKEYLENG       . STORE OUTKEYLENG  
BLK1      LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . YES, CONVERT # TO OCTAL 
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          L         A2,A4               . 
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A4,TYPECONST1       . LOAD CHAR/WORD    
          DI        A1,A4               . DIVIDE BY CHAR/WORD     
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . ADD ONE     
          L         A2,A1               . 
          SZ        A1                  . 
          L         A4,TEMP4            . LOAD OUTLENG OR RECSIZ  
          DI        A1,A4               . 
          TZ        A2                  . WAS DIVISION EXACT?     
          A,U       A1,1                . ADD ONE MORE BLOCK
          S         A1,OUTDATALENG      . YES, STORE OUTDATALENG  
          S         A1,NEWBLKLN       .   
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         OWNPARAM            . NO    
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . YES, CONTINUE PROCESSING
OWNPARAM  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('OWN   ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . OWN?  
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,OWNSW            . SET OWN SWITCH    
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
.     
OUTLABEL.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          S         A1,OUTLABELSW       . STORE LABEL 
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
.     
OUTOPEN.    
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('NORWD ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . NO REWIND?  
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . NO, CONTINUE PROCESSING 
          SZ        OUTOPENSW         .   
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
.     
OUTCLOSE.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         OUTERR              . OUTFIL ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          L         A2,('NORWD ')       . 
          TNE       A1,A2               . NO REWIND?  
          J         NORWDOUT            . YES   
          L         A2,('UNLD  ')       . 
          TE        A1,A2               . REWIND AND UNLOAD?
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . NO, CONTINUE PROCESSING 
          L,U       A1,02               . 
          S         A1,OUTCLOSESW       . SET SWITCH  
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
NORWDOUT  SZ        OUTCLOSESW        .   
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
.     
OUTCHANGE.  
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,OUTCHANGESW      . SET SWITCH  
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
.     
OUTNOTPMK.  
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,TPMKSW           . SET SWITCH  
          J         TDOSOUTPUT          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
/.    
.     
. ********************************************************* 
. THE FOLLOWING CODING DECODES THE WORKFIL PARAMETER CARD.  
. FACILITY ASSIGNMENTS ARE MADE FOR THE WORK FILES.   
.     
RCAWORKFIL. 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         WORKFILERR          . 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET LEFT PAREN    
          J         WORKFILERR          . 
          L         X1,PARPNT           . LOAD PARAMETER TABLE POINTER  
          LXI,U     X1,1                . 
          L         X2,(2,WORKTAPES)    . LOAD TAPE FILE ADDRESS  
          L,U       X3,023              . 
          L         A0,(2,0)            . 
          S         A0,TEMP1            . 
WORKLOOP  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET WORK FILE NAME
          J         WORKFILERR          . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD FILE NAME    
          L         A0,TEMP1            . LOAD TAPE FILE POINTER  
          DS        A1,WORKTAPES,*A0    . STORE TAPE FILE NAME    
          S         A0,TEMP1            . STORE TAPE FILE POINTER 
          L         A0,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A0,')'              . RIGHT PAREN?
          J         WORKLOOP            . NO, CONTINUE READING WORK FILE NAMES
          L         A0,TEMP1            . LOAD TAPE FILE POINTER  
          SZ        A1                  . INITIALIZE A1     
          LXM       A1,A0               . LOAD NUMBER OF FILES TIMES 2 +2     
          SZ        A0                  . 
          DI,U      A0,2                . DIVIDE TO GET NUMBER OF FILES 
          AN,U      A0,1                . 
          S         A0,R1               . 
          S,H2      A0,SCRTPC         . STORE #SCR FILES ASSIGNED 
          L         A0,(2,0)            . 
          S         A0,TEMP1            . 
ASGWORK   L         A0,TEMP1            . 
          DL        A1,WORKTAPES,*A0    . LOAD TAPE FILE NAME     
          S         A0,TEMP1            . 
JUSTIFY   LDSC      A1,06             .   
          L,U       A0,' '            .   
          S         A1,TEMP3          .   
          TE,S1     A0,TEMP3          .   
          J         JUSTIFY           .   
          DS        A1,ASGFIL+2       . LOAD FILE NAME INTO PACKET
          L         A0,ASGPKT           . LOAD PACKET FOR CSF$    
          ER        CSF$
          TP        A0                .   
          J         BADCSF            .   
          L,U       A0,0,X2           .   
          S         A0,0,X1           .   
          S,S1      X3,0,*X1          .   
          NOP       0,0,*X2           .   
          JGD       R1,ASGWORK          . CONTINUE ASSIGNING FILES
          S,H2      X1,PARPNT         .   
          J         GETNEXTONE          . WORKFIL IGNORED   
BADCSF    L         A3,(ASGLENG,ASGFIL) . 
          LMJ       X10,OPREQ         .   
          L         A0,TEMP1          .   
          AN,U      A0,2              .   
          S         A0,TEMP1          .   
          J         ASGWORK           .   
/.    
. ********************************************************* 
. THE FOLLOWING CODING DECODES THE OPTION PARAMETER CARD.   
.     
RCAOPTION.  
          L         A1,NGX              . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER     
          TE,U      A1,' '              . BLANK?
          J         OPTIONERR           . NO, OPTION CARD ERROR   
          J         LISTCON3  
TDOSOPTION. 
          L         A1,NGX    
          TNE,U      A1,' '   
          J         GETNEXTONE
LISTCON3  LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         OPTIONERR           . OPTION ERROR
          L,U       R1,NOOPTCRDS        . 
          L         A0,(1,0)            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          SE        A1,RCAOPTCRDS,*A0   . SEARCH VALID COMMANDS   
          J         TDOSOPTION          . NOT A COMMAND - CONTINUE PROCESSING 
          J         *OPTCRDJMP-1,A0       . HANDLE COMMAND  
$(0). 
RCAOPTCRDS. 
          'NOSEQ'                       . VALID COMMANDS    
          'RCTL'                        . 
          'SKIPRE'                     .  
          'VOLS'                        . 
          'MRKCTR'                      . 
          'OUTPUT'                      . 
          'VERIFY'                      . 
          'FILES'                       . 
          'CKPT'                        . 
NOOPTCRDS EQU       $-RCAOPTCRDS        . NUMBER OF COMMANDS
$(1). 
OPTCRDJMP.  
          +         OPTNOSEQ            . 
          +         OPTRCTL             . 
          +         OPTSKIPREC          . 
          +         OPTVOLS             . 
          +         OPTMRKCTR           . 
          +         OPTOUTPUT           . 
          +         OPTVERIFY           . 
          +         OPTFILES            . 
          +         OPTCKPT             . 
. ********************************************************************  
.     
. THE FOLLOWING CONVERTS THE OPTION PARAMETER CARD    
.     
OPTNOSEQ.   
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,NOSEQSW          . SET NOSEQ SWITCH  
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OPTRCTL.    
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,RCTLSW           . SET READ CONTROL SWITCH 
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OPTSKIPREC. 
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET PARAMETER     
          J         OPTIONERR           . OPTION ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT # TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          S         A4,SKIPSAVE         . SAVE SKIPREC
          SZ        SKIPFLG             . RESET SKIP FLAG   
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OPTVOLS.    
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET PARAMETER     
          J         OPTIONERR           . OPTION ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT # TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          S         A4,VOLCNT           . SAVE VOLUME 
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OPTMRKCTR.  
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET PARAMETER     
          J         OPTIONERR           . OPTION ERROR
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT # TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          S         A4,MRKCTRVAL        . STORE MRKCTR VALUE
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OPTOUTPUT.  
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT WORD     
          J         OPTIONERR           . OPTION ERROR
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,OUTWORKSW        . SET SWITCH  
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OPTVERIFY.  
          L,U       A1,1                . 
          S         A1,VERIFYSW         . SET VERIFY SWITCH 
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
OPTFILES.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NUMBER OF FILES INPUT TO SORT   
          J         OPTIONERR           . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT NUMBER TO OCTAL 
          J         DTOERR              . 
          S         A4,FILECTR          . STORE NUMBER OF FILES   
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING CARD
OPTCKPT.    
          SZ        CKPFLG              . RESET CHECKPOINT FLAG   
          L,U       A0,CONSOL4          . SEND MESSAGE TO MOUNT TAPE    
          ER        COM$                . 
          L         A0,CKPTPKT          . 
          ER        CSF$                . 
          TP        A0                .   
          J         BCSF              .   
          L         A1,('CKPTTA')       . LOAD NAME OF
          L         A2,('PE    ')       .  CHECKPOINT TAPE  
          DS        A1,CKFLNM           . STORE TAPE NAME   
          L         A1,('     T')       . 
          S         A1,DEVTYPE          . STORE DEVICE TYPE 
          L,U       A1,9                . STORE NUMBER OF POSSIBLE CHECKPOINTS
          S         A1,NUMCKS           . 
          J         TDOSOPTION          . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
BCSF      L         A3,(CKPTLENG,ASGCKPT)                               .     
          LMJ       X10,OPREQ         .   
          J         $-14              .   
/$(0).
RCAALLOCRDS.
          'CORE'                        . VALID COMMANDS    
          'VM'                          . 
          'DM'                          . 
NOALLOCRDS EQU      $-RCAALLOCRDS       . NUMBER OF VALID COMMANDS
$(1). 
. ********************************************************************  
.     
. THE FOLLOWING CONVERTS THE ALLOC PARAMETER CARD     
. UNDER THE VMOS SYSTEM.
.     
VMOSALLOC.  
          L         A1,NGX    
          TNE,U      A1,' '   
          J         GETNEXTONE        .   
RCAALLOC.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET NEXT PARAMETER
          J         ALLOCERR            . ALLOC ERROR 
          L,U       R1,NOALLOCRDS       . 
          L         A0,(1,0)            . 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          SE        A1,RCAALLOCRDS,*A0  . SEARCH VALID COMMANDS   
          J         ALLOCERR          .   
          J         *ALLOCRDJMP-1,A0      . HANDLE COMMAND  
ALLOCRDJMP. 
          +         ALLOCORE            . 
          +         ALLOCVM             . 
          +         ALLOCDM             . 
.     
ALLOCORE.   
          LMJ       X11,GIT             . GET PARAMETER     
          J         ALLOCERR            . ALLOC ERROR 
          DL        A1,GITV             . LOAD PARAMETER    
          LMJ       X11,DTO             . CONVERT # TO OCTAL
          J         DTOERR              . DTO ERROR   
          MSI,U     A4,4096             . MULTIPLY BY WORDS/PAGE  
          S         A4,CORESAVE       .   
          J         VMOSALLOC           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
ALLOCVM.    
ALLOCDM.    
          J         VMOSALLOC           . CONTINUE PROCESSING     
/.

. ***********************************************************     
. THE FOLLOWING CODING SPECIFIES THE TERMINATION OF THE     
.   CONVERSION MODE.  CONTROL IS RETURNED TO GSM.     
.     
RCAENDCVRT. 
          SZ        RETBLANK  
          TZ        USRCSW            . USER CONTROL EXIT REQUIRED?     
          J         NXTPARAM-1
          L         A1,USRCSW+3       .   
          LMJ       X6,0,A1           . JUMP TO USER CODE   
          J         NXTPARAM-1        .   
WFORM     FORM      6,6,6,18            . 
EF        FORM      18,18             .

. ***********************************************************     
.  THE FOLLOWING CODING DECODES THE MODS PARAMETER CARD OF  
.  THE FOLLOWING OPERATING SYSTEMS: DOS, TDOS, AND VMOS.    
.     
RCAMODS     
          L         A1,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TE,U      A1,' '            . BLANK?  
          J         MODERR            .   
          J         MODCONT           .   
MODREP    LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET COMMA OR BLANK  
          J         MODERR            .   
          L         A1,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TNE,U     A1,' '            . BLANK?  
          J         GETNEXTONE        . GET NEXT PARAMETER CARD   
MODCONT   LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NEXT WORD 
          J         MODERR            .   
          L         A1,OPSYSTEM       . LOAD TYPE OF SYSTEM 
          TNE,U     A1,'V'            . VMOS?   
          J         VMOSMOD           . YES     
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET LEFT PAREN
          J         MODERR            .   
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET ROUTINE NAME    
          J         MODERR            .   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD ROUTINE NAME   
          DS        A1,TEMP1          . STORE NAME    
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET LENGTH    
          J         MODERR            .   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD ROUTINE LENGTH 
          DS        A1,TEMP0          . STORE LENGTH  
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET EXIT NUMBER     
          J         MODERR            .   
VMOSMOD   L         A1,GITV           . LOAD EXIT NUMBER    
          LSSC      A1,6              . SHIFT OUT LETTER 'E'
          SSL       A1,24             .   
          L,U       R1,EXITNO         . LOAD NUMBER OF EXITS
          L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          SE,H1     A1,MODEXITS,*A0   . SEARCH EXITS  
          J         MODERR2           . NO FIND 
          L         A4,MODEXITS-1,A0  .   
          L         A0,A4             .   
          LXI,U     A0,0              .   
          J         0,A0              .   
$(0). 
MODEXITS.   
          EF        '01',USRCTL       .   
          EF        '02',MODCDE    .
         EF        '03',STATGY      .     
          EF        '10',ELMLAB       .   
          EF        '11',INLBL      .     
          EF        '12',MODCDE    .
          EF        '13',MODCDE    .
          EF        '14',EQLREC       .   
          EF        '15',REDERR      .    
          EF        '21',MODCDE    .
          EF        '30',ELMLAB    .
          EF        '31',MODCDE    .
          EF        '32',MODCDE    .
          EF        '33',MODCDE    .
          EF        '34',OUTBOK      .    
          EF        '40',ELMLAB    .
          EF        '41',OUTLBL      .    
          EF        '42',EQLREC       .   
          EF        '43',OUTREC       .   
          EF        '44',OUTBOK      .    
          EF        '45',INLBL      .     
          EF        '46',INPREC       .   
          EF        '47',ELMLAB    .
          EF        'XT',COLSEQ       .   
          EF        'TB',TRANTB      .    
EXITNO    EQU       $-MODEXITS        .   
$(1). 
USRCTL    SZ        USRCSW            .   
          L,U       A4,USRCSW         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
STATGY    SZ        STRATSW           .   
          L,U       A4,STRATSW        .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
ELMLAB    SZ        ELLBSW            .   
          L,U       A4,ELLBSW         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
INLBL     SZ        INLBSW            .   
          L,U       A4,INLBSW         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
EQLREC    SZ        EQRCSW          .     
          L,U       A4,EQRCSW         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
REDERR   SZ        RDERSW         . 
          L,U       A4,RDERSW         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
OUTBOK   SZ        OUTBKS         . 
          L,U       A4,OUTBKS         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
OUTLBL   SZ        OUTLBS            .    
          L,U       A4,OUTLBS         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
OUTREC    SZ        OUTRECSW          .   
          L,U       A4,OUTRECSW       .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
INPREC    SZ        INRCSW          .     
          L,U       A4,INRCSW         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
COLSEQ    SZ        COLSEQSW          .   
          L,U       A4,COLSEQSW       .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
TRANTB    SZ        TRTBSW            .   
          L,U       A4,TRTBSW         .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
MODCDE    L         A0,OPSYSTEM       . LOAD TYPE OF SYSTEM 
          TE,U      A0,'D'            . DOS?    
          J         NODOS            . NO 
          L,U       R1,NODEXITS       . NUMBER OF EXITS     
          L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          SE,H1     A1,DEXITS,*A0     . SEARCH EXITS  
          J         MODERR2           . NO FIND 
          L         A4,DEXITS-1,A0    .   
          L         A0,A4             .   
          LXI,U     A0,0              .   
          J         0,A0              .   
$(0). 
DEXITS.     
          EF        '12',INPREC       .   
          EF        '13',INPBLOK      .   
          EF        '31',OUTLBL      .    
          EF        '32',EQLREC       .   
          EF        '33',OUTREC       .   
NODEXITS  EQU       $-DEXITS          .   
$(1). 
INPBLOK   SZ        INPBLOKSW         .   
          L,U       A4,INPBLOKSW      .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
NODOS      L         A0,OPSYSTEM       . LOAD TYPE OF SYSTEM
          TE,U      A0,'T'            . TDOS?   
          J         TDOSNOT           . NO
          L,U       R1,NOTEXITS       . LOAD NUMBER OF EXITS
          L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          SE,H1     A1,TEXITS,*A0     . SEARCH EXITS  
          J         MODERR2           . NO FIND 
          L         A4,TEXITS-1,A0    .   
          L         A0,A4             .   
          LXI,U     A0,0              .   
          J         0,A0              .   
$(0). 
TEXITS.     
          EF        '02',WORKLBL      .   
          EF        '12',INPBLOK      .   
          EF        '13',INPREC       .   
          EF        '31',OUTLBL      .    
          EF        '32',EQLREC       .   
          EF        '33',OUTREC       .   
NOTEXITS  EQU       $-TEXITS          .   
$(1). 
WORKLBL   SZ        WORKLBLSW         .   
          L,U       A4,WORKLBLSW      .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
TDOSNOT   L,U       R1,NOVEXITS       . LOAD NUMBER OF EXITS
          L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          SE,H1     A1,VEXITS,*A0     . SEARCH EXITS  
          J         MODERR2           . NO FIND 
          L         A4,VEXITS-1,A0    .   
          L         A0,A4             .   
          LXI,U     A0,0              .   
          J         0,A0              .   
$(0). 
VEXITS.     
          EF        '02',VALLOC       .   
          EF        '12',OUTLBL      .    
          EF        '21',INPREC     
          EF        '23',OUTREC       .   
          EF        '31',VWORK        .   
          EF        '32',VWORK        .   
          EF        '33',VWORK        .   
NOVEXITS  EQU       $-VEXITS          .   
$(1). 
VALLOC    SZ        ALLOCSW           .   
          L,U       A4,ALLOCSW        .   
          J         EXITCONT          .   
VWORK     SZ        WORKAREASW        .   
          L,U       A4,WORKAREASW     .   
EXITCONT.   
          L         A1,OPSYSTEM       . LOAD TYPE OF OPERATING SYSTEM   
          TNE,U     A1,'V'            . VMOS?   
          J         VOPTSFND          . YES     
          DL        A1,TEMP1          . LOAD ROUTINE NAME   
          L         A0,A4             .   
          DS        A1,1,A0           . STORE NAME AFTER SWITCH   
          L,U       R1,10             .   
          L         A0,(3,1)          .   
          J         $+3               .   
NTRYPT    JGD       R1,$+2            .   
          J         NPTERR            . NO FIND ENTRY POINT IN TABLE    
          DTE       A1,ENTRY$,*A0     .   
          J         NTRYPT            . CHECK NEXT ENTRY POINT    
          AN,U      A0,1              .   
          L         A1,ENTRY$,A0      . LOAD ADDRESS OF ENTRY POINT     
          L         A2,A4             .   
          S         A1,3,A2           . STORE ADDRESS AFTER SWITCH INFO 
          LMJ       X11,EXTADR        . JUMP TO HANDLE EXIT ADDRESS     
          TZ        EXITNSW           . ANOTHER EXIT NUMBER?
          J         EXITNCONT         . YES     
          TZ        OWNFLG            .   
          J         0,X6              . RETURN  
          J         NOTVMOSMOD        .   
VOPTSFND  S         A4,TEMP1          .   
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET LEFT PAREN
          J         MODERR            .   
VCONT     L         A1,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TNE,U     A1,')'            . RIGHT PAREN?  
          J         MODREP            . YES     
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET FIRST OPTION    
          J         MODERR            .   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD OPTION   
          L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          L,U       R1,NOVOPTS        . LOAD NUMBER OF OPTIONS    
          SE        A1,VOPTS,*A0      . SEARCH OPTIONS
          J         MODERR3           .   
          J         *JMPVOPTS-1,A0    . JUMP TO OPTION LABEL
$(0). 
VOPTS.
          'LINK  '                    .   
          'TERM  '                    .   
          'CONT  '                    .   
          'NEXT  '                    .   
          'FIN   '                    .   
          'CONV  '                    .   
NOVOPTS   EQU       $-VOPTS           .   
$(1). 
JMPVOPTS.   
          +         VOPTSLINK         .   
          +         VOPTSTERM         .   
          +         VOPTSCONT         .   
          +         VOPTSNEXT         .   
          +         VOPTSFIN          .   
          +         VOPTSCONV         .   
VOPTSLINK.  
          LMJ       X11,GIT           . GET NAME OF ROUTINE 
          J         MODERR            .   
          DL        A1,GITV           . LOAD ROUTINE NAME   
          L         A0,TEMP1          . LOAD SWITCH ADDRESS 
          DS        A1,1,A0           . STORE ROUTINE NAME  
          L,U       R1,10             .   
          L         A0,(3,1)          .   
          J         $+3               .   
NTYPT2    JGD       R1,$+2            .   
          J         NPTERR            . NO FIND ENTRY POINT IN TABLE    
          DTE       A1,ENTRY$,*A0     .   
          J         NTYPT2            .   
          AN,U      A0,1              .   
          L         A1,ENTRY$,A0      . LOAD ADDRESS OF ENTRY POINT     
          L         A2,A4             .   
          S         A1,3,A2           . STORE ADDRESS AFTER SWITCH INFO 
          LMJ       X11,EXTADR        . JUMP TO HANDLE EXIT ADDRESS     
          J         VCONT             .   
VOPTSTERM.  
          L         A0,TEMP1          . LOAD SWITCH ADDRESS 
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S,S1      A1,0,A0           . STORE TERM FLAG IN SWITCH 
          J         VCONT             .   
VOPTSCONT.  
          L         A0,TEMP1          . LOAD SWITCH ADDRESS 
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S,S2      A1,0,A0           . STORE CONT FLAG IN SWITCH 
          J         VCONT             .   
VOPTSNEXT.  
          L         A0,TEMP1          . LOAD SWITCH ADDRESS 
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S,S3      A1,0,A0           . STORE NEXT FLAG IN SWITCH 
          J         VCONT             .   
VOPTSFIN.   
          L         A0,TEMP1          . LOAD SWITCH ADDRESS 
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S,S4      A1,0,A0           . STORE FIN FLAG IN SWITCH  
          J         VCONT             .   
VOPTSCONV.  
          L         A0,TEMP1          .   
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S,S5      A1,0,A0           . STORE CONV FLAG IN SWITCH 
          J         VCONT             .   
NOTVMOSMOD. 
          L         A1,NGX            . LOAD NEXT CHARACTER 
          TNE,U     A1,')'            .   
          J         MODREP            .   
          LMJ       X11,GIT           .   
          J         MODERR            .   
          L,U       A1,1              .   
          S         A1,EXITNSW        .   
          J         VMOSMOD           .   
EXITNCONT.  
          SZ        EXITNSW           . RESET MULTIPLE EXIT SWITCH
          J         NOTVMOSMOD        .   
.     
. THE FOLLOWING CODING STORES THE EXIT ROUTINE'S ENTRY POINT
. ADDRESS INTO THE PARAMETER TABLE  
.     
EXTADR.   ENTRY TO EXTADR VIA  LMJ  X11,EXTADR  
          L         A2,A4             . LOAD ADDRESS OF SWITCH    
          L,U       R1,NOSWCH         . LOAD NUMBER OF SWITCHES   
          L         A0,(1,0)          .   
          SE        A2,SWTTBL,*A0     . SEARCH SWITCH TABLE 
          J         NOSTFD            . NO FIND ON SWITCH   
          J         *SWTCH-1,A0       . JUMP TO HANDLE SWITCH     
$(0). 
SWTTBL.     
          +         USRCSW            .   
          +         STRATSW           .   
          +         ELLBSW            .   
          +         INLBSW            .   
          +         EQRCSW            .   
          +         RDERSW            .   
          +         OUTBKS            .   
          +         OUTLBS            .   
          +         OUTRECSW          .   
          +         INRCSW            .   
          +         COLSEQSW          .   
          +         TRTBSW            .   
          +         INPBLOKSW         .   
          +         WORKLBLSW         .   
          +         ALLOCSW           .   
          +         WORKAREASW        .   
NOSWCH    EQU       $-SWTTBL          .   
$(1). 
SWTCH.
          +         USREC             .   
          +         STRTGY            .

          +         EQRECS            .   
          +         REDERS            .

USREC.
          J         0,X11             . RETURN

          DL        A1,('          ST') . 
          DS        A1,MSG21          .   
          L         A1,('RATEGY')       . 
          J         EXERR             .

          DL        A1,(' LABEL ELIMI') .

          J         EXERR             .

          DS        A1,MSG21          .   
          L         A1,('ERFLOW')       . 
          J         EXERR             .   
EXTFIX    L         A2,PARPNT         . LOAD PARAMETER TABLE POINTER    
          S,S1      A0,0,A2           . STORE INTO PARAMETER TABLE
          S,H2      A1,0,A2           . STORE ADDRESS INTO PARAMETER TABLE    
          A,U       A2,1              . INCREMENT POINTER   
          S         A2,PARPNT         . RESTORE POINTER     
          J         0,X11             . RETURN
